dveṣarāgasamutthitānām| ato'pyanivṛtāvyākṛtā eva gataya iti| yasmāduktaṃ nirvṝtte vipāke nāraka iti saṃkhyāṃ gacchatīti| gatiṣu sarve'nuśayā anuśerata iti prakaraṇagranthaḥ parihāryaḥ vaktavyaḥ parihāra ityarthaḥ virodhaprasaṅgāt| yadi hi pañcaprakārā dharmā gatisvabhāvā bhaveyuḥ evaṃ sarve'nuśayā anuśayīran| yadi tvavyākṛtā eva gatayaḥ syurevaṃ vaktavyaṃ syāt| bhāvanāprahātavyāḥ sarvatragāśceti| anivṛtāvyākṛtā hi saṃskṛtā dharmā bhāvanāprahātavyā eva| teṣu ca bhāvanāprahātavyā eva sarvatragāścānuśayā anuśerate ālambanataḥ prayogato vā nānye| tena jñāyate nānivṛtāvyākṛtā eva gataya iti| sandhicittāni hi gatīnāṃ pañcaprakārāṇiti| sandhicittāni gatīnāṃ praveśaḥ teṣāṃ pañcaprakāratvāt| duḥkhadarśanaprahātvyaṃ yāvad bhāvanāprahātavyamiti| tatra sarve'nuśayā anuśerata iti yuktam| grāmopavicāragrahaṇavaditi| grāmaparisāmantakagrahaṇavadityarthaḥ| pañcasu kaṣāyeṣviti| pañcakaṣāyā āyuḥkaṣāyaḥ kleśakaṣāyo dṛṣṭikaṣāyaḥ kalpakaṣāyaḥ sattvakṣāyaśca teṣu| kleśadṛṣṭikaṣāyau pṛthaguktau| kleśakaṣāyāddṛṣṭikaṣāyaḥ pṛthagukta ityarthaḥ| anayoḥ pṛthagvacanaṃ gṛhasthapravrajitapakṣādhikarāt| gatihetujñāpanārthamiti| gatīnāṃ karmodbhavo heturiti| antarābhave'pyeṣa prasaṃga iti| evamantarābhavo'pi gatiśca syāt| pṛthak cāsya vacanaṃ syāt gatigamanajñāpanārthamiti gacchanti tāmiti| karmasādhana parigrahaḥ| na cantarābhava eva yujyata iti na gatiḥ| ārūpyā apīti vistaraḥ| ārūpyā na gatiḥ syuḥ| cyutideśa evotpādāt| ārūpyagā hi yatra cyavante vihāre vā vṛkṣamūle vā yāvaccaturthā dhyānabhūmau tatraivotpadyanta iti| antarābhavavanna gatiḥ syāt| evaṃ tarhīti| purvapakṣaṃ parityajya pakṣāntaramāśrīyate| gatyorantarā antarāle bhavatīti antarābhava ityanvarthasaṃjñākaraṇānnāntarābhavo gatiriti| vaibhāṣikā āhuḥ yattarhīti vistaraḥ| nirvṛtte vipāka iti| vipākaśabdavacanādvipākasvabhāvā gatiriti tadabhiprāyaḥ| itara āha| nirvṝtte vipāke nāraka iti nārako vyavasthāpyate| na tūktaṃ vipāka eveti| na tūkta vipākasvabhāvaiva gatiriti| kiṃ tarhi vipākāvipākasvabhāvā gatiḥ| tasmāt kuśalakliṣṭā api gatayo bhavantītyabhiprāyaḥ| vaibhāṣikaḥ punarāha| yattarhyuktaṃ tatreti vistaraḥ| anyatra tebhyo dharmebhya iti| vipākajebhya ityabhiprāyaḥ| na tu skandhāntarapratiṣedhaṃ karotītyadhikṛtam| tāśceti vistaraḥ| avyākṛtatvapakṣe'pi dvavidhyam| aupacayikasvabhāvā apīti| apiśabdādvipākasvabhāvā apīti| yathokta

vipāko jīvitaṃ dvedhā dvādaśa

iti| naiṣyandikāstu na gṛhyante| vipākasvabhāvā evetyācāryasaṅghabhadraḥ pūrvameva pakṣamicchati|| 4||  
[ nānātvakāyasaṃjñāśca nānākāyaikasaṃjñina|  
viparyayāccaikakāyasaṃjñāścārūpiṇastrayaḥ|| 5||  
vijñānasthitayaḥ sapta śeṣaṃ tatparibhedavat| ]

kāmāvacarāḥ ṣaṭ prathamadhyānikāśca| katame te vahirdeśakanayena brahyakāyikāḥ vradyapurohitā mahābrahyāṇaśca prathamābhinivṛttavarjyāḥ| kāśmīranayena punarbrahyakāyikā vrahyapurohitāśca prathamābhinivṛttavarjyāḥ| vrahyapurohiteṣveva hi teṣāṃ nayena mahābrahyāṇo na sthānāntaranayāḥ| anekavarṇaliṃgasaṃsthānatvāditi| varṇo nīlatvādiḥ| liṅgaṃ vastrābharaṇādi| saṃsthānaṃ dairdhyādi| āroha unnatatā| pariṇāhastiryakapramāṇam| ākṛtivigrahaḥ ākṛtilakṣaṇo vigraha ākṛtivigrahaḥ| evaṃ viśeṣite vedānādīnāṃ vigrahaḥ paryudasto bhavati| nāsāvākṛtilakṣaṇa iti| vigraha punaḥ śarīram| vāgbhāpā vāguccaraṇam| kathamidānīmiti vistaraḥ| prathamadhyānapratyāgamanena taidvitīyaṃ tyaktam| tadbhūmikaṃ dvitīyadhyānabhūmikam| pūrvanivāsaṃ kathamanvasmāpuḥ| na tāvat prathamadhyānabhūmikena cittena tadaviṣayatvāt na dvitīyadhyānabhūmikena tasyālabdhatvāt| punarlabdhamiti cet| atāha| labdhāyāṃ ceti vistaraḥ| ślavrataparāmarṣadṛṣṭirmahāvrahyālambanā prathamadhyānabhūmikaiva yujyate| sā ca dvitīyadhyānalābhāt prahīnā na samudācarati| dvitīyadhyānabhūmiketi cet na adharālambanatvāyogāt| na hyadharabhūmyālamvanaḥ kaścidapi kleśa iṣyate| antarābhavasthā adrākṣuriti| antarābhavasthāste taṃ mahābrahyāṇaṃ duṣṭaivamūcuḥ imaṃ vayaṃ sattvamadrākṣmeti| ayamapi pakṣo na ghaṭate| tathāhi tatrāpyantarābhave dīrghamadhvānamavasthātuṃ na sambhavo hyupapattuṃ pratibandhābhāvāt śukaśoṇitānapekṣatvāt| teṣāṃ kathaṃ bhavatīyaṃ buddhirimaṃ vayaṃ sattvama drākṣma dīrghāyupaṃ dīghamadhvānaṃ tiṣṭhantamiti| dīrghādhvāvasthānāvabodho na prāpnotītyarthaḥ| tasmāttatrasthā eveti vistaraḥ| prathamadhyānabhūmiṣṭā eva tasya mahābrahyaṇaḥ pūrvavṛttāntaṃ samanusmarantaḥ| utpadyamānāvasthāyāmeva te taṃ mahābrahyāṇaṃ pūrvotpannaṃ dīrghamadhvānaṃ tiṣṭhantaṃ dṛṣṭavanto dṛṣṭā ca paścā uttarakālamadrākṣmetyeṣāṃ babhūva buddhiriti vākyaśeṣaḥ| evaṃ tu satīdamaparihṛtaṃ bhavet| asya ca sattvasyaivaṃ cetasaḥ praṇidhiḥ vayaṃ copapannā iti| upapattipratilambhikena prathamadhyānabhūmikena tasya mahāvrahyaṇa evaṃ cetasaḥ praṇidhimanvasmārṣuḥ|samānabhūmikatvādityadoṣa eva| tatsakāśaṃ śravaṇādvā|  
nanu ca śubhakṛtasnepvapyeṣa prasaṅgaḥ| ekatvakāyā nānātvasaṃjñina iti| katham yathoktam| te hi maulyāṃ bhūmau sukhendriyaparikhinnāḥ sāmantakopekṣendriyaṃ saṃmukhokurvantīti vistareṇa vaktavyaṃ syāt| te caikatvakāyā ekatvasaṃjñina iti paścadvakṣyate| tena sukheneti tṛtīyadhyānabhūmikena| cetasa utplāvakatvāditi audvilyakaratvāt| indrasukheneti dvitīyadhyāna āgamavyapagamasaṃjñitvāt| aciropapannānāmāgasaṃjñitvācciropapannānāṃ vyapagamasaṃjñitvāt| athavā'ciropapannānāmāgamasaṃjñitvācciropapannānāmāgamavyapagamasaṃjñitvāt| bhītā bhītasaṃjñitāt| yathākramamaciraciropapannatvāt te nānātvasaṃjñino na sukhāduḥkhāsukhasaṃjñitvāt yathokta vaibhāṣikairiti|  
prathame dhyāne kliṣṭayā saṃjñayeti| śllavrataparāmarśasaṃprayuktayā akāraṇe kāraṇābhiniviṣṭatvāt| dvitīye kuśalayā saṃjñayeti| sāmantakamaulasaṃgṛhītatvāt| tṛtīye vipākajayā saṃjñeti| vipākajasukhasamprayogāt|  
ārūpyāstrayo yathāsūtramiti| arūpiṇaḥ santi sattvā ye sarvaśo rūpasaṃjñānāṃ samatikramādanantamākaśamityākāśānantyāyatanamupasaṃpadya viharanti| tadyathā devā ākāśānantyāyatanopagāḥ| iyaṃ pañcamī vijñānasthitiḥ| arūpiṇaḥ santi sattvā ye sarvaśa ākāśānantyāyatanaṃ samatikramyānantyaṃ vijñānamiti vijñānānantyāyatanamupasaṃpadya viharanti| tad yathā vijñānānantyāyatanopagā devāḥ| iyaṃ ṣaṣṭī vijñānasthitiḥ| arūpiṇaḥ santi sattvā ye sarvaśo vijñānānantyāyatanaṃ samatikramya nāsti kiñcidityākiñcanyāyatanamupasaṃpadya viharanti| tadyathā ākiṃcanyāyatanopagā devāḥ| iyaṃ saptamī vijñānasthitiriti|  
pañcaskandhāścatvāraśca yathāyogamiti| ākāśānantyāyatanāditrayapratisaṃyuktāścatuskandhā anyapratisaṃyuktāḥ pañcaskandhāḥ|  
śeṣaṃ tatparibhedat

iti| paribhidyate'neneti paribhedaḥ tasya vijñānasya paribhedastatparibhedaḥ| so'smin vidyata iti tatparibhedavat| śeṣaṃ durgatyādi| apāyeṣu cobhayaṃ nāstīti| nehasthānāṃ gantukāmatā na tatrasthānāṃ vyuccalitukāmatā| apaṭupracāratvāditi| cittacaittānāmatra mandapracāratvāt| avalavadvijñānaṃ na tiṣṭhatīti yaktu vaktum|  
[ bhavāgrāsajñisattvāśca sattvāvasā nava smṛtāḥ|| 6||  
anicchāvasanaṃ nānye catasraḥ sthitasyaḥ punaḥ| ]

bhavāgrāsaṃjñisattvāścasattvāvasā nava

iti sūtra uktam| nava sattvāvāsāḥ| katame nava rūpiṇaḥ santi sattvā nānātvakāyā nānātvasaṃjñinaḥ| tadyathā manupyāḥ tadekatyāśca devāḥ ayaṃ prathamaḥ sattvāvāsaḥ| dvitīyatṛtīyacaturthā api yathā vijñānasthitiṣu tathā vaktavyāḥ| ayaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ| rūpiṇaḥ santi sattvā asaṃjñino'pratisaṃjñinaḥ| tadyathā devā asaṃjñisatvāḥ ayaṃ pañcamaḥ sattvāvāsaḥ| ārūpyāṇāñca sattvāvāsānāṃ trayo vijñānasthitivad vaktavyāḥ| caturthastvārūpyaḥ arūpiṇaḥ santi sattvāḥ sarvaśa ākiñcanyāyatanaṃ samatikramya naivasaṃgyānāsaṃjñāyatanamupasaṃpadya viharanti| tad yathā deva naivasaṃjñānāsaṃjñāyatanopagāḥ ayaṃ navamaḥ sattvāvāsa iti|  
asaṃjñisattvebhyo ye'nye caturthadhyānopagāḥ kiṃ te sattvāvāsā uta neti| netyāhuḥ| kasmādevamuktamācāryeṇa| ke punaranye apāyā iti mukhamātremetaduktam| asaṃjñisattvāḥ sattvāsāsaṃgṛhītāścaturthadhyānopagā ityapi vaktavyam| ihāpi tadeva kāranaṃ vaktavyaṃ yadvijñānasthitiṣūktam ihasthānāṃ hyapāyāgamanaprārthanā nāsti tatrasthānāñca na vastukāmateti nāpāyāḥ sattvāvāsāḥ| asaṃjñisattvāsaṃgṛhīteṣu bṛhatphaleṣu yadyapīhasthānāṃ gamanaprārthanāsti| tatrāsthānāṃ pṛthagjanānā māsaṃjñikapravivikṣā bhavati| āryāṇāṃ tvanabhrakapuṇyaprasaveṣvapi śuddhāvāsārūpyapravivikṣā| parinirvātukāmatā ca śuddhāvāsānāmapīti na teṣāṃ tatrāvasthānabuddhiḥ| ato na te sattvāvāsā ityācāryasaṅghabhadraḥ| anye punarvyācakṣate| ke punaranye apāyā iti| apāyānāmeva vacanādanabhakrādayaḥ sattvāvāsā iṣṭā ācāryasyeti| tairyuktiranveṣṭavyā| bandhanasthānavaditi| yathā bandhanasthānāni na sattvāvāsāḥ anichāvasanāt evamapāyā iti|  
[ catvāraḥ sāsravāḥ skandhā svabhūmāveva kevalam|| 7||  
vijñānaṃ na sthitiḥ prokta catuḥkoṭistu samgrahe| ]

rūpopagā vijñānasthitiriti| vijñānaṃ tiṣṭhatyasyāmiti vijñānasthitiḥ| upa gacchatīti upagā| vijñānasya samīpacāriṇī ityarthaḥ| rūpaṃ ca tadupagā ca sā rūpopagā| evaṃ yāvat saṃskārāśca te upagā ca saṃskāropageti| bhagavadviśeṣastu vyācaṣṭe| rūpopagā vijñānasthitiryāvat saṃskāropageti| sautrāntikānāṃ nayenocyate| tatra vijñānasthitirbhavasantatyanucchedaḥ rūpamupagamasyā vijñānasthiteḥ seyaṃ rūpopagā upagamiti upagamyate tadātmīyata iti| evaṃ yāvat saṃskārā upagā asyā vijñānasthiteḥ seyaṃ saṃskāropagā| athavā tṛṣṇā sthitiḥ tiṣṭhatyanayā vijñāna punarbhavasantatyanucchedāditi kṛtvā vijñānasya sthitirvijñānasthitiḥ| sā rūpamupagacchati tadabhiṣvaṅgata iti rūpopagā| evaṃ yāvat saṃskāropagā| vaibhāṣikāṇāṃ punarnayena kārakartho na śakyate yojayitumiti dūṣayati| atra brūmaḥ dvividhe'pyasmin vyākhyāne rūpādivyatiriktā vijñānasthitirūktā| tā hi

catvāraḥ sāsaravāḥ skandhāḥ

iti

catuṣkoṭistu saṃgrahe

ityetat sarvaṃ nirastaṃ bhavatīti doṣāntaram| tasmāt pūrvakameva vyākhyānaṃ sādhu etadavaibhāṣikavyākhyānaṃ varṇayāmo na tairevaṃ vyākhyātam| rūpamupagacchatīti rūpopagā vijñānasthitirityādi| apare punarvyācakṣate rūpopagā vijñanasthitiriti rūpasvabhāvetyarthaḥ| yathānyatrokata khakkhaṭakharagatamiti| svabhāvo'rtho gatyartha ityabhiprāyaḥ|  
sthātuḥ parivarjanena| anyo loke sthāta devadattādiranyā sthitiḥ sthānaṃ gṛhādikamityarthaḥ| vijñānaṃ vāhayati pravartayatītyarthaḥ| naunāvikanyāyena| yathā nāviko nāvaṃ vāhayati tadvat| na tu vijñānaṃ vijñānamevābhirūhya vāhayatītyarthaḥ| ekasantāne vijñānadvayasamavadhānam| parasantānavijñānaṃ tu yadyapi yugapad bhavati na tu taditarasya samīpe vartata iti na tattasya sthitirbhavati| nandī saumanasyam| sapta ca vijñānasthitasyaḥ pañcaskandhasvabhāvāstat kathamiti| saptasu vijñānasthitiṣu pañcaskandhasvabhāvatveneṣṭāsu| tat kathaṃ vijñānaṃ na vijñānasthitiriṣṭamiti| evaṃ tarhīti vistaraḥ| abhedenaikapiṇḍarūpeṇa rūpādīnāṃ skandhānāmupapattyāyatanasaṃgṛhīteṣu skandheṣu manuṣyādinikāyasabhāgasaṃgṛhīteṣu sābhirāmāyāṃ sābhiratyāṃ vijñānapravṛttau vijñānaṃ vijñānasthitiḥ saptavijñānasthitideśanāyāmabhiprīyate| pratyekaṃ tvekamekaṃ skandhaṃ prati| yathā rūpādayo vijñānasya saṃkleśāya bhavanti āśrayasamprayogasahabhūbhāvenopagatatvāt| naivam kevalam| pṛthag| vijñānam kiṃ saṃkleśāya bhavatītyadhikṛtam| kasmāt yugapadāśrayatvādyayogāt| tasmāccaturvijñānasthitideśanāyām kevalam| pṛthag

vijñānaṃ na sthitiḥ proktam|  
tatā vineyajanāpekṣayā hyekatra sūtre anyathā vijñānasthitirūktā anyatra cānyatheti ata āha| api ca kṣetrabhāveneti vistaraḥ| vijñānasya kṣetrabhāvena catasro vijñānasthitayo rūpavedanāsaṃjñāsaṃsaṃskārasvabhāvā deśitāḥ| vijñānaṃ bījabhāvena vapanīyarūpena sopādānam| svabhūmikayā tṛṣṇayā satṛṣṇam| kṛtasnameva santānagataṃ deśitamiti| na punarvījaṃ vījasya kṣetrabhāvena vyavasthāpayāṃbabhūva bhagavān| nahi loke bījaṃ kṣetrabhāvena vyavasthāpyate| ye ca dharā iti vistaraḥ| ye ca dharmā vijñānasya sahavartino rūpādayaḥ| ta eva kṣetrabhāvena sādhutarā bhavanti| sahavati hi bījasya kṣetraṃ loke dṛśyate| vijñānaṃ tu vijñānena saha navartate yugapadabhāvāt| atītānāgatāstarhi rūpādayo na vijñānasthitayaḥ prāpnuvanti| te'pyatītānāgatā yathāsvamatītānāgatānāṃ vijñānānāṃ sthitayo bhavanti| tasmāt svabhūmau sarva eva sāsravā rūpādayo vijñānasthitayo bhavantītyavagantavyam|  
prathamā koṭiriti vistaraḥ| saptasu yadvijñānamiti pañcaskandhasvabhāvatvāt saptānāṃ vijñānasthitīnām| tatra vijñānaṃ saṃgṛhītaṃ na catasṛṣu vijñānasya tatrāgrahaṇāt| vijñānavarjyāḥ skandhā iti apāyādiṣvapi vijñānaṃ na catasṛṣu saṃgṛhītaṃ na saptasvapāyādiṣu vijñānaparibhedenāgrahaṇā| saptasu catvāraḥ skandhā iti| saptasu vijñānasthitiṣu ye vijñānavarjyāścatvāraḥ skandhā uktāḥ te catasṛṣvapi vijñānasthitiṣu saṃgṛhītā ityubhayatrāpi saṃgṛhītā iti tṛtīyā koṭiḥ| caturthyetānākārān sthāpayitveti| apāyeṣu caturthe dhyān bhavāgre ca yadvijñānamanāsravāśca dharmāḥ |  
[ catasro yonayastatra sattvānamaṇḍajādayaḥ|| 8||  
caturdhā naratiryañco nārakā upapādukāḥ|  
antarābhavadevāśca pretā api jarāyujāḥ|| 9|| ]

yaccaitad gatyādibhedabhinnamiti| ādiśabdena bhājanāntarābhavabhūmyādigṛhyate| jarāyuryena mātuḥ kukṣau garbho veṣṭitastiṣṭhati tasmājjātā jarāyujāḥ| bhūtasaṃsvedajā iti| bhūtānāṃ pṛthivyādīnāṃ saṃsvedāddavatvalakṣaṇājjātā bhūtasaṃsvedajāḥ|  
avikalāścakṣurindriyādyavaikalyāt| ahīnendriyāḥ kāṇavibhrāntāderabhāvāt| sarvāṅgairūpetā hastapādādibhiḥ| savaiśca pratyaṅgairaṅgulyādibhirūpetāḥ| sakṛdupajāyante na kalalādyanupūrvyā aṇḍajādivat| upapādane upapattau sādhukāritvāt| śukaśoṇitādyanupādānena sakṛdupajātatvāt| upapādukā ityucyante| kraucīnirjātāviti vistaraḥ| bhinnayānapātrau kila vaṇijau samudratīre kaucīṃ samabhigatau| tato nirjātau śailopaśailau sthavirāviti| pañcālarājasyeti| tasya mahādevyāḥ pañcāṇḍaśatāni jātāni| tena rājñā maṃjūṣāyāṃ prakṣipya gaṅgāyāmavavāhitāni| licchavirājena sāntaḥ pureṇa snāyatā sā maṃjūṣohyamānā dṛṣṭā udghāṭitā ca| tasyāṃ pañcaśatāni dārakāṇāṃ dṛṣṭāni gṛhītāni ceti| māṃdhātṛprabhṛtayaḥ saṃsvedajāḥ| katham upoṣadhasya kila rājño mūrdhi piṭako jātaḥ tasya vṛdheranvayātparipākānvayāt paribhedānvayaddārako jātaḥ so'yaṃ māndhāteti saṃsvedajo bhavati| rājñaḥ khalvapi māndhāturjānūpari piṭakau jātau tayorvṛddheranvayāt pūrvavadyāvaddārakau jātau tāvimo cārūpacārau| brahyadattasya kila rājña urasi piṭako jātaḥ| tasya vṛddheranvayāt pūrvavadyāvaddārikā jātā seyaṃ kapotamālinīti| āmrapālyapi kadalīstaṃbhājjāteti śrūyate| prāthamakalpikā iti|  
prāgāsaṃ rūpivat sattvāḥ

iti vacanāt| suparṇī garūḍaḥ| pretānāṃ jarāyujatvasiddhayarthamucyate| āyuṣmata iti vistaraḥ| kathaṃ gamyate jarāyujatvasiddhayarthamiti| rātrau pañcatāṃ janitveti vacanāt| nanu ca sutarāmupapādukatvasiddhayarthamiti gamyate| pañca rātau

divāpañca tathā'parān| nāsti tṛptisthāpi me iti vacanāt| jarāyujairhi bhakṣitaistṛptirbhavet| jarāyujasiddhayarthameva nopapādukasiddhayartham| rātrau pañcānāṃ sakṛjjanma divā cāpareṣāṃ pañcānāṃ na virūdhayte| tāvatkālena tadātmabhāvaparpūritaḥ| krameṇāpi ca rātrau pañcānāṃ divāpareṣāṃ pañcānāṃ janma na virūdhyate| sattvajātiḥ sā tādṛśī karaṇāṃ cācintyo vipāka iti| abhipravṛddhajighāṃsādoṣatvāttu nāpi tṛptirastīti|  
prāptopapattivaśitvo'pītyanena jarāyujopapattau karmabalātkārāyogaṃ darśayati| yathānyatīrthaśāstre ṛddhiṃ bhadanta ko darśayati māyāvī gautama iti| tathā bhagavantamevoddiśyānyatroktam| kalpaśatasyātyayādevaṃvidho loke māyāvī prādubhuya māyayā lokaṃ bhakṣayatīti upajīvatītyarthaḥ| bāhyabījābhāvāditi| bāhyasya śukaśoṇitakardamāderabhāvādityarthaḥ| ādhiṣṭhānikīmiti yadadhitiṣṭhati idamevaṃ bhavatviti tadadhiṣṭhānam| tatprayojanamasyāstatra vā bhavā ṛddhirādhiṣṭhānikī| tāmicchatāṃ bauddhānāṃ na yukta epa parihāra iti| katamaḥ| ya uktaḥ| śarīradhātūnāmavasthāpanārthamiti|  
praśāta praśāntaramutpadyata iti| atha kimartha caramabhivako bodhosattva iti praśno ukto vistareṇa| tatra coktam śarīradhātūnāmavasthāpanārthamiti vistaraḥ| tasmāt praśnādidaṃ praśnāntaramutpadyate| yadyu papādukānāmiti vistaraḥ| kāyanidhanaṃ kāyanāśaḥ| upapādukaḥ suparṇī upapādukaṃ nāgamiti viśeṣaṇadvayaṃ kimartham kimanupapādukā api suparṇino nāgāśca santi| santītyāhuḥ| caturvidhā hi garūḍā nāgāścaṇḍajādibhedāt| teṣāmupapādukā uttamāḥ saṃvedajā upottamāḥ jarāyujā madhyāḥ aṇḍajā jaghanyāḥ| tatropapādaukaḥ suparṇī sarvānupapādakādīnuddharati bhakṣārtham saṃvedajastrīn saṃsvedajādīn jarāyujo dvau jarāyujāṇḍajau aṇḍajo'ṇḍajameveti varṇayanti| yāvannamṛta iti yadā jīvati tadā tanmāṃsādi nāntardhīyata iti nidarśitaṃ bhavati| na punarmṛtasyāsya tṛpyatīti| na punarmṛtānena tṛpyatītyarthaḥ| tṛpipūrau vibhāṣeti vibhāṣāṣaṣṭhīyate| tasya mṛtasyākāśasyeva bhakṣitasya māṃsapiṇḍāntardhānānna tṛpyatītyavagantavyam|  
sa hi dve gatī iti narakagatirdevagatiśca| tisṛṇāṃ ca pradeśa iti tiryakpretamanuṣya gatyantarālatvāt||  
[ mṛtyūpapattibhavayārantarā bhavatīha yaḥ|  
gamyadeśānupetatvānnopapanno'ntarābhavaḥ|| 10|| ]

gatyorantarālatvāt| nanu cāvyākṛtaiva gatiriṣyate| upapattibhavaścaikāntena kliṣṭaḥ| maraṇabhavo'pi kadācit kuśalaḥ kliṣṭo vā bhavati na gatiḥ| katha tayorgatiśabdamadhyāropya gatyantarālatvādityucyate| naiṣa doṣaḥ| maraṇopapattibhavayoranivṛtāvyākṛtānāṃ nikāyasabhāgajīvitendriyajātyādīnāṃ gatisvabhāvānāṃ tatkāle vidyamānatvāt| kāmarūpadhātvośca kāyendriyasyāpyavaśyambhāvāt| na copapattivanmarāṇabhavo'vaśyaṃ kliṣṭo nāpyavaśyaṃ kuśalaḥ| tasmātteṣu gatiśabdamāropya gatyantarālatvādityuktam| pade gatyarthatvāditi| padagatāviti paṭhyate| tenopapannaśabdasyopagatārtha darśayati

nopapanno'ntarābhavaḥ|  
kiṃ tarhi upapaydamāna iti| abhivyaktiḥ samāptiśceti| ākṣepakeṇa karmaṇā nikāyasabhāgasyābhivyaktiḥ paripūrakaiḥ parisamāptiḥ sarvasmin janmani karmadvayasya vyāpārāt|  
ekaṃ janmākṣipatyekamanekaṃ paripūrakam

iti vacanāt| athavā yatra deśe ākṣiptasya karmaṇā nāmarūpasya vipākasya prādurbhāvo'bhivyaktiḥ ṣaḍāyatanapūritaśca samāptiḥ sa deśo'vagantavyaḥ|| 10||  
[ brīhisantānasādharmyādavicchinnabhavodbhavaḥ|  
pratibimbamasiddhatvādasāmyāccānidarśanam|| 11|| ]

grīhisantānasādharmyāt

iti vistaraḥ| saṃbandhnaṃstānaḥ saṃtāno vrīheḥ saṃtāno vrīhisantānaḥ| tena sādharmyādavicchinnasya bhavasyodbhavo bhavati yato'paiti yatra cotpadyate tadantarālasantānavartirūpapūrvakamupapattibhavarūpam svopādānarūpasantānarūpasvabhāvatvāt vrīhisantānapaścāttararūpavat| santānavartināṃ hi dharmāṇāmavicchedena deśāntarotpattyā deśāntareṣu prādurbhāvo dṛṣṭaḥ| tad yathā vrīhisantānasya| vrīhisantānapaścāttararūpaṃ hi yato deśāntarādapaiti yatra ca deśāntara utpadyate tadantarālasantānavartirūpapūrvakamutpadyate| kṣaṇikavādināṃ hyakmasmākaṃ siddhāntaḥ| yadā grāmādgāmāntaraṃ nīyate vrīhiḥ na sa vrīhiḥ purvatra grāme nirūdhya tadgāmāntarāle'nutpadyamāno'nekayojanāntarite'pi grāmāntara utpadyate| kiṃ tarhi nirantarākāśadeśotpādanirodhakrameṇotpadyate grāmāntare| tacca svopādānarūpasantānarūpasvabhāvam| tathopapattibhavarūpamapyutpadyata iti| atrācāryaguṇamatiḥ saha śiṣyeṇācāryavasumitreṇa svanikāyānurāgabhāvitamatirvyākhyānavyāpāramapāsya pratyavasthāpare eva vartate| vayamiha śāstrārthavivaraṇaṃ pratyādriyāmahe na taddūṣaṇam niḥsāratvāt bahuvaktavyabhayācca| bhaviṣṇurbhavanaśīlaḥ| vicchinno'pīti vistaraḥ| ādarśādiṣu bimbāt pratibimbamiti| ādarśodakādiṣu bimbānmukhāt pratibimbaṃ mukhacchāyārūpaṃ vicchinnamutpadyamānaṃ dṛṣtamityanenānaikāntikatāmudgrāhayati pratisādhanaṃ vā karoti yato'paiti yatra cotpadyate na tadantarālasantānavartirūpapūrvakamupapattibhavarūpam svopādānarūpsantānarūpasvabhāvatvāt pratibimbarūpavaditi| pratibimba nāmānyadevotpadyate dharmāntaramityasiddhametaditi| bimbasāmarthyādeva tatrādarśādiṣu pratibimvākāraṃ bhrāntaṃ vijñānamutpadyata ityācāryasyābhiprāyaḥ| na tatra pratibimbaṃ nāma kiñcidastītyanena dharmyasiddhirnāma dṛṣṭāntadoṣaṃ darśayati| tasmānnānaikāntikatāsti| na caitat pratisādhanaṃ sādhviti| siddhavapi ca satyām

asāmyādanidarśanam

iti| pratibimbasya dravyasattvena siddhāvapi satyāmasāmyāddārṣṭāntikena na nidarśanamanudāharaṇaṃ pratibimbakamiti| kathaṃ tāvadasiddhamiti tāvacchabdaḥ krame|| 11||  
asiddhatāyukti tāvat darśayannāha

[ sahaikatra dvayābhāvādasantānāddayodayāt|  
kaṇṭhokteścāsti gandharvaḥ pañcoktergatisūtrataḥ|| 12|| ]

sahaikatra dvayābhāvāt

iti vistaraḥ| tatraiva hi ādarśadeśe ādarśarūpaṃ dṛśyate pārśvasthitena na pratibimbakam| pratibimbakaṃ ca tatraiva dṛśyate'bhimukhāvasthitena nādarśarūpam| na caikatra deśe upādayarūpadvayasyāsti sahabhāvaḥ| kiṃ kāraṇam āśrayabhūtabhedāt| āśrayabhūtāni hi tayorbhinnānīti| nāstyādarśarūpāśrayabhūtairavaṣṭavdhe deśe pratibimbakarūpāśrayabhūtānāṃ tatrāvakāśaḥ| ato nopādāya rūpadvayasyāsti sambhavaḥ| tadevaṃ yugapadvijñānadvayasyābhāvāt upādāyarūpadvayasya caikatra sahabhāvābhāvāt na tat kiñcidastīti darśayati| yatraiva hi deśe ādarśarūpaṃ dṛśyata iti bimvakaṃ ca tatraiva tvanyatra| ko doṣa ityāha| na caikatra rūpadvayasyāti sahabhāva iti pūrvavadvācyam| athavā sahaikatra dvayābhāvādityasya sūtrasyāyamarthaḥ| ekatra rūpe purūṣadvayasya paśyata evābhāvāt| sahadarśanasya pratibimvamasiddhamiti vākyādhyāhāraḥ| kathamityāha digbhedavyavasthitaiḥ purūṣaikairūttaradigvyavasthitairitarairdakṣiṇadigvyavasthitaiḥ ekasmin vāpyambudeśe ekasmistaṭākajaladeśe svābhimukhadeśasthitānāṃ rūpāṇāṃ dakṣiṇataṭordhvasthitāni rūpāṇyuttaradigvyavasthitapurūṣasvābhimukhasthānyuttarataṭordhvasthitānyapi rūpāṇi dakṣiṇadigvyavasthitapurūṣasyābhimukhasthāni teṣāmubhayeṣām| kim pratibimbakamanyonyamupalabhyate uttaradigvyavasthitairdakṣiṇataṭādhaḥ prativimvakamupalabhyate dakṣiṇadigvyavasthitairapyuttarataṭādhaḥ prativimbakamupalabhyate| evamanyonyamupalabhate na tūbhayaṃ yugapadupalabhyata iti vākyārthaḥ| na hi svataṭādhaḥ prativimvakamekataṭasthaiḥ samantato'pi vāpījalaṃ paśyadbhirūpalabhyate| tataḥ kimiti cedata āha| na tvekatra rūpe ghaṭ paṭe vā dvayoḥ paśyutoḥ purūṣayoḥ sahadarśanaṃ na bhavati  bhavatyevetyarthaḥ| iha tu na bhavati sahadarśanamityato na tatra rūpāntarotpattiryukteti| athavāsyāyamarthaḥ| chāyātapayośca dvayoḥ sahaikatra bhāvo na dṛṣṭo loke| yatrachāyā bhavati maṇḍape'nyatra vā na tatrātapo yatra vātapo bhavatyabhyavakāśe na tatra chāyā upalabhyate ca chāyāstha ādarśe sūryasya pratibimbakamiti| katham taṭāke sūryapratibimbakamutpadyate| tattaṭākataṭasthe ca chāyāvati maṇḍape ādarśa sthāpyate| tatrādarśake taṭākasthasya sūryapratibimbakasyāparaṃ pratibimbakamutpadyate| taṭāke ca yat sūryapratibimbakaṃ tat sūryasamutthamityavaśyamātapasvabhāvamityabhyupagantavyam| yaccāparamādarśe pratibimbakaṃ tadātapasvabhāvapratibimbakasamutthamityetadapyavaśyaṃ ātapasvabhāvamabhyupagantavyam| evaṃ sati chāyātapayoḥ sahasvabhāvaḥ syāt| na cānyatra loke chāyātapayoḥ sahabhāva iti| na yukto'sya pratibimbakasya tatra prādurbhāvaḥ| kūpa ivodakamiti| yathā kūpe dūrāntargatamudakaṃ dṛśyate| tathā candrapratibimbakamapyatraivāntargataṃ dṛśyate| na cādarśastāvat vastuphalo'sti| tacca tatrotpadyamānam tacca pratibimbakamādarśa evotpadyamānam| nānyatrādhastā dupakabhyate| upalabhyate ca ato nāsyeva tat kiñcit| sāmagyāstu bimvādarśadilakṣaṇāyāḥ| sā tasyāḥ prabhāvo yattathā darśanaṃ bhavati| acintyo hi dharmāṇāṃ śaktibhedaḥ ayaso'yaskāntābhigamanadarśanānna kāṣṭhādīnāṃ yathā candrakāntāccandrodaye'saṃbhavaprakṣaraṇadarśanaṃ nāṅgārādīnāmityevamādi vaktavyam| ādarśasantānasambaddhatvāditi| na bimbasantānabhūtaṃ pratibimbamādarśasambandhatvādādarśarūpavat| sahabhāvācca vimvasamānakālarūpāntaravat| tadevaṃ yathā maraṇabhavasyopapattibhavaḥ santānabhūto naivaṃ bimbasya pratibimbakamiti sādhanānvitaḥ prativimvakadṛṣṭāntaḥ| vrīhisantānapaścāttararūpadṛṣṭāntastu svasantānapūrvarūpasantānabhūta iti| tasmādasāmyaṃ dṛṣṭāntasya pratibimbasya ceti|  
dvayodayāt

iti vistaraḥ| dvayonodayo dvayodayaḥ| tasmādvimvāccādarśāccetyarthaḥ| yat pradhānaṃ kāraṇamādarśādi tadāśriyotpadyate pratibimbam| ādarśādi hi pratibimbasya pradhānaṃ kāraṇaṃ tadanuvidhānāt| tad yathā yadyasistiryagavasthito bhavati pratibimbamapi tiryagāyataṃ ca dṛśyate| yadyūrdhvamavasthito bhavati prativimvamapi tathaiva dṛśyate| na caivamupapattibhavasyāpi dvābhyāṃ kāraṇābhyāṃ sambhavo maraṇabhavāccānyataśca pradhānabhūtādādarśasthānīyāt| brāhya śukaśoṇitaṃ pradhānakāraṇamiti cet na acetanatvāt| upapādukānāṃ ca tadabhāvādato'pyayamasamāno dṛṣṭāntaḥ| kimanena darśitaṃ bhavati| utkālitaviśeṣaṇe hetāvayamatulyo dṛṣṭānta iti| kathamiti yato'paiti yatra cotpadyate tadantrālasantānavartirūpapurvakamupapattibhavarūpam| advayotpannasvopādānarūpasantānarūpasvabhāvatvāt| vrīhisantānapaścāttararūpavaditi| tadevaṃ sati nāsya sādhanasyānaikāntikā śakyamudgrāhayitu nāpi pratisādhanaṃ kartumityukta bhavati| āgamenāntarābhavasyāstitvaṃ sādhayannāha

kaṇṭhokteścāsti

iti| svaśabdābhidhānādityarthaḥ naitat sūtra tairāmnāyata iti| tairnikāyāntarīyaiḥ kasmānnamnāyate| tadāgameṣvabhāvāt| nikāyāntarāgamaprasiddhaṃ kimiti na pamāṇaṃ kriyate| mūlasaṃgītibhraśena samāropitasūtrāśaṃkitatvāt| trayāṇāṃ sthānānāmiti| trayāṇāṃ hetūnāṃ mātā kalyā mātā nīrogā ṛtumatī rajasvalā tadetadubhayaṃ prathamaṃ sthānaṃ bhavati| raktau sannipatitāviti maithunadharmaṃ kurvantau idaṃ dvitīyaṃ sthānam| gandharvaśca pratyupasthita iti tṛtīyam  skandhabhedaśca pratyupasthita iti maraṇabhavaḥ| pañcānāgāminaḥ sapta satpurūṣagatayaḥ pudgalanideśakośasthāne vakṣyante| upapadyādayopīti| upapadyā nāma te devā ityevamādayaḥ saṃprajyante| sapta satpurūṣagataya iti| eta eva pañcāntarāparinirvāyiṇaṃ tridhā bhittvā sapta bhavanti tenāha antarāparinirvāyiṇastraya uktāḥ| kālaprakarṣabhedeneti| kālaprakarṣabhedena deśaprakarṣabhedena ca yathāyogam sūtraṃ cātra paṭhyate śrāvastyāṃ nidānam| tatra bhagavān bhikṣūnāmantrayate sma| sapta vo'haṃ bhikṣavaḥ satpurūṣagatīrdeśayiṣyāmyanupādāya ca parinirvāṇam tacchṛṇuta sādhu ca suṣṭhu ca manasikurūta bhāṣiṣye| sapta satpurūṣagatayaḥ katamāḥ iha bhikṣurevaṃ pratipanno bhavati| no ca syāṃ no ca me syāt na bhaviṣyāmi na me bhaviṣyati yadasti yad bhūtaṃ tat prajahāmītyupeṣāṃ pratilabhate| sa bhave'sminna rajyate sa bhave'sminna sajyate|  
athottaraṃ padaṃ śāntaṃ prajñayā pratividhyati

taccānena padaṃ kāyena sākṣātkṛtaṃ bhavati| evaṃ pratipannasya bhikṣoḥ kā gatiḥ syāt kopapattiḥ ko'bhisaṃparāya iti syuḥ praṣṭāraḥ| tad yathā bhikṣavaḥ parīttaśakalikāgnirabhinivartamāna eva nirvāyāt evameva tasya tāvanmānāvaśeṣamaprahīnaṃ bhavatyaparijñātam| tasya tāvanmānāvaśeṣasyāprahāṇādaparijñānāt pañcānāmavarabhāgīyānāṃ saṃyojanānāṃ prahāṇādantarāparinirvāyī bhavati| iyaṃ prathamā satpurūṣagatirākhyātā| punaraparaṃ bhikṣurevaṃ pratipanno bhavati| no ca syāmiti pūrvavat yāvat syuḥ praṣṭāra iti| tad yathā 'yoguḍānāṃ vāyasphālānāṃ vā pradīptāgnisaṃprataptānāmayoghanena hanyamānā nāmayasprapāṭikā utpatantyeva nirvāyāt evameva tasya pūrvavat yāvat pañcānāmavarabhāgīyānāṃ saṃyojanānāṃ prahāṇādantarāparinirvāyī bhavati| iyaṃ dvitīyā satpurūṣagatiḥ| punaraparaṃ bhikṣurevaṃ pratipanno bhavati| pūrvavadyāvadayasprapāṭikā utplutyā patitvaiva pṛthivyāṃ nirvāyāt| evameva tasya pūrvavad yāvadantarāparinirvāyī bhavati| iyaṃ tṛtīyā satpurūṣagatiḥ| punaraparaṃ bhikṣurevaṃ pratipanno bhavatīti pūrvavad yāvadayasprapāṭikā utplutya patitamātraiva pṛthivyāṃ nirvāyāt| evameva tasya pūrvavad yāvat pañcānāmavarabhāgīyānāṃ saṃyojanānāṃ prahāṇādupapadyaparinirvāyī bhavati| iyaṃ caturthī satpurūṣagatiḥ| punaraparaṃ bhikṣurevaṃ pratipanna iti pūrvavad yāvadayasprapāṭikā utplutya parītte tṛṇakāṣṭhe nipatet| sā tatra dhūmamapi kuryādarcirapi saṃjanayet| sā tatra dhūmamapi kṛtvā'cirapi saṃjanayya tadevaṃ parīttaṃ tṛṇakāṣṭha dagdhvā paryādāya nirūpādānā nirvāyāt| evameva tasya pūrvadayāvat pañcānāmavarabhāgīyānāṃ saṃyojanānāṃ prahāṇadanabhisaṃskāraparinirvāyī bhavati| iyaṃ pañcamī satpurūṣagatiḥ| punaraparaṃ bhikṣurevaṃ pratipanna iti pūrvavad yāvadayasprapāṭikā utplutya mahati vipule tṛṇakāṣṭhe nipatet| sā tatra dhūmamapi kuryādarcirapi saṃjanayet| sā tatra dhūmamapi kṛtvā'rcirapi saṃjanayya tadeva mahad vipulaṃ tṛṇakāṣṭha dagdhvā paryādāya nirūpādānā parinirvāyāt| evameva tasya pūrvavad yāvat pañcānāmavarabhāgīyānāṃ saṃyojanānāṃ prahāṇāt sābhisaṃskāraparinirvāyī bhavati| iyaṃ ṣaṣṭhī satpurūṣagatiḥ| punaraparaṃ bhikṣurevaṃ pratipanna iti pūrvavad yāvadasprapāṭikā utplutya mahati vipule tṛṇkāṣṭhe nipatet sā tatra dhūmamapi kuryādarcirapi saṃjanayet sā tatra dhūmamapi kṛtvārcirapi saṃjanayya tadeva mahad vipulaṃ tṛṇakāṣṭha dagdhvā grāmamapi dahed grāmapradeśamapi nagaramapi nagarapradeśamapi janapadamapi janapadapradeśamapi kakṣamapi dāvamapi dvīpamapi ṣaṇḍamapi dahet| grāmamapi dagdhvā yāvat ṣaṇḍamapi dagdhvā mārge hyagamya udakāntaṃ vā'lpaharitakaṃ vā pṛthivīpradeśamāgamya paryādāya nirūpādānā nirvāyāt| evameva tasya yāvat pañcānāmavarabhāgīyānāṃ saṃyojanānāṃ prahāṇadūrdhvasrotā bhavati| iyaṃ saptamī satpurūṣagatirākhyātā| anupādāya parinirvāṇaṃ katamat iha bhikṣurevaṃ iti pūrvavad yāvat syuḥ praṣṭāra iti| tasyaivaṃ pratipannasya bhikṣorna pūrvasyāṃ diśi gatiṃ vadāmi na dakṣiṇasyāṃ na paścimāyāṃ nottarasyāṃ nordhva nādho nānuvidikṣu nānyatra dṛṣṭa eva dharmeniśchāyaṃ parinirvṛtaṃ śītībhūtaṃ brahībhūtamiti| idamucyate'nupādāya parinirvāṇam| sapta vo'haṃ bhikṣavaḥ satpurūṣagatīrdeśayiṣyāmi| anupādāya ca parinirvāṇamiti yaduktam idametat pratyuktam| anye punarāhuritivistaraḥ| āyuṣaḥ pramāṇaṃ tasyāntaraṃ tasmin āyuḥpramāṇāntare'parisamāpta āyuṣītyarthaḥ| devasamīpāntare vā| devanāmantikaṃ devasamīpaṃ gatasyāntare yaḥ kleśān prajahāti so'ntarāparinirvāyī| sa punardhātugato vā parinirvāti yaḥ kleśavījāvasthāyāmupapannamātra asamudācārakleśa ityarthaḥ| saṃjñāgato vipasaṃjñāsamudācārāvasthāyaṃ yaḥ parinirvāti| dhātugatācciratareṇāyaṃ parinirvāti| vitarkagato vā vitarkasamudācāravasthāyāṃ yaḥ parinirvāti| ayamapi saṃjñagatācciratareṇa parinirvāti| tena trividho bhavatyantarāparinirvāyī yathoktena dṛṣṭāntatrayeṇa| prathamo vā nikāyasabhāgaparigrahaṃ kṛtvā jātamātra ityarthaḥ| yaṃ pratyeṣa parīttaḥ śakalikāgniriti dṛṣṭāntaḥ| dvitīyo devasamṛddhiṃ cānubhūya parinirvātīti yaṃ pratyeṣa dṛṣṭāntaḥ| tad yathā'yasprapāṭiketi| tṛtīyo dharmasaṃgītimanupraviśya dharmasāṃkathyamanupraviśay parinirvāti yaṃ pratyeṣa dṛṣṭāntaḥ  tad yathā'yasprapāṭikā utplutya pṛthivyāmapatitaiva parinirvāyāt tadevaṃ vināpyantarābhavena yathāntarāparinirvāyī bhavati| yathā ca tasya dṛṣṭāntatrayeṇa trividho bhedastathā sādhitaṃ nikāyāntarīyaiḥ| tasmin sādhite idaṃ codyeta| yadi dharmasaṅgītiman upraviśya yaḥ parinirvāti sāntarāparinirvāyīṣyate| kīdṛśa upapadyaparinirvāyī bhaviṣyatītyata āha| upapadyaparinirvāyī punaḥ prakarṣa yuktāṃ saṅgītimanupraviśya parinirvātīti| prakarṣayogādutpattyartho bhavatītyabhiprāyaḥ| saṅgītyanupraveśasāmānyānnaiṣa bhedo yujyata iti vacanāvakāśaṃ matvā punarāha bhūyasā vāyurapahatyeti āyuṣo bahu kṣayitvā parinirvātītyarthaḥ|  
ta eta iti vistaraḥ ta ete sarve'pi dhātugatādayastrayaḥ śakalikādibhirdṛṣṭāntairsambadhyante| kasmāt deśagativiśeṣābhāvāt deśagateviśeṣasyābhāvāt na hyeṣāṃ deśagativiśeṣo'sti tatraiva deśe parinirvāṇāt| sūtroktānāṃ trayāṇāṃ dṛṣṭāntānāṃ derśagativiśeṣo'stīti naibhairete sambadhyante| cakravartisaṃvartanīyāni karmāṇi vipākābhinivṛttau kadācideva vṛtti labhante bahupratyayāpekṣitvāt| sambhavaiṣitvādupapattyabhilāṣitvāt| karmāṇyeva pratyayānāṃ sāmagrīmāvahantīti| karmādhipatyenāśvādīnāṃ prasiddhaṃ kālamatikramya kālānyare'pi maithunaṃ bhavatīti| ekakarmākṣepāditi| yenaiva karmaṇā gavādinikāyasabhāga ākṣipyate tenaiva karmaṇā tadantarābhava ākṣipyata ityato vaktavyametat yaduktaṃ yenānyatra kāle goṣūpapattavyaṃ sa gavayeṣūpapadyata iti vistareṇa| na nikāyabhedādekākṣepakatvaṃ hīyate goṣūpapattavyaṃ sa gavayeṣūpapadyata iti vistareṇa| na nikāyabhedādekākṣepakatvaṃ hīyate tatkarmaṇa ekajātīyatvāt gatyākṛtisaṃsthānāntarāparityāgācca| gatiniyatānāṃ hi karmaṇāmupapattivaicitryaṃ dṛṣṭam| kalmāpapādādivaditi| nāstyeṣa doṣa ityācāryasaṅghabhadraḥ|| 14 ||  
[ viparyastamatiryāti gatideśaṃ riraṃsayā|  
gandhasthānābhikāmo'nya ūrdhvapādastu nārakaḥ|| 15|| ]

viparyastamatiḥ

iti anunayasahagatena vā pratighasahagatena vā cittena yathā viparyastamatirbhavati tad darśayannāha| tatrāsya pitroroti vistaraḥ| mātāpitrostāṃ vipratipattiṃ dvīndriyasamāpattilakṣaṇam| puṃsaḥ sataḥ purūśasyāntarābhavasya pūṃso'yaṃ pausno rāga utpadyate mātari bhāryāyāmiva| striyā ayaṃ straiṇo rāga utpadyate pitari bhartarīva| viparyayāt pratigha iti puṃsaḥ sataḥ pausnaḥ pratigha īrṣyotthānabhūta utpadyate pitari pratisapatnapurūśa iva striyāḥ straiṇaḥ pratigha utpadyate mātari pratisapatnayāmiva| sa tābhyāmev viparyastābhyāṃ cittābhyāṃ viparyasto rantukāmatayā maithunakarmakāmatayā taṃ deśamidnriydvayasthānamāśrliṣya tāmavasthāṃ vipratipattyavasthāmātmanyadhimucyate mayaitat karma kriyata iti| tasmiṃścāśucau śukrośoṇite garbhasthānasaṃprāpte āmāśayapakkāśayāntara sthānasaṃprāpte abhiniviśate pratitiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ| pṛṣṭhābhimukha utkuṭuka iti| maithukriyātmābhinānakriyāyogāt| āpyāyamāno vardhamānaḥ| etānyeveti śukraśoṇitamahābhūtāni| bhūtāntarāṇyeveti śukraśoṇitavyatiriktāni| bījāṅkunirodhotpādanyāyenaikasminneva kṣaṇe bījaṃ nirūdhyate'ṅkaraścotpadyate tulādaṇḍanāmonnāmavannāśotpādayoḥ samakālatvāt| yattat kalalamityākhyāyata iti| kalalaṃ prathamaṃ bhavati kalalājjāyate'rvudamityādivacanāt| evaṃ ca kṛtvā sūtrapadamiti| valmīka iti bhikṣo asya kāyasyaitadadhivacanam| rūpiṇa audārikasya cāturmahābhūtasya adanakulmāṣopacitasya mātāputraśucikalalasambhūtasyeti vistaraḥ| mātāpitraśucyeva kalalaṃ tataḥ sambhhutasyetyarthaḥ| kaṭasiriti śmaśānam| rūdhirabindurūpātta itīdamudāharaṇam| asya kāyendriyabhāvāpattitaḥ| bhūtāntarāṇyeva śukraśoṇitavyatiriktāni tad yathā parṇakrimeriti| yathā parṇakrimeḥ parṇamahābhūtānyupaniśritya bhjtāntarāṇi karmabhirjāyante yānīndriyasvabhāvamāpadyante na parṇamahābhūtāni bahutvāttatkrimīṇām khaṇḍaśaḥ patrasyādarśanācca| sūtrapadaṃ kathaṃ tarhi nīyate mātāpitraśucikalalasambhūtasyeti ata āha| aśuci saṃniśrayotpattyabhisandhivacanāttu kalalasya sūtrābirodha iti| aśucisanniśrayeṇotpattirbhūtāntarāṇām tatrābhi sandhirabhiprāyaḥ tena vacanaṃ kalalasya tasmānmātāpitraśucikalalasambhūtasyetyasya sūtrasyāvirodhaḥ| etadukta bhavati śukraśoṇitasanniśrayeṇa kalalākhyāni bhūtāntarāṇi śukraśoṇitasamakālānyutpadyante sendriyāṇi| yathā parṇasanniśrayeṇa parṇasamānakālāni sendriyāṇi krimimahābhūtānyutpadyanta iti|  
anyatra tu yathāyogaṃ vaktavyamityāhuriti| anyatra saṃsvedajaupapādukayoryenyoryathāyogaṃ vyākhyeyamityāhurabhidharmācāryāḥ| tatra cāyaṃ yogi dṛśyeta ityācāryo vravīti| śaityañca narakeṣviti śītanarakopapattikāle| yadavastha iti aurabhikādyavasthaḥ| yathābhūtena tadvedyaṃ kṛtaṃ prākkarma tādṛśān sattvān svapna iva prekṣya narake sa dhāvati| pūrvācāryāḥ yogācārā aryāsaṅgaprabhṛtayaḥ|  
āsanādivottiṣṭhanniti| ūrdhvopapattisthānagamanāt| manuṣyādivaditi| yathā manuṣyastiryak pretaśca gacchati tadvat| ṛṣīṇāmativaktāra iti| adhikṣeptāro'pavaditāra ityarthaḥ|| 15||  
[  smaprajānan viśatyekastiṣṭhatyapyaparo'paraḥ|  
niṣkrāmatyapi sarvāṇi mūdo'nyo nityamaṇḍajaḥ|| 16|| ]

[ garbhāvakrāntayastisraścakravartisvayaṃbhuvau|  
karmajñānobhayeṣāṃ vā viśadatvādyathākramam|| 17   ]

nityamaṇḍajaḥ

iti nityagrahaṇāt

sarvāṇi mūdaḥ

ityatra nāsti niyamaḥ| bhavati tu saṃprajānanapi pratilomaṃ nirdiṣṭā iti| sūtre hi yaḥs arvāṇyasamprajānan karoti eṣā prathamā garbhāvakrātirūktā tisrastu yathānirdiṣṭānupūrvikā eveti| yo'pi janiṣyate so'pyaṇḍaja iti| aṇḍājjaniṣḥyate aṇḍaja ityabhiprāyaḥ| āvaśyako hyabhūtārtho bhūtavaducyate tathāvartamānasamīpye vartamānavadvā āśaṃsāyāṃ bhūtavacceti| kaścit kasmaicidvaktā bhavet āgacchantameva tathā āgatameva māṃ viddhīti| tasmādadoṣa eṣaḥ| aṇḍājjāto janiṣyate jāyate cetyaṇḍajaḥ| anyebhyo'pi dṛśyata iti vacanāt| tasmādaduṣṭametadityācāryasaṅgabhadraḥ| athavā bhāvinyāpisaṃjñayeti| bhaviṣyantyāpi saṃjñayā nirdeśaḥ kriyate| tadyathā saṃsṛtamabhisaṃskarotīti| janitāvasthāyāṃ saṃskṛtaṃ bhavati na janyamānāvasthāyām| na hi saṃskṛtasya punaḥ saṃskāro yujyate| bhaviṣyantyā tu saṃjñayā tathā nirdeśaḥ kriyate| evamodanaṃ pacatītyādi yojyam kathaṃ punarasaṃprajānan mātuḥ kukṣiṃ praviśatīti vistaraḥ| sannikṛṣṭāvasthā upapattideśasya|  
viparyastamatiryāti

iti viprakṛṣṭāvasthā abhimokṣo'dhimuktiḥ| tathā tiṣṭhāmi niryāmīti| kathaṃ tathā tiṣṭhato'pyeṣu tiṣṭhāmiti niṣkrāmato'pyebhyo niryāmīti| ayamanayorviparyāsayorviśeṣa iti| apare punarāhuḥ na kevalaṃ rāgadveṣābhyāmeva viparyastamatirūtpattideśaṃ gacchati kiṃ tarhi anyathāpi| kathamiti vāto vāti devo vapatītyādi| tathā ārāmaṃ pratiśāmyudyānaṃ cetyādi| samprajāṃnastu samyak cittena prajānāti mātuj kukṣḥi praviśāmītyevamādi| yadi samyak prajānāti kathaṃ kliṣṭenaiva pratisandhivandho vyavasthāpyate| mātṛsnehāriyogāt kliṣṭa cittaṃ bhavati| apara āha mātuḥ kukṣiṃ pratiśata evaṃ viparītau saṃjñādhimokṣāviti| upapannamātrāvasthāyāgetad bhavati vāto vāti yāvadṛkṣamūlaṃ copasarpāmi kuḍyamūlaṃ ceti| tiṣṭhato'pyeṣu tiṣṭhāmīti| niṣkrāmatop'pyebhyo niryāmītyevamādi| tatra grathameti vistaraḥ| prathamā cakravartinaḥ| yeyaṃ

samprājanan viśatyekaḥ

iti dvitīyā pratyekabuddhasya| yeyaṃ

tiṣṭhatyaparaḥ

iti| tṛtīyā buddhasya| yeyam

aparo niṣkrāmatyapi

iti atrāpi bhāvinyeti te na cakravartyādayastadānīmiti kṛtvā| ta evaṃ tvete cakravartyādaya iti| paryāyamātrametaduktam| arthastu sa evetyabhiprāyaḥ|| 16-17||  
[ nātmāsti skandhamātrā tu karmakleśābhisaṃskṛtam|  
antarābhavasantatyā kukṣimeti pradīpavat|| 18|| ]

[ yathākṣepaṃ kramād vṛddhaḥ santānaḥ kleśakarmabhiḥ|  
paralokaṃ punaryātītyanādibhavacakrakam|| 19|| ]

saṅketo hetuphalasambandhavyavasthā

kleśakarmābhisaṃskṛtam

iti kleśakarmaparibhāvitamityarthaḥ|  
pradīpavat

iti| santatinyāyasañcāri skandhapañcakaṃ kṣaṇikatvāt pradīpavaditi darśayati|  
yathākṣepam

iti ākṣepastāvat kālapravandhaḥ| etacca viśeṣaṇamāyuṣyasyāyuḥsaṃvataṇīyasya karmaṇo bhedāt| vyañjanānyanupūrvaśa iti| vyañjanāni cakṣurādyadhiṣṭhānāni tairhi cakṣurādīnyabhivyajyante| tasmin varcaḥkūpa iveti| tasmin kāyanāḍīvraṇe nindāvacanametat| nāḍī vraṇa iva nāḍīvraṇa iti| ugro durgandhaḥ ugradurgandhaḥ| andhakāraścāsāvālokarahitatvāt| samalānāṃ ca palvalo'sau palvala iveti kṛtvā tasmin satatapratikriye nityakartavyaśaice| śukreṇa pausnena| śoṇitena straiṇena lasīkayā dravaviśeṣalakṣaṇayā malena ca saṃklinne samantāt klinne viklinne viśeṣeṇa klinne kathite pūtau picchile ca tasmin hastau saṃpraveśyāṃṅgamaṅga hastapādādikaṃ nikṛtya cchitvā adhyāharanti adhyākarṣayanti| tarūṇaṃ vraṇaṃ sarūjaṃ tarūṇavraṇamivātmānamācaraṃstarūṇavraṇāyamānam tarūṇavraṇāyamāna ātmā'syeti tarūṇavraṇāyamānātmānaṃ bālakam śastraṃ ca kṣārañca śastrakṣāram tadivātmānamācaran śastrakṣārāyamāraṇaḥ śastrakṣārāyamāṇaḥ saṃsparśo'yoriti śastrakṣārāyamānasaṃspaśau pāṇī tābhyāṃ parigṛhya snāpayanti tasya vṛddheranvayādanugamāt

tasyāhetukatvamiti āderahetukatvam| sati cāsyāhetukatve| tadvadeva sarvamahetukaṃ prāduḥsyāt| dṛṣṭa cāṅkurādipu bījādīnāṃ āmarthyam| aṅkuranālakāṇḍapatrādiṣḥu vījāṅkaranālakāṇḍādīnāṃ sāmarthyamutpādanāy| kasmāt deśakālapratiniyamāt deśakālayostu pratiniyamāt| tatra deśapratiniyamo bījādisambaddha eva deśa utpatteḥ kālapratiniyamo bījānantaramutpatteḥ| agnyādīnāṃ vāgniśītoṣṇābhighātacakṣurādīnāṃ pākājādiṣu pākajasukhaduḥkhaśabdacakṣurvijñānādiṣu dṛṣṭaṃ sāmarthyaṃ deśakālapratiniyamāt| yadi hi nirhetukaḥ prādurbhāvaḥ syāt| vījādīnāmaṅkarādiṣu agnyādīnāñca pākajādiṣu deśakālapratiniyamenotpattiprati sāmarthya na syāt sarva sarvatra sarvadotpadyeta| na caivaṃ dṛṣṭamityato nāsti nirhetukaḥ prādurbhāvaḥ| nityakaraṇāstitvādaḥ prāgeva pasryudastaḥ

neśvarādeḥ kramādibhiḥ

itivacanāt|| 18-19||  
[ sa pratītyasamutpādo dvādaśāṅgastrikāṇḍakaḥ|  
pūrvāparāntayordve dve madhye'ṣṭau paripūriṇaḥ|| 20||  
śeṣānyaṣṭau madhya iti vijñānanāmarūpaṣaḍāyatanasparśavedanātṛṣṇopādānabhavāṅgāni| nāpi rūpārūpyāvacara iti rūpāvacarasya vijñānāṅgakālaṃ eva nāmarūpapaḍāyatanāṅgayoḥ sadbhāvata| na madhye'ṣṭāvaṅgāni ārūpyāvacarasya ca nāmarūpaṣaḍāyatanāṅgayorabhāvāt| mahānidānaparyāyasūtre vijñānaṃ cedānanda mātuḥ kukṣi navākrāmedapi tu tannāmarūpaṃ kalalatvāyābhisaṃmūrcchet no bhadantetyādi tat sūtraṃ paṭhyate| mātuḥ kukṣyavakramaṇaṃ hi kāmadhātāveveti| saphalahetukayoḥ

pūrvāparāntayo ḥ

grahaṇāditi yathākrāmetat| pūrvāntasya saphalasya grahaṇādaparāntasya ca sahetukasya grahaṇāditi| tatra pūrvānte heturavidyā saṃskārāśca tasya phalaṃ pañcāṅgāni| vijñānaṃ yāvadvedaneti| aparānte jātirjarāmaraṇaṃ ceti phalam| tasya trīṇyaṅgāni hetavastṛṣṇopādānabhavāḥ| tadevaṃ saptāṅgāni paurvāntikaḥ pañcāṅgānyaparāntika iti vyākhyātaṃ bhavati|| 20||  
pūrvakleśadaśāvidyā saṃskārāḥ pūrvakarmaṇaḥ|  
sandhiskandhāstu vijñānaṃ nāmarūpamataḥ param|| 21|| ]

atha ka ime'vidyādaya iti| kimavidyaiva kevalā utāho sarve kleśāḥ āhosvit kleśavastheti sandehe pṛcchati| sāhacaryāditi yasmādavidyāsahacāriṇaḥ kleśāḥ| tadvaśena teṣāṃ samudācarāñca| yasmāccāvidyāvaśena teṣāṃ kleśānāṃ samudācāraḥ sa punastadanuvṝttiḥ| mūdasya hi kleśasamudacāro nāmūdasya| rājagamanavacana iti vistaraḥ| yathā rājā gacchatītyukte tadanuyātṛkāṇāṃ bhṝtyānāmāgamanasiddhiḥ sāhacaryāt tadvaśena teṣāṃ samudācārāt tadvadetat| tadevamavidyāvaśādavidyāṅgaḥ kleśadaśetyarthaḥ| tasmādukta

pūrvakleśadaśāvidyā

iti na tūkta pūrvāvidyādaśavidyeti pūṇyādikarmāvastheti puṇyāpuṇyakarmāvasthetyarthaḥ||21||  
[ prāk ṣaḍāyatanotpādāttat pūrva trikrasaṅgamāt|  
sparśaḥ prāk sukhaduḥkhādikāraṇajñānaśaktitaḥ|| 22|| ]

prāk caturāyatanotpādāditi vaktavya iti| manaḥkāyāyatanayorūpapattibhava evotpādāt| tadā tadyavasthāpanāditi cakṣḥurādyāyatanotpattikāle kāyamana āyatanayorvyavasthāpanādityarthaḥ|  
tatpūva trikasaṅgamāt

iti| tat ṣaḍāyatanāṅga prāk trikasaṅgamāt prāgindriyaviṣayavijñānatrikasannipātādityarthaḥ|  
sparśaḥ prāk

iti| ṣaḍīndriyaviṣayavijñānasannipātaḥ sparśo jātāvasthāyāṃ vyavasthāpayate paripūrṇaṣaḍāyatanasannipātasadbhāvāt| ā kutaḥ| prāk sukhaduḥkhāduḥkhāsukhavedanākāraṇeṣu

jñānaśaktitaḥ|  
sa bālo yāvat sukhāyā vedanāyā etat kāraṇaṃ duḥkhāyā etat kāraṇam aduḥkhasvāsukhāyaścaitat kāraṇamiti paricchede na śakto bhavati sāvasthā sparśe ityucyate| tathā hi bālako'gnimapi sṟośedaduḥkhasyaitat kāraṇamityaparichindan|| 22||  
[ vittiḥ prāṅmaithunāttṛṣṇa bhogamaithunarāgiṇaḥ|  
upadānaṃ tu bhogānāṃ prāptaye paridhāvataḥ|| 23|| ]

paricchede sāmarthye sati

vittiḥ

vedanetyarthaḥ| ā kutaḥ

prāṅmethunāt

yāvanmaithunarāgo na samudācarati| sāvasthā vedenetyucyate| vedanāprakarṣiṇī hi sāvasthā vedanā kāraṇavedanāt|  
tṛṣṇā bhogamathunarāgiṇaḥ

iti| bhogeṣu rūpādikāmaguṇalakṣaṇeṣu maithune ca lokapratīte rāgiṇaḥ pudgalasya tadrāgasamudācārāvasthā tṛṣṇetyucyate| paryeṣṭimāpanna iti| eṣa ityetasya ghātoretadrūpama| upādīyate viṣaya ātmabhāvo'nenetyupādānam caturvidhaḥ kleśa sāvasthopādānamityucyate|| 23||  
[ sa bhaviṣyadbhavaphalaṃ kurūte karma tadbhavah|  
pratisandhiḥ punarjātirjarāmaraṇamā vidaḥ|| 24||  
sa bhaviṣyadabhavaphalam

iti| sa paridhāvato nirdeśaḥ| bhaviṣyadbhavaḥ phalamasyeti bhaviṣyadbhavaphalaṃ

karma

pānarbhavikamityarthaḥ|  
tadbhavaḥ

iti| bhavatyeneti bhavaḥ karmaparyāyaḥ|  
jaramaraṇamā vidaḥ

iti| ā vida ā vedanaṅgāt| jarāmaraṇaṃ nāmarūpādicaturaṅgasvabhāvam| vineyajanodvejanārtha bhagavatā jarāmaraṇaśabdena catvāryaṅgānyuktāni yo bhikṣavo rūparayotpādaḥ sa jarāmaraṇasyotpādaḥ| evaṃ yāvad vijñānasyeti|| 24||  
kṣaṇikaḥ kṣaṇe bhavaḥ| kṣaṇo'syāstīti vā kṣaṇikaḥ| prakarṣeṇa dīvyati carati vā prākarṣikaḥ| prabandhayukta ityarthaḥ| evaṃ sāmbandhikaḥ hetuphalasambandhayukta ityarthaḥ| evam āvasthikaḥ dvādaśapañcaskandhikā avasthā ityarthaḥ| vijñānasahabhuva iti vistaraḥ| rūpamatra sahabhūrvijñaptayavijñaptayādi| saṃjñādiskandhatrayaṃ nāma| nāmarūpavyvasthitānīndriyāṇi tatpratibaddhavṛttivyādindriyāṇām| āśrayatvena vā tāni nāmni vyavasthitānīti| ṣaḍāyatanābhinipātaḥ sparśa iti| cakṣurādīnāṃ svasmin viṣaye pravṛttirabhinipātaḥ| sa tu sambhavataḥ| yasya tadānīmabhinipātaḥ sambhavati sa tasya sparśa iti veditavyaḥ| yasya tadānīmabhinipātaḥ sambhavati sa tasya sparśa iti veditavyaḥ| tatsamprayuktāni paryavasthānānyupādānam| tayā tṛṣṇayā samprayuktānyahrīkyānapatrāpyādīni paryavasthānanai| tadupādānaṃ tatsamutthamiti tṛṣṇāsamutthaṃ cetanāsamutthamityapare| unmajjanamutpādaḥ paripāko jareti| phalākṣepasāmarthyopaghātaḥ| pūrvakṣaṇāpekṣayā vā| bhaṅgo maraṇamiti| tatkṣaṇavināśaḥ| bhaṅgābhimukhyaṃ bhaṅga ityapare| kṣaṇikaḥ sambandhikaśca yathāprakaraṇeṣviti| katham prakaraṇeṣu pratītyasamutpādaḥ katabhaḥ| sarve saṃskṛtā dharmā iti| tatra saṃskṝtānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ pratikṣaṇaṃ vināśayogāt kṣaṇikaḥ pratītyasamut pādaḥ dvādaśapañcaskandhikā avasthā atītānāgatapratyutpannajanmasambaddhāḥ āvasthikaḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ nirantaragrahaṇaṃ trikāṇḍaprasiddhayartham sa eva prākrṣika iti| sa evāvasthikaḥ prakarṣayogāt prākarṣikaḥ anekakṣaṇikatvādanekajanmikatvādvā| cakravartisaṃvartanīyāni karmāṇi vipākābhinivṝttau kadācideva vṛtti labhante| bahupratyayāpekṣitvāt| sambhavaiṣitvādupapattyabhilāṣitvāt| karmāṇyeva pratyayānāṃ sāmagrīmāvahantīti| karmādhipatyenāśvādīnāṃ prasiddhaṃ kālamatikramya kālāntare'pi maithunaṃ bhavatīti| ekakarmākṣepāditi| yenaiva karmaṇā gavādinikāyasabhāga ākṣipyate tenaiva karmaṇā tadantarābhava ākṣipyata ityato vaktavya metat| yaduktaṃ yenānyatra kāle goṣūpapattavyaṃ sa gavayeṣūpapadyata iti vistareṇa| na nikāyabhedādekākṣepakatvaṃ hīyate tatkarmaṇā ekajātīyatvāt iti vistareṇa| na nikāyabhedādekākṣepakatvaṃ hīyate tatkarmaṇā ekajātīyatvāt gatyākṛtisaṃsthānāntarāparityāgācca| gatiniyatānāṃ hi karmaṇāmupapattivaicitryaṃ dṛṣṭam| kalmāṣapādadivaditi| nāstyeṣa doṣa ityācāryasaṅghabhadraḥ|| 14| |  
[ viparyastamatiryāti gatideśaṃ risaṃsayā|  
gandhasthānābhikāmo'nya ūrdhvapādastu nārakaḥ|| 15|| ]

viparyastamatiḥ

iti anunayasahagatena vā pratighasahagatena vā cittena yathā viparyastamatirbhavati tad darśayannāha| tatrāsya pitroriti vistaraḥ| mātāpitrostāṃ vipratipattiṃ dvīndriyasamāpattilakṣaṇām| puṃsaḥ sataḥ purūṣasyāntarābhavasya puṃso'yaṃ pausno rāga utpadyate mātari bhāryāyāmiva| striyā ayaṃ straiṇo rāga utpadyate pitari bhartarīva| viparyayāt pratigha iti puṃsaḥ sataḥ pausnaḥ pratigha īrṣyotthānabhūta utpadyate pitari pratisapatnapurūṣa iva striyāḥ satyāḥ straiṇaḥ pratigha utpadyate mātari pratisapatnayāmiva| sa tābhyāmeva viparyastābhyāṃ cittābhyāṃ viparyasto rantukāmatayā maithunakarmakāmatayā ta deśamindriyadvayasthānamāśrliṣya tāmavasthāyāṃ vipratipattyavasthāmātmanyadhimucayte mayaitat karma kiyata iti| tasmiṃścāśucau śukaśoṇite garbhasthānasamprāpte āmaśayapakkāśayāntara sthānasamprāpte abhiniviśate pratitiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ| pṛṣṭhābhimukha utkuṭuka iti| maitunākriyātmābhimānakriyāyogāt| āpyāyamāno vardhamānaḥ| etānyeveti śukraśoṇitamahābhūtāni bhūtāntarāṇyeveti śukraśoṇitavyatiriktāni bījāṅkuranirodhotpādanyāyenaikasminneva kṣaṇe bījaṃ nirūdhyate'ṅkuraścotpadyate| tulā daṇḍanāmonnāmavannāśotpādayoḥ samakālatvāt| yattat kalalamityākhyāyata iti| kalalaṃ prathamaṃ bhavati kalalājjāyate'rvudamityādivacanāt| evaṃ ca kṛtvā sutrapadamiti| valmīka iti bhikṣo asya kāyasyaitadadhivacanam| rūpiṇa audārikasya cāturmahābhūtasya audanakulmāṣopacitasya mātāpitraśucikalalasambhūtasyeti vistaraḥ| mātāputraśucyeva kalalaṃ tataḥ sambhūtasyetyarthaḥ| kaṭasiriti śmaśānam| rūdhirabindurūpātta itīdamudāharanam| asya kāyendriyabhāvāpattitaḥ| bhūtāntarāṇyeva śukraśoṇitavyatiriktāni tad yathā parṇakriyma riti| yathā parṇakrimeḥ parṇamahābhūtānyupaniśritya bhūtāntarāṇi karmabhirjāyante yānīndriyasvabhāvamāpadyante na parṇamahābhūtāni bahutvāttatkrimīṇām khaṇḍaśaḥ patrasyādarśanācca| sūtrapadaṃ kathaṃ tarhi nīyate mātāpatrāśucikalalasambhūtasyeti ata āha| aśucisaṃniśrayotpattyabhisandhivacanāttakalalasya sūtrāvirodha iti| aśucisaṃniśrayeṇotpattirbhūtāntarāṇām tatrābhisandhirabhiprāyaḥ tena vacanaṃ kalalasya tasmānmātāpitraśucikalalasambhūtasyetyasya sūtrasyāvirodhaḥ| etadukta bhavati śukraśoṇitasaṃniśrayena kalalākhyāni bhūtāntarāṇi śukraśoṇitasamakālānyutpadyante sendriyāṇi| yathā parṇasaṃniśrayeṇa parṇasamānakālāni sendriyāṇi krimimahābhūtānyutpadyanta iti|  
anyatra tu yathāyogaṃ vaktavyamityāhuriti| anyatra saṃsvedajaupapādukayoryonyoryathāyogaṃ vyākhyeyamityāhurabhidharmācāryāḥ| tatra cāyaṃ yogo dṛśyeta ityācāryo vravīti| śatyañca narakeṣviti śītanarakopapattikāle| yadavastha iti aurabhrikādyavasthaḥ| yathābhūtena tadvedyaṃ kṛtaṃ prākakarma tādṛśān sattvān svapna iva prekṣya narake sa dhāvati| pūrvācāryāḥ yogācārā āryāsaṅgaprabhṛtayaḥ|  
āsanādivottiṣṭhanniti| ūrdhvopapattisthānagamanāt| manuṣyādivaditi| yathā manuṣyastiryak pretaśca gacchati| tadvat | ṛṣīnāmativaktāra iti adhikṣeptāro'pavaditāra ityarthaḥ|| 15||  
[ samprajānan viśatyekastiṣṭhatyapyaparo'paraḥ|  
niṣkrāmatyapi sarvāṇi mūdo'nyo nityamaṇdajaḥ|| 16||  
garbhāvakrāntayastisraścakravartisvayaṃbhuvau|  
karmajñānobhayeṣāṃ vā viśadatvādyathākramam|| 17|| ]

nityamaṇḍajaḥ

iti nityagrahaṇāt

sarvāṇi mūdaḥ

ityatra nāsti niyamaḥ| bhavati tu samprajānannapi pratilomaṃ nirdiṣṭā iti| sūtre hi yaḥ sarvāṇyasamprajānan karoti eṣā prathamā garbhāvakrāntirūktā tisrastu yathānirdiṣṭānupūrvikā eveti| yo'pi janiṣyate so'pytaṇḍaja iti| aṇḍājjaniṣyate aṇḍaja ityabhiprāyaḥ| āvaśyako hyabhūtārthe bhūtavaducyate tathā vartamānasāmīpye vartamānavadvā āśaṃsāyāṃ bhūtavacceti| kaścit kasmaicidvaktā bhavet āgacchantameva tathā āgatameva māṃ viddhīti| tasmādadoṣa eṣaḥ| aṇḍājjāto janiṣyate jāyate cetyaṇḍajaḥ| anyebhyo'pi dṛśyata iti vacanāt| tasmādaduṣṭametadityācāryasaṅghabhadraḥ| athavā bhāvinyāpi saṃjñayeti bhaviṣyantyāpi saṃjñayā nirdeśaḥ kriyate| tadyathā saṃskṛtamabhisaṃskarotīti| janitāvasthāyāṃ saṃskṛtaṃ bhavati na janyamānāvasthāyām| na hi saṃskṛtasya punaḥ saṃskāro yujyate| bhaviṣyantyā tu saṃjñayā tathā nirdeśaḥ kriyate| evamodanaṃ pacatītyādi yojyam kathaṃ kathaṃ punarasamprajānat mātu kukṣiṃ praviśatīti vistaraḥ| sannikṛṣṭāvasthā upapattideśasya|  
viparyastamatiryāti

iti viprakṛṣṭāvasthā abhimokṣo'dhimuktiḥ| tathā tiṣṭhāmi niryāmīti| kathaṃ tathā tiṣṭhato'pyeṣu tiṣṭhāmīti niṣkrāmato'pyebhyo niryāmīti| ayamanayorviparyāsayorviśeṣa iti| apare punarāhuḥ na kevalaṃ rāgadveṣābhyāmeva viparyastamatirūtpattideśaṃ gacchati kiṃ tarhi anyathāpa| kathamiti vāto vāti devo varṣatītyādi| tathā ārāmaṃ praviśāmyudyānaṃ cetyādi| samprajānaṃstu samyak cittena prajānāti mātuḥ kukṣi praviśamītyevamādi| yadi samyak prajānāti kathaṃ kliṣṭenaiva pratisandhibando vyavasthāpyate| mātṛsnehādiyogāt kliṣṭa cittaṃ bhavati| apara āha mātuḥ kukṣiṃ praviśata evaṃ viparītau saṃjñādhimokṣāviti| upapannamātrāvasthāyāmetad bhavati vāto vāti yāvaddhṛkṣamūlaṃ copasarpāmi kuḍyamūlaṃ ceti| tiṣṭhato'pyeṣu tiṣṭhāmīti| niṣkrāmato'pyebhyo niryāmītyevamādi| tatra prathameti vistaraḥ| prathamā cakravartinaḥ| yeyaṃ

samprajānan viśatyekaḥ

iti dvitīyā pratyekabuddhasya| yeyaṃ

tiṣṭhatyaparaḥ

iti| tṛtīyā buddhasya| yeyam

aparaḥ|  
niṣkrāmatyapi

iti atrāpi bhāvinyeti te na cakravartyādayastadānīmiti kṛtvā| ta evaṃ tvete cakravartyādaya iti| paryāyamātrametaduktam| arthastu sa evetyabhiprāyaḥ|| 16-17||  
[ nātmāsti skandhamātraṃ tu karmakleśābhisaṃskṛtam|  
antarābhavasantatyā kukṣimeti pradīpavat|| 18|| ]

[yathākṣepaṃ kramāda vṛddhaḥ santāḥ kleśakarmabhiḥ|  
paralokaṃ punaryātītyanādibhavacakrakam|| 19|| ]

saṅketo hetuphalasambandavyāsthā|  
kleśakarmābhisaṃskṛtam

iti kleśakarmaparibhāvitamityarthaḥ|  
pradīpavat

iti| santatinyāyasañcāri skandhapañcakam kṣaṇikatvāt pradīpavaditi darśayati|  
yathākṣepam

iti ākṣepastāvat kālaprabandhaḥ| etacca viśeṣamāyuṣsyāyuḥsaṃvartaṇiyasya karmaṇo bhedāt| vyañjanānyanupūrvaśa iti| vyañjanāni cakṣurādyadhiṣṭhānāni tairhi cakṣurādīnyabhivyajyante| yasmin varcaḥkūpa iveti| tasmin kāyanāḍivraṇe nidnāvacanametat| nāḍī vraṇa iva nāḍīvraṇa iti| ugro durgandhaḥ ugradurgandhaḥ| andhakāraścāsāvālokarahitatvāt| samalānāṃ ca palvalo'sau palvala iveti kṛtvā tasmin satatpratikriye nityakartavyaśaice| śukreṇa pausnena| śoṇitena strairageṇa lasīkayā dravaviśeṣalakṣṇayā malena ca saṃkinne samantāt klinneviklinne viśeṣeṇa klinne kathite pūtau picchile ca tasmin hastau saṃpraveśyāṅgamaṅga hastapādādikaṃ nikṛtya chittvā adhyāharanti adhyākarṣayanti| tarūṇaṃ vraṇaṃ  sarūjam tarūnavraṇamivātmānamācaraṃstarūṇavraṇāyamānaḥ tarūṇavraṇāyamāna ātmā'syeti tarūṇavraṇāyamānātmānaṃ bālakam śastra ca kṣārañha śāstrakṣāram tadivātmānamācaran śāstrakṣārāyamāṇaḥ śastrakṣārāyamāṇaḥ saṃsparśo'nayoriti śastrakṣārayamāṇasaṃsparśo pāṇī tābhyāṃ parigṛhya snāpayanti tasya vṛddheranvayādanugamāt|  
tasyāhetukatvamiti āderahetukatvam| sati cāsyāhetukatve| tadvadeva sarvamahetukaṃ prāduḥsyāt| dṛṣṭa cāṅkurādiṣu bījādīnāṃ sāmarthyam| aṅkuranālakāṇḍapatrādiṣu bījāṅkuranālakāṇḍādīnāṃ sāmarthyamutpādanāya| kasmāt deśakālapratiniyamāt deśakālayostu pratiniyamāt| tatra deśapratiniyamo bījādisambaddha eva deśa utpatteḥ kālapratiniyamo bījānantaramutpatteḥ| āgyādīnāṃ vāgniśītoṣnābhighātacakṣurādīnāṃ pākājādiṣu pākajasukhaduḥkhaśabdacakṣurvijñānādiṣu dṛṣṭa sāmarthya deśakālapratiniyamāt| yadi hi nirhetukaḥ prādurbhāvaḥ syāt| bījādīnāmaṅkarādiṣu agnyādīnāñca pākajādiṣu deśakālapratiniyamenotpatti prati sāmarthya na syāt sarva srvatra sarvadotpadyeta| na caivaṃ dṛṣṭamityato nāsti nirhatukaḥ prādurbhāvaḥ| nityakāraṇāstitvavādaḥ prāgeva paryudastaḥ

neśvarādeḥ kramādibhiḥ

iti vacanāt|| 18- 19||  
[ sa pratītyasamutpādo dvādaśāṅgastrikāṇḍakaḥ|  
pūrvāparāntayordve dve madhye'ṣṭau paripūriṇaḥ|| 20|| ]

śeṣāṇyaṣṭhau madhya iti vijñānanamārūpaṣaḍāyatanasparśavedanātṛṣṇopādānabhavāṅgāni| nāpi rūpārūpyāvacara iti rūpāvacarasya vijñānāṅgakāla eva nāmarūpaṣaḍāyatanāṅgayoḥ sadbhāvāt| na madhye'ṣṭāvaṅgāni ārūpyāvacarasya ca nāmarūpaṣaḍāyatanāṅgayorabhāvāt| mahānidānaparyāyasūtra vijñānaṃ cedānanda mātuḥ kukṣi nāvakrāmedapi tu tannāmārūpakalalatvāyābhisaṃmūrcchat no bhadantetyādi tat sūtra paṭhyate mātuḥ kukṣyavakramaṇaṃ hi kāmadhātāveveti| saphalahetukayoḥ

pūrvaparāntayoḥ

grahaṇāditi yathākramametat| pūrvāntasya saphalasya grahaṇādaparāntasya ca sahetukasya grahaṇāditi| tatra pūrvānte heturavidyā saṃskārāśca tasya phalaṃ pañcāṅgāni| vijñānaṃ yāvadvedaneti| aparānte jātirjaramaraṇaṃ ceti phalam| tasya trīṇyaṅgāni hetavastṛṣṇopādānabhavāḥ| tadevaṃ saptāṅgāni paurvāntikaḥ pañcāṅgānyaparāntika iti vyākhyātaṃ bhavati|| 20||  
[ pūrvakleśadaśavidyā saṃskārāḥ pūrvakarmaṇaḥ|  
sandhiskandhāstu vijñānaṃ nāmarūpamataḥ param|| 21|| ]

atha ka ime'vidyādaya iti| kimavidyaiva kevalā utāho sarve kleśāḥ āhosvit kleśāvastheti sandehe pṛccheti| sāhacaryāditi yasmādavidyāsahacāriṇaḥ kleśāḥ| tadvaśena teṣāṃ samudācārācca| yasmāccāvidyāvaśena teṣāṃ kleśānāṃ samudācāraḥ sa punastadanuvṛttiḥ| mūdasya hi kleśasamudācāro nāmūdasya| rājagamanavacana iti vistaraḥ| yathā rājāgacchatītyukte tadanuyātṛkāṇāṃ bhṛtyānāmagamanasiddhiḥ sāhacaryāt tadvaśena teṣāṃ samudācārāt tadvadetat tadevamavidyāvaśādavidyāṅgaḥ kleśadaśetyarthaḥ| tasmādukta

pūrvakleśadaśāvidyā

iti na tūkta pūrvāvidyādaśāvidyeti puṇyādikarmāvastheti puṇyāpuṇyakarmāvasthetyarthaḥ|| 21||  
[ prāk ṣaḍāyatanotpādāttat pūrvaṃ trikasaṅgamāt|  
sparśaḥ prāk sukhadūhkhādikāraṇajñānaśaktitaḥ|| 22|| ]

prāk caturāyatanotpādāditi vaktavya iti| manaḥ kāyāyatanayorūpapattibhava evotpādāt| tadā tadvayavasthāpanāditi cakṣurādyāyatanotpattikāle kāyamana āyatanayorvyavasthāpanādityarthaḥ|  
tatpūrva trikasaṅgamāta

iti| tat ṣaḍāyatanāṅga prāk trikasaṅgamāt prāgindriyaviṣayavijñānatrikasannipātādityarthaḥ|  
sparśāḥ prāk

iti| ṣaḍindriyaviṣayavijñānasannipātaḥ sparśo jātāvasthāyāṃ vyavasthāpyate paripūrṇaṣaḍāyatanasannipātasadbhāvāt | ā kutaḥ| prāk sukhaduḥkhāduḥkhāsukhavedanākāraṇeṣu

jñānaśaktitaḥ|  
sa bālo yāvat sukhāyā vedanāyā etat kāraṇaṃ duḥkhāyā etat kāraṇam aduḥkhāsukhāyāścaitat kāraṇamiti paricchede na śakto bhavati sāvasthā sparśa ityucyate| tathā hi bālako'gnimapi spṛśed duḥkhasyaitat kāraṇamityaparicchindan|| 22||  
[ vittiḥ prāṅmaithunāttṛṣṇa bhogamaithunarāgiṇaḥ|  
upādānaṃ tu bhogānāṃ prāptaye paridhāvataḥ|| 23|| ]

paricchede sāmarthye sati

vittiḥ

vedanetyarthaḥ| ā kutaḥ

prāṅmaithunāt

yāvanmaithunarāgo na samudācarati| sāgasthā vedenetyucyate| vedanāprakarṣiṇī hi sāvasthā vedanā kāraṇavedanāt|  
tṛṣṇā bhogamethunarāgiṇaḥ

iti| bhogeṣu rūpādikāmaguṇalakṣaṇeṣu maithune ca lokapratīte rāgiṇaḥ pudgalasya tadrāgasamudācārāvasthā tṛṣnatyucyate| payaṣṭimāpanna iti| eṣa ityetasya dhātoretadrūpam| upādīyate viṣaya ātmabhāvo'nenetyupādānam caturvidhaḥ kleśaḥ sāvasthopādānamityucyate|| 23||  
[ sa bhaviṣyadbhavaphalaṃ kurūte karma tadbhavaḥ|  
pratisandhiḥ punarjātirjarāmaraṇamā vidaḥ|| 24|| ]

sa bhaviṣyadbhavaphalam

iti| sa paridhāvato nirdeśaḥ| bhaviṣyadbhavaḥ phalamasyeti bhaviṣyadbhavaphalaṃ

karma

paunarbhavikamityarthaḥ|  
tadbhavaḥ

iti| bhavatyaneneti bhavaḥ karmaparyāyaḥ|  
jarāmaṇṇamā vidaḥ

iti ā vida ā vedanāṅgāt| jarāmaraṇaṃ nāmarūpādicaturaṅgasvabhāvam| vineyajanodvejanārtha bhagavā jarāmaraṇaśabdena catvāryaṅgānyuktāni| yo bhikṣav rūpasyotpādaḥ sa jarāmaraṇasyotpādaḥ| evaṃ yāvad vijñānasyeti|| 24||  
kṣaṇikaḥ kṣaṇe bhavaḥ kṣaṇoasyāstīti vā kṣaṇikaḥ| prakarṣeṇa dīvyati carati vā prākarṣḥikaḥ prabandhayukta ityarthaḥ| evaṃ sambandhikaḥ hetuphalasambandhayukta ityarthaḥ| evam āvasthikaḥ dvādaśapañcaskandhikā avasthā ityarthaḥ| vijñānasahabhuva iti vistaraḥ| rūpamatra sahabhūrvijñaptyavijñaptyādi| saṃjñādiskandhatraya nāma| nāmarūpavyavasthitānīndriyāṇi tatpratibaddhavṛttatvā dindriyāṇām| āśrayatvena vā tāni nāmni vyavasthitānīti| ṣaḍāyatanābhinipātaḥ sparśa iti| cakṣurādīnāṃ svasmin viṣaye pravṛttirabhinipātaḥ| sa tu sambhavataḥ| yasya tadānīmabhinipātaḥ sambhavati sa tasya sparśa iti veditavyaḥ| tatsamprayuktāni paryavasthānānyupādānam| tayā tṛṣṇayā samprayuktānyāhrīkyānapatrāpyādīni paryavasthānāni tadupādānam| tatasamutthamiti tṛṣnāsamuttham cetanāsamutthamityapare| unmajjanamutpādaḥ paripāko jareti| phalākṣepasāmarthyopaghātaḥ| pūrvakṣaṇāpekṣayā vā| bhaṅgo maraṇamiti| tatkṣaṇavināśaḥ| bhaṅgābhimukhyaṃ bhaṅga ityapare| kṣaṇikaḥ sambandhikaśca yathāprakaraṇeṣviti| kathaṃ prakaraṇeṣu pratītyasamutpādaḥ katamaḥ| sarva saṃskṛtā dharmā iti| tatra saṃskṛtānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ pratikṣaṇaṃ vināśayogāt kṣaṇikaḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ hetuphalabhūtobhayakṣaṇasambandhāt sāmbandhikaḥ| nirantarajanmaytrayasambaddhā iti dvādaśapañcaskandhikā avasthā atītānāgatapratyutpannajanmasamabaddhāḥ āvasthikaḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ nirantaragrahaṇaṃ trikāṇḍaprasiddhyartham sa eva prākrṣika iti| sa evāvasthikaḥ prakarṣayogāt prākarṣikaḥ anekakṣaṇikatvādanejanmika tvādvā|  
[ āvasthikaḥ kliṣṭo'yaṃ prādhānyātvaṅgakīrtanam|  
pūrvaparāntamadhyeṣu sammohavinivṛttaye|| 25|| ]

abhiprāyika iti abhiprāye bhava ābhiprāyikaḥ| avineyābhiprāyavaśattathā deśita ityarthaḥ| lākṣaṇiko'bhidharme lakṣaṇapradhānatvāt| prakaraṇeṣu hi sarvasaṃskṛtagrahaṇāt sattvāsattvākhyaḥ pratītyasamutpāda uktaḥ sarvasaṃskṛtahetutvayogāt| vineyasammohavinivṛttihetuḥ sattvākhya eva dvādaśāṅgaḥ pratītyasamutpādo deśitaḥ| ata āha

pūrvāparāntamadhyeṣu sammohavinivṛttaye

iti ata eva ca trikāṇḍa iti kaṇḍa iti bhāgaḥ ko nvahamabhūvamiti devo manuṣya iti vā kathaṃ nvahamabhūvamiti kena prakāreṇa kayā yuktetyarthaḥ| kiṃ nu bhaviṣyāmyanāgate'dhvanīti visatraḥ| kiṃ nu bhaviṣyāmyanāgate'dhvani āhosvinna bhaviṣyāmi ko nu bhaviṣyāmi kathaṃ nu bhaviṣyāmīti kena prakāreṇa kayā yuktayetyarthaḥ| kiṃ svididamiti ātmabhāvadravya manvepate kathaṃ svididamiti kena prakāreṇa kayā yuktayeti tadevātmabhāvadravyaṃ nāvadhārayati| ke santa iti ke vayamidānīṃ vidyamānāḥ ke bhaviṣyāma iti evaṃ nāvadhārayati| sūtre yathākramamiti| avidyā saṃskārāśca pūrvāntasammohavyāvartanārtham jātirjarāmaraṇaṃ cāparāntasammohavyāvartanārtham vijñānaṃ yāvadbhavaśca madhyasammohavyāvartanārthamti| tathāhi sūtra evoktamiti vistaraḥ| sūtre'pyevamevaṃ darśitamiti sūtreṇa svavyākhyānaṃ samarthayati| sa na pūrvāntaṃ pratisarati kiṃ nvahamabhūvamatīte'dhvanīti vistaraḥ| sa na pūrvāntaṃ pratidhāvati| kiṃ nvahamabhūvamatīte'dhvanīti pratītyasamutpādayathābhūtadarśanena naivamatīte'dhvani saṃmuhyata ityarthaḥ| vistaraśabdena sa nāparāntaṃ pratisarati kiṃ nu bhaviṣyāmyanāgate'dhvanīti vistaraḥ| tathā na sa madhyāntaṃ pratisarati kiṃ svididamiti vistaraḥ|  
tṛṣṇopādānabhavā apīti| sarve tṛṣṇepādanābhavā aparāntasammohavyāvartanārthaṃ na madhyasammohavyāvartanārtham| yatham pūrvamupadiṣṭaṃ vijñānaṃ yāvadbhavaśceti kā punaratra yuktirityāha tasyaiva hyeta hetava iti yasmādaparāntasyaivaite hetava iti|| 25||  
[ kleśasatrīni dvayaṃ karma sapta vastu phalaṃ tathā|  
phalahetvabhisaṃkṣepo dvayormadhyānumānataḥ|| 26|| ]

kleśakarmāśrayatvāditi vijñānādīni saptāṅgāni vastūnyadhiṣṭhānāni kleśakarmaṇamityarthaḥ|  
phalaṃ tathā iti saptavāṅgāni vijñānāmarūpaṣaḍāyatanasparśavedanāṅgānyavidyāsaṃskārāṇāṃ phalāni| jātirjarāmaraṇaṃ ca tṛṣṇopādānabhavānāṃ phalāni| pañcahetubhūtānīti| avidyāsaṃskāratṛṣṇopādanabhavāḥ| karmakleśasvabhāvatvāditi avidyātṛṣṇopādā nāni kleśasvabhāvāni saṃskārā bhavāśca karmasvabhāvāḥ|  
vastunaḥ pañcadhā bhedāditi| vijñānanāmarūpaṣaḍāyatanasparśavedanā iti kleśasya ca dvidheti| tṛṣṇopādanamiti| anāgate'dhvani phalaṃ saṃkṣipta dvidhā bhedāditi| jātirjarāmaṇamiti| atīte'dhvani hetuḥ saṃkṣipta ekamukhakleśopadeśāt avidyāmukhakleśopadeśādityarthaḥ| na punaranāgate'dhvani phalamevaṃ pañcadhā viśālitam atīte cādhvani naivaṃ dvidhā heturviśālita iti|| 26||  
[kleśāt kleśaḥ kriyā caiva tato vastu tataḥ punaḥ|  
vastu kleśāśca jāyante bhavāṅgānāmayaṃ nayaḥ|| 27|| ]

yusmādupadarśito'tra bhagavateti kaḥ

kleśāt kleśaḥ

ityevamādikaḥ|  
bhavāṅgānamayaṃ nayaḥ

tatā vastu

iti tataḥ kriyayāḥ

tataḥ punaḥ

vastu

iti tato vastunaḥ vastu|  
kleśāśca jāyante

iti vastuna ityadhikṛtaṃ jāyanta ityatretikaraṇo'dhyāhāryaḥ vastunaḥ kleśādveti jñāpitamiti| hati kleśo vastuna utpadyamāno dṛṣṭo vedanāyastṛṣṇeti | kleśādapi tathā dṛṣṭastṛṣṇāyā upādānamiti| avidyā ca pūrvānta sarvakleśasvabhāvākhyātā| tad yadyupādānaṃ tṛṣṇāyāstutudutpadyata iti gamyate| atha tṛṣṇā tadvastuna utapadyata iti gamyate tasmādavidyā kleśasvabhāvā satī vastunaḥ kleśadveti jñāpitaṃ bhavati| jarāmaraṇaṃ caturaṅgasvabhāvaṃ vastviti vyākhyātam vastunaśceha kleśa utpadyamāno dṛṣṭo vedanāpratyayā tṛṣṇeti| tasmāt tataḥ kleśo bhāvī bhaviṣyatīti jñāpitaṃ bhavati nātra punaḥ kiñcidupasaṃkhyeyamevaṃ jñāpitatvāt| kathaṃ punarevaṃ nayo darśita iti gamyata ityāha| evamasya kevalasya mahato duḥkhaskandhasya samudayo bhavatīti vacanāt| anyathā hi kimasya sāmarthya syāditi| yadyayaṃ yathokto nayo na syāt

kleśāt kleśaḥ kriyā caiva tato vastu tataḥ punaḥ|  
vastu kleśāśca jāyante

ityetāvadeva brūyāt avidyāpratyayāḥ saṃskārā yāvajjātipratyayā jarāmaraṇaśokaparidevaduḥkhadaurmanasyopāyāsāḥ sambhavantīti| idaṃ na brūyāt evamasya kevalasya mahato duḥkhaskandhasya samudayo bhavatīti| samudaya utpāda ityanarthāntaram| tasmādayathoktaprakārābhidyotakamasya sāmarthyamiti gamyate| ayoniśomanaskārahetukā'vidyoktā sūtrāntare iti sahetusapratyayasanidānasūtre| kathamavidyā bhikṣavaḥ sahetukā sapratyayā sanidānā| kaśca bhikṣavo'vidyāyā hetuḥ kaḥ pratyayaḥ kiṃ nidānam| avidyāyā bhikṣavo'yoniśomanaskāro heturayoniśomanaskāraḥ pratyayo'yoniśomanaskāro nidānamiti sūtre vacanāt| avidyāhetukaścāyoniśomanaskāra iti sūtrāntara ukta ityadhikṛtam| cakṣuḥ pratītya rūpāṇi cotpadyate| āvilo manaskāro mohaja iti sūtre vacanāt| kimartha tasyehāvacanam| kathaṃ ca nāṅgāntaraṃ bhavatīyata āha| sa cehāpyupādānāntarbhūtatvādukto bhavatīti sa ityāvilo manaskāraḥ| iheti pratītyasamutpādasūtre kathamukta upādānāntarbhūtatvā| tathāhyupādānaṃ bhogānāṃ prāptaye kleśasamudācārāvasthāḥ| tatra cāyoniśomanaskāro viparītacetasa ābhāgo'ntarbhūta itukto bhavatyato nāstyahetukatvamavidyāyāḥ| nā cāṅgāntaramupasaṃkhhyātavyaṃ dvādaśambho'vidyādibhyaḥ| na cānavasthāprasaṅgo'vidyāhetukatvādayoniśomanaskārasyeti| apara iti| sthaviro vasunbdhorācaryo manorathopādhyāya eva māha| ācārya āha  kathamiti vistaraḥ| kathamayoniśomanaskārasyopādāne'ntarbhāva| na hyayoniśomanaskāra upādānasvabhāvaḥ yadi saṃprayogataḥ| yadyayoniśomanaskāra upādānena saṃprayujyate ityato bhagavato'syopādāne'ntarbhāva iṣyate| tṛṣṇāvidyayorapi pratītyasamutpādāṅgayostasyāyoniśomanaskārasyāntarbhāvaḥ prāpnoti| tṛṣnavidyābhyāmapi hyasau samprayujyata iti| tataścaivaṃ vaktavyaṃ syāt sa cehāpyavidyātṛṣṇopādānāntarbhūtatvādukto bhavatīti| natvevaṃ vaktavyam sa cehāpyupādānāntarbhūtatvādukto bhavatīti| natvevaṃ vaktavyam sa cehāpyupādānāntarbhūtatvādukto bhavatīti| naivamupādānāntarbhāvāvadhāraṇaṃ kartavyamityabhiprāyaḥ| satyapi cāntarbhāva iti| asambhavantamapyantarbhāvamabhyupetya brūmaḥ| kathamayoniśomanaskāro heturavidyāyā iti vijñāpitaṃ bhavati| na hi tathā sūtre vacanamasti| yadi hyantarbhāveneti vistaraḥ| yadi yasmādupādāne'ntarbhavatyayoniśomanaskāraḥ tasmādayoniśomanaskāro heturavidyāyā iti vijñāpitaṃ bhavati| na ca tadaṅgāntaraṃ vaktavyam upādānavacanādeva tatsiddheḥ| tṛṣṇāvidye api tarhyupādāne'ntarbhavataḥ| atastayorapi tatrāntarbhāvādaṅgāntaratvaṃ śakyamakartum upādānavacanādeva tatsiddheḥ| ukte ca pṛthak tṛṣṇāvidyāṅge tasmādaparihāra eṣaḥ|  
anyaḥ punarāheti bhadantaśrīlābhaḥ| ayoniśomanaskāro heturavidyāyā uktaḥ sūtrāntara iti| sahetusapratyayasanidānasūtre| avidyāpi bhikṣavaḥ sahetukā sapratyayā sanidāneti vistareṇa| sa cāpīti vistaraḥ| sa cāpyayoniśomanaskāraḥ sparśakāle nirdiṣṭaḥ| kathamityāha cakṣuḥ pratītya rūpāṇi cotpadyate āvilo manaskāro mohaja iti| sparśakālo yo'yaṃ cakṣurvijñānotpattikālaḥ| āvilo manaskāraḥ ayoniśaḥpravṛttatvānmohajatvādvā| mohājjāto mohaja iti | avidyāsaṃsparśajaṃ veditamiti| avidyāsamprayuktāt saṃsparśajjātamavidyāsaṃsparśajaṃ veditam yatra veditamavidyāsaṃsparśajam avaśyaṃ tatrāvidyā samprayujyate| na hi v editamasamprayuktamavidyayā tṛṣṇāheturbhavatīti| tadevaṃ sparśakāle bhavannayoniśomanaskāro vedanāsahavartinyā avidyāḥ pratyayabhāvena siddha iti| jñāpitamidaṃ bhavatyayoniśomanaskārahetukā'vidyeti| ato nāstyahetukatvamavidyāyāḥ| ayoniśomanaskārahetukatvāt| na cāṅgāntaramupasaṃkhyeyaṃ dvādaśabhyo'nyat yasmāt sparśakāle bhavannayoniśomanaskāro vedanāsahavartinyā avidyāyāḥ pratyayabhāvena siddha iti | na cāpyanavasthāprasaṅgaḥ tasyāpyayoniśomanaskārasya punarmohajavacanāt| āvilo manaskāro mohaja iti| tadetaccakrakamukta bhavati ayoniśomanaskārādavidyā avidyāyāścāyoniśomanaskāra iti|  
evaṃ punaḥ parihāramācāryaḥ sadopaṃ paśyaṃstasya dāṣasyābhivyaktayartha prasaṅgamārabhate| tattarhīti vistaraḥ| tadyathoktamayoniśomanaskāro heturavidyāyā iti vistareṇa sūtrato yuktayā vā tarhītyarthāntaravivakṣāyām idānīmartha iti bhagavadviśeṣah| etadanyatra sūtra uktamiha punaḥ pratītyasamutpādasūtrai vaktavyam| na vaktavyamiti bhadantaśrīlābhaḥ| kayā yukjtyeti pṛṣṭo bhadantaśrīlābha āha| na hīti vistaraḥ| arhatāmasti vedanā| na ca sā tṛṣnāyāḥ pratyayībhavatīti sāvidyaiva vedanā tṛṣnāpratyaya iti gamyate| na cāviparītasparśaḥ kliṣṭāyā vedanāyāḥ pratyayībhavatīti prakṛtam| na ca punarniravidyasyārhato viparītaḥ sparśa iti| sparśo'pi sāvidya eveti gamyate yo vedanāyāḥ pratyaya uktaḥ pratītyasamutpādasūtre| avidyā cāsya sparśasya heturiti gamyate| ityanayā yuktayā pratītyasamutpādasūtre| avidyā cāsya sparśasya heturiti gamyate| ityanayā yuktayā pratītyasamutpādaṣūtre sāvidyasparśapratyayā vedanā sāvidyavedanāpratyaya ca tṛṣṇeti gamyate ato yuktayā yathoktayā| sa cāpi sparśakāle nirdiṣṭa iti vistareṇātra pratītya samutpāde siddhaṃ bhavatyayoniśomanaskāro heturavidhāyā iti| atra codyate| yadi tarhi niravdyā vedanā tṛṣṇāyāḥ pratyayo na bhavatītyādivacanena svagatayaiva yuktayā ayoniśomanaskāro heturavidyāyā avidyā cāyoniśomanaskārasya heturiti gamyate| kiṃ sūtrāntarāpāśrayeṇāvidyāyoniśomanaskārayoranyonyahetukatvapradarśanenan prayojanamiti| atrocyate| satyamastyetat kintu yuktimātramidaṃ pratītyasamutpādasūtre na kaṇṭhoktiḥ| ataḥ sūtrāntare kaṇṭhoktayeyaṃ yuktirvyavasthāpyata iti tat sūtrāntaropanyāsaprayojanamavagantavyam| atiprasaṅga evaṃ prāpnotīti śāstrakāraḥ| kathamatiprasaṅga ityāha| avidyāsparśajaṃ veditam pratītyotpannā tṛṣṇeti sūtrāntare nirdiṣṭam| na ca niravidyaveditasyārhato vedanā tṛṣṇāyāḥ pratyayo bhavati na cāviparītaḥ sparśaḥ kliṣṭāyā vedanāyāḥ| na ca niravidyasyārhato viparītaḥ sparśa ityanayā yuktayā etadapyaṅgadvayaṃ sparśavedanākhyamiha pratītyasamutpādasūtre'nucyamānamapyanyatropadiṣṭatvādagamyate ityetasyāpyaṅgadvayasyāvacanaṃ prāptam| tathā sūtrāntara upādiṣṭam| sa puṇyāṃśceti saṃskārānabhisaṃskaroti puṇyopagamasya vijñānaṃ bhavati| evamapuṇyopagamāniñjayopagaṃ ca| na ca niravidyasaṃskārasyārhataḥ puṇyopaga vijñānaṃ yāvadaniñjayogaṃ vijñānamiti| etadapi saṃskārāṅga yuktayā siddham| vaktavyamatreti prāptam tathā jātimantareṇa jarāmaraṇaṃ na bhavatīti yuktayā gamyata iti| jātyaṅga na vaktavyaṃ syādityādiprasaṅgaḥ prāpnoti|  
acodyameva tvetaditi vistareṇācāryaḥ svamatamāha kathaṃ paralokādihaloka iti vistaraḥ| tatrāvidyāsaṃskārābhyāmihalokaḥ saṃbadhyate sa cehaloko vijñānanāmarūpaṣaḍāyatanasparśavedanāṅgāni| tṛṣṇopādānabhavaiḥ paralokaḥ saṃbadhyate sa ca paraloko jātirjarāmaraṇaṃ ca| ityetāvato'rthasyātra pratītyasamutpādasūtre vineyebhyo vivakṣitatvāt| etasya cārthasya pūrvamevoktatvāt|  
pūrvāparāntamadhyeṣu sammohavinivṛttaye

ityanena uktatvāt| acodyameva tvetat vistareṇa yāvat na cāparipūrṇo nirdeśa ityabhisambandhaḥ| ubhayaṃ hi sarve saṃskṛtā dharmā iti | traiyadhvikāḥ sarve saṃskṛtā dharmāḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ| ata eva ca pratītyasamutpannā iti na kaścidveṣaḥ| kathaṃ tāvadakṛtā iti bhāvinyā saṃjñayā yathānāgatāḥ saṃskṛtā ucyante tathā pratītyasamutpannā ityabhiprāyaḥ| ābhisaṃskārikayā cetanayeti| sarvacetanānāṃ svalakṣaṇagabhidyotayannābhisaṃskārikayetyāha| sā hi vipākābhisaṃskaraṇādibhisaṃskārikā tayā cetitatvāt praṇihitatvāt| devo bhaviṣyāmi manuṣyo bhaviṣyimītyevamādi| ato'nāgatāḥ saṃskṛtā ucyante| na tu bhāvinyā saṃjñeti tamabhiprāyaṃ vihanti| anāsravāḥ kathamiti ye sāsravā anāgatā evaṃ cetayituṃ yujyante | ye tvanāsravā anāgatāste kathaṃ cetayituṃ yujyante| codaka āha te'pi cetitāḥ kuśalayā cetanayā prāptiṃ prati anāsravā dharmāḥ prāpyeranniti| nirvāṇe'pi prasaṅga iti nirvāṇamapi saṃskṛtatvaṃ prasajyate| prāptiṃ prati cetitatvānnirvāṇaṃ prāpyeteti| tadevamanenārthenānāgatānāṃ saṃskṛtatvavyavasthāpane doṣa iti vidhyantaramupanyasyate| tajjātīyatvātviti vistaraḥ| yathā na ca tāvadrapyate yadanāgataṃ rūpaṃ rūpyamāṇarūpajātīyatvādrūpamityatideśaḥ| tathā pratītyasamutpannadharmajātīyatvādanāgata api dharmāḥ pratītyasamutpannā ityatideśa eṣāmanāgatānāṃ bhavatīti|| 27||  
[ heturatra samutpādaḥ samutpatra phalaṃ matam|  
vidyāvipakṣo dharmo'nyo'vidyamitrānṛtādivat|| 28|| ]

hetuphalabhāvāditi yasmāttadevāṅga hetustadeva phalam| na caivaṃ satyavyavasthānaṃ bhavatīti| pārāpāravat| hetuphalavaditi| nāvyavasthitaḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ pratītya samutpanno bhinnapekṣatvāt| hetuphalavat pitṛputravadvā| tatra hetuphalam tad yathā bījāṅkarādi yathā yadapekṣya heturvyavasthāpyate na tadapekṣya phalaṃ yadapekṣya phalaṃ vyavasthāpyate na tadapekṣya hetuḥ| kiṃ tarhi phalamapekṣya heturvyasthāpyate hetuñcāpekṣya phalamiti| yathā ca yamapekṣya pitā na tamapekṣya putraḥ| yamapekṣya putro na tamapekṣya pitā| kiṃ tarhi tatputramapekṣya piteti pitaraṃ cāpekṣya putra iti| na ca tadavyavasthitam tadvadetat|  
kiletyasaṃbhavanāyām| prathamā koṭiranāgatā dharmā iti| anyadharmahetutvāt pratītyasamutpādaḥ samutpadyate'smāditi kṛtvā| naṃ pratītyasamutpannā anutpannatvāt| dvitīyārhataścaramā iti utpannatvāttataḥ phalānut patteśca| tṛtīyā tadanya iti| arhataścaramebhyo'nye'tītapratyutpannā dharmāḥ| caturthyasaṃskṛtā iti| tatphalābhāvādanutpattimattvācca| kiṃ khalvetā iṣṭaya ucyanta iti| āvasthikaḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ syāt pratītyasamutpādo na pratītyasamutpannā ityevamādyāḥ| pūrvānte'jñānamiti yasmādavidyaivoktā nānye skadhāstasmādavidyaivāṅgamiti| nītārthametaditi darśayati nītārthamiti vibhaktārtha hastipadopame sūtre| santi ca tatrānye'pi rūpādaya iti| teṣu keśaromādiṣu| na pṛthivīdhātumātrameveti| evamatrāpi yathāpradhānaṃ nirdeśaḥ śāditi| evamatrāpi pratītyasamutpādasūtre dvādaśasu pañcaskandhikāsvavasthāsu yatra yasya prādhānyaṃ tatra tasya tasya nirdeśaḥ syāt avidyāpradhānāyāmavidyeti nirdeśaḥ syāt saṃskārādipradhānāsvavasthāsu saṃskārādīnāṃ nirdeśaḥ syāt| santi ca tatrānye'pi rūpavedanādayo dharmā iti| nāhi tatreti vistaraḥ| nahi tatra sūtre keśādayaḥ pṛthivīdhātunā nirdiśyante| keśādayaḥ katame| pṛthivīdhāturiti| yata eṣāṃ keśādīnāmaparipūrṇo nirdeśaḥ syāt| santi hi tatrānye'pi rūpādaya iti| rūpādisvabhāvā api keśādayo na kevalaṃ pṛthivīdhātusvabhāvā iti| api tu keśādibhireva pṛthivīdhāturnirdiśyate| pṛthivīdhātuḥ katamaḥ keśaromāṇīti| na ca keśānabhyatītyāpyatikramyāpyasti pṛthivīdhāturādhyātmika iti| saṃpūrṇa evāsya pṛthivīdhātornirdeśaḥ| evamihāpyavidyādīnām evamihāpi sūtre'vidyāsaṃskāravijñānādīnāṃ paripūrṇa eva nirdeśo'yaṃ na sāvaśeṣaḥ| aśrusiṃghāṇakādiṣviti| aśrusiṃghāṇakādīni na tatra sūtre paṭḥyante tasmānna tadgataḥ pṛthivīdhāturūkta iti sāvaśeṣa eveti darśayati| bhavatu vā tathaiveti vistaraḥ| yathā pṛthivīdhātoraśrusiṃghāṇakādiṣvavaśeṣamanuktatvāt| tathaivāvidyāvaśeṣamastu yadi śakyate darśayitum| na śakyate darśayitumiti manyamāno yadi śabdaṃ prayuṅkte pūrvānte'jñānamaparānte'jñānaṃ madhyānte'jñānaṃ buddhe'jñānaṃ dharme'jñānaṃ saṃghe'jñānaṃ ityevamādīnāṃ sarveṣāmajñānasthānānāmuktatvāt| jātyantarasya tu vedanāderavidyāyāṃ kiṃ kṛtaḥ prekṣapaḥ pañcaskandhikāvasthavidyeti| na hi vedanādīnāmavidyātvaṃ yujyata iti| bhāvābhāvaniyama iti| yasya bhāvābhāvayoryasya bhāvābhāvau yathāsaṃkhyena niyatau tadevāṅgam| avidyābhāve saṃskārāṇāṃ bhāvastadabhāve teṣāmabhāva ityavidyaivāṅgaṃ na vedanādi| na hi vedanādisadbhāve'rhatāṃ saṃskārā bhavanti na bhavantīti vistaraḥ| satyapi ca pañcaskandhake arhatvāvasthāyām| puṇyopagaṃ yāvadāniñjayopagaṃ ca vijñānaṃ na bhavati| pratisandhivijñānaṃ na bhavatītyarthaḥ antāmupapattiṃ gacchatītyupagam puṇyopagaṃ vijñānaṃ puṇyopagam evaṃ yāvadāniñjayopagam| tathā satyapi pañcaskandhe niravidyāvasthāyāṃ tṛṣṇopādānādayaśca na bhavantīti| yathānirdeśa eva sūtrārthaḥ| yathā saṃkīrtitānāmevāvidyadīnāṃ grahaṇamityarthaḥ| ato vidyamānā apyanye skandhā na pratyayāḥ saṃskārādīnāmiti na te'ṅgam|  
evaṃ mayā śrutamityādi| bhagavān rājagṛhe viharati gṛdhakūṭe parvate mahatā bhikṣusaṅghena sārdhamardhatrayodaśabhirbhikṣuśataiḥ saṃbahulaiśca bodhisattvairiti āyuṣmān śāriputro maitreyaṃ bodhisattvametadavocat| śālistamvamavalokya bhikṣubhya sūtramidamuktam| yo bhikṣavaḥ pratītyasamutpādaṃ paśyati sa dharma paśyati yo dharma paśyati sa buddhaṃ paśyati ityukta bhagavāṃstuṣṇīmvabhūva| tadasya bhagavatā bhāṣitasya sūtrasya ko'rthaḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ katamaḥ dharmaḥ katamaḥ buddhaḥ katamaḥ kathaṃ ca pratītyasamutpādaṃ paśyan dharma paśyatītyādi| tatra pratītyasamutpādo nāma yadidamavidyetyādi| idaṃ cābhisandhāyedamukta tatra sūtre| utpādādvā tathāgatānāmanutpādād vā tathāgatānāṃ sthitaiveyaṃ dharmatā dharmasthititā dharmaniyamatā tathatā avitathā ananyatathatā bhūtatāsatyatā tattvamaviparītatā aviparyastetetyevamādi bhagavanmaitreyavacanam yadyanagatā pratītyasamutpannā iti| yadukta prathamā koṭiranāgatā dharmā iti sūtraṃ virūdhyata iti| jātijaramaraṇayoḥ pratītyasamutpannatvaṃ na syāt ukta ca avidyā yāvajjātijarāmaraṇamiti| atha veti vistaraḥ| atha jātijarāmaraṇāṅge'pi pratītyasamutpanne iṣṭe| tayoranāgatādhvavyavasthānaṃ naiṣṭavyamiti kṛtvā trikāṇḍa vayavasthā bhidyate|  
pūrvāparāntayordve dve madhye'ṣṭau

iti nikāyāntarīyā ityāryamahīśasakāḥ| utpādasya saṃskṝtalakṣaṇatvāditi| yo'yamutpādaḥ pratītyasamutpāda iti nikāyāntarīyairnityaḥ parikalpyate sa saṃskṝtalakṣaṇamuktam| trīṇīmāni saṃskṛtasya saṃskṛtalakṣaṇāni saṃskṛtasyotpādo'pi prajñāyate vyayo'pi sthityanyathātvamapīti| saṃskṛtalakṣaṇatvāccānityamityabhiprāyaḥ| brūyāstvaṃ lakṣanamapi nityaṃ bhavatīti| ata āha na ca nityaṃ bhāvāntaramanityasya lakṣaṇaṃ yujyata iti| na hi nirvāṇamākāśaṃ vā kasyacidanityasya lakṣaṇaṃ bhavati| prāgutpatterhi lakṣyasya tadastīti kasya lakṣaṇam vinaṣṭe ca lakṣye kasyeti na yujyate| utpādaśceti visatraḥ| utpādaśca nāmābhūtvā bhāvalakṣaṇaḥ| sautrāntikanayenātpattirdharmasya tadanīmeva bhavatīti| ko'syotpādasyāsaṃskṛtasya tanmatenāvidyādibhirbhisambandhaḥ yathaudanena pākasyāsti sambandhaḥ kartṛkriyālakṣaṇa ityodanasya pāka ityucyate| na hyākāśarūpayorabhisambandha utpattyutottṛlakṣaṇo'sti yena rūpasyākāśaityuhcyate| evaṃ ko'syāvidyādibhirbhisambandhaḥ| yatasteṣāmavidyādīnāṃ pratītyasamutapāda ityucyate padārthaśchāsambandho bhavatīti pratyayaṃ prāpya samudbahvaḥ pratītyasamutpāda iti padārthaḥ| sa kathaṃ nityaśca nāma pratītyasamutpādaśceti| pratiḥ prāptyartha iti| prāptidyotaka ityarthaḥ| i gatāviti dhātvarthapariṇāmāditi| anekārthā hi dhātavaḥ| pratiścopasargaḥ prāptidyotaka ityayamiḥ gatyarthamujj_hitvā prāptyarthamāpadyate| padiḥ sattārtha iti pada sattāyāmiti| teneti kāraṇena| pūrvakālāyāṃ kriyāyāṃ ktvāvidheriti| samānakartṛkayoḥ pūrvakāl iti vacanāt| na cāsau pūrvamutpādāt kaścidastīti sautrāntikamatena| na cāpyakartṛkāsti kriyeti kartair sati kriyāyā vyavasthā panāt| āha cātreti etadvaiyākaraṇacodya śrlokenopanibadhātyācāryaḥ|  
yadi pūrvamutpādāt pratyeti

prāpnoti|  
asattvānna yujyate|  
na hyavidyamānaḥ kartā snānādikriyāṃ kurvan dṛṣṭa it i|  
saha cet

atha manyase pratītyakriyāṃ samutpādakriyāṃ ca sahakarotītyevam|  
ktvā na sidvo'tra

pratyayaḥ| kasmāt

pūrvakālavidhānataḥ

pūrvasmin kale vidhānaṃ ktvāpratyayasya| samānakartṛkayoḥ pūrvakāla iti vacanāt| ataḥ ktvāpratyayo na siddho bhavet| punarūtpattāviti| utpannasya punarūtpattau kalpyamānāyāmanavasthāprasañgaḥ| dvitīye tṛtīye kṣaṇe punaḥ punarūtpadyata ityaniṣṭhāprasaṅgaḥ athānāgata iti vistaraḥ| alabdhātmako'nāgata iti kathamasato'labdhātmalābhasya kartṛtvaṃ sidhyati akartṛkā vā kriyā kathaṃ sidhyati| yata evamato yadasvastha utpadyate śabdikasya śabdavidaḥ tadavastha eva mama pratyeti| kimavasthaśceti kāvasthāsyeti kimavasthaḥ| utpādābhimukho'nāgata iti| na sarvo'nāgata utpadyate kiṃ tarhi utpādābhimukhaḥ| utpāde'bhimukha utpitasurityarthaḥ| tadavastha eva| utpādābhimukhāvastha eva pratyetītyucyate aniṣpannaṃ cedamiti vistaraḥ| śābdikānāmidaṃ śābdikīyam| kartuḥ kriyāyāśca vyavasthānam| kasmāt bhavituḥ kartṛrūpakalptādarthāt kriyārūpakalpitāyā bhūteranyatvādarśanāt| tasmādacchalaṃ bhavatyutpadyate pratyetītyevamādiṣu| āha cātreti| tat siddhāntena prakriyāṃ darśayati| asannutpadyate yadvaditi vistaraḥ| asannabhāvo'labdhātmaka utpadyate yathā pratyetyapi tathā| asannityarthaḥ| atha san atha labdhātmaka utpadyata iti kalpyate| utpanna utpadyate ityaniṣṭhā| utpanno'pi punarūtpadyata ityavasthānādanyadaniṣṭhā prāpnoti| tathāhi sāṃkhyamatānusāreṇa sata evotpādo nāsata iti tadetadadhikṛtya vravīti| san purāpi veti| vā śabdo matavikalpārthaḥ| yadi bhavatāṃ sannutpadyate iti pakṣaḥ asmākamapi sannutpadyate vaibhāṣikanayenānāgatānāmastitvāt sautrāntikanayena ca janakadharmabījasadbhāvāt| tataḥ kimidamucyate na cāsau pūrvamutpādāt kaścidasti yaḥ pratītyottarakālamutpadyata iti| athavā yadi tarhi sannevotpadyate pūrvameva ghaṭo mṛtpiṇḍādīnanapekṣya svataḥ prasiddho'stīti prāpnoti| apare punarvyācakṣate aniṣṭhā'san punarāpi veti| atha mābhūdaniṣṭheti asannutpadyata iti pratipadyase| punastadeva vopatiṣṭhate asannutpadyate yadvat pratyetyapi tathati siddho naḥ pakṣa iti pūrvavadvācyam| yadapyuktamekasyu hi karturdvayoḥ kriyayoḥ pūrvakālāyāṃ kriyāyāṃ ktvāvidhirbhavatīti tadvayabhicārayannāha sahabhāve'pi ca ktvāstīti vistaraḥ| samānakālāyāmapi kriyāyāṃ ktvāvidhirdṛṣṭaḥ tadyathā dīpaṃ prāpya tamo gatamiti| atra na pūrva tamo dīpaṃ prāpnoto paścādgacchati vinaśatītyarthaḥ| kiṃ tarhi tassinneva kāle tamo dīpaṃ pāpnuvad vinśyati| atha matam samanantarameva tanna yugapaditi| ataḥ punaridamucyate āsyaṃ vyādāya śete veti| na hyasau pūrva mukhaṃ vyādadāti vidārayati paścācchete kiṃ tarhi yo mukhaṃ vyādadacḥete sa mukhaṃ vyādāya śeta ityucyate| atha matam pūrvamasau mukhaṃ vyādadāti paścāccheta iti ata āha paścāccet kiṃ na saṃvṛte| yaḥ kaścinmukhaṃ vyādāyānantāṃ ca saṃvṛtya śete sa mukhaṃ vyādāya śeta ityucyeta na cocyate tena jñāyate yo mukhaṃ gyādadacchete sa mukhaṃ vyādāya śeta ityucyata iti| anye punariti bhadantaśrīlābhaḥ| pratirvīpsārtha iti| anānāvācināmadhikaraṇānāṃ sarveṣāṃ kriyāguṇābhyāṃ vyāptumicchā vīpsā tāmayaṃ pratirdyotayati| itau gatau sādhava ityāḥ tatra sādhuriti yatpratyayaḥ itau vinaṣṭau sādhavaḥ| anavasthāyina ityarthaḥ| samupasargaḥ samavāyārtha dyotayati utpūrvaḥ padiḥ prādurbhāvārtha dhātvarthapariṇāmāt| tāṃ tāṃ sāmagrīṃ pratīti| pretaīpsārthatāṃ darśayati| ityānāṃ vinaśvārāṇāṃ samavāyenotpādaḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ| na kaściddharma eka utpadyate sahotpādaniyamāt rūpādīnām

kāme'ṣṭadravyako'śabdaḥ paramāṇuḥ

yāvat

cittacaittāḥ sahāvaśyam

iti niyamāt| eṣā tu kalpaneti vistaraḥ| prativīpsārtha ityevamādikā kalpanā'traiva pratītyasamutpādasūtre yujyate| iha kathaṃ bhaviṣyati cakṣuḥ pratītya rūpāṇi cotpadyate cakṣurvigyānamiti| na hi patītyānāṃ cakṣūṣi pratītyacakṣūṣīti samāsaḥ sambhavatyarthāyogāt cakṣurhi pratītya prāpya rūpāṇi cotpadyate cakṣurvijñānamityayamartho gamyate tana tadavasthameva taccodya na yukta eṣa padārtha iti vistareṇa yaduktam| katham akas hi karturdvayoḥ kriyayoḥ pūrvakālāyāṃ kriyāyāṃ ktāvidhirbhavati| snātvā bhuṅkta iti| na ca pūrvamutpādāccakṣurvijñānamasti yaccakṣuḥ pūrva pratītya rūpāṇi cottarakālamutpadyate na cāpyakartṛkā kriyeti|  
avadhāraṇārtha paryāyadvayamāha asmin satītyetāvatyucyamāne| na kavalamavidyāyāṃ satyāṃ saṃskārā bhavanti anyasminnapi satīti gamyeta yathā cakṣuṣi sati cakṣurvijñānaṃ bhavatviti na cakṣuṣyeva satītyavadhāryate| tena rūpe'pi satīti gamyate| dvitīyena tu paryāyeṇāsyot pādādimutpadyat ityanena pūrvaparyāyamevāvadhāyayati| asyaivotpādādidamutpadyate| nānyasmāditi  'ṅparamparāṃ vā darśayitumiti| asminnaṅge sati| avidyāṅge satīdaṃ saṃskārāṅga bhavati asya punaraṅgasya saṃskārāṅgasyotpādā didaṃ vijñānāṅgamutpadyata iti| evamitareṣāmapyaṅgānāṃ paramparā vaktavyā| janmaparamparāṃ vā| kim darśayituṃ paryāyadvayamāheti vartate| sākṣāditi vistaraḥ| sākṣahat pāramparyeṇa ca santatyā'vidyādīnāṃ pratyayabhāvaṃ darśayituṃ paryāyadvayamāha| asmin satīdaṃ bhavatīti sākṣāt pratyayabhāvaṃ darśayati| asyotpādādidamutpadyata iti pāramparyeṇa| tatra sākṣād yadā avidyāyāḥ samanntaraṃ kliṣṭā saṃskārā utpadyante pāramparyeṇa tu yadā kuśalā utpadyante kuśalāvasthāyā mavidyāyā abhāvāt| kiñca avidyā saṃskārāṇāṃ sākṣāt pratyayo vijñānādīnāṃ pāramparyeṇa| taduktaṃ bhavati| avidyāṅge sati saṃskārāṅgaṃ bhavatyasya saṃskārāṅgasyotpādādidamutpadyate vijñānāṅga
