sākṣādavidyapratyayaṃ pāramparyeṇeti| etat sarvamācāryamatam| pratiṣedhāthamityapara iti sthaviravasuvarmā| nāsati hetau bhāvo bhavatītyahetuvādapratiṣedhārtha prathamena paryāyeṇa| nityahetuvādapratiṣedhārtha na cānutpattimato nityāt prakṛtipurūṣādikāt kiñcidutpadyata iti dvitīyeṇa| ācāryā āha| asyāntu kalpanāyāmiti vistaraḥ| purvapadasya grahaṇamanarthakamiti| asmin satīdaṃ bhavatyasya| ubhayavādapratiṣeghasiddheriti| asyot pādādidamutpadyata ityanenaiva paryāyeṇa| hetorastitvamutpattimataśca hetutvamityubhaya sthāhetutvanityahetutvalakṣaṇasya vādasya pratiṣedhasiddhitaḥ pūrvaparyāyasyānarthakyaṃ prāpnoti| santi tarhi keciditi vistaraḥ| santi kecidvādinaḥ| ya ātmani satyāśrayabhūte| avidyāyā āśrayabhūte| saṃskārādīnāṃ saṃskāravijñānanāmarūpādīnāṃ bhāvamutpādaṃ kalpayanti avidyādīnāṃ cāvidyāsaṃskārādīnāmutpādāttadutpattiṃ saṃskārādyutpattiṃ kalpayantītyadhikṛtam ataḥ kāraṇāttaiṣāṃ vādināṃ kalpanāṃ paryudāsayituṃ pratiṣeddhumidaṃ nidhārayāmbabhūvāmreḍayāmbabhūva| kimityāha yasyaivotpādāt yāvat skandhasya samudayo bhavatīti| yasyaivotpādāda yadutpadyate| tasminneva sati tad bhavati nānyasminnātmani| yadutāvidyāpratyayāḥ saṃskārāḥ| avidyāyāmeva satyāṃ saṃskārā bhavanti nānyasmin yāvadeva masya kevalasya mahato duḥkhaskandhasya samudayo bhavati| katham jātipratyayā jarāmaraṇaśokaparidevaduḥkhadaurmanasyopāyāsāḥ saṃbhavanti| evamasya kevalasya mahato duḥkhaskandhasya samudayo bhavati| jātyāmeva satyāṃ jarāmaraṇādayaḥ saṃbhavanti nānyasminnātmani| anyathā hyasmin satīdaṃ bhavatītyucyamāne vaktāra syuḥ| ātmani satyāśrayabhūte avidyādiṣu satsu saṃskārādayo bhavantīti| atha punardvitīyameva paryāyaṃ brūyāt| asyotpādādidamutpadyata iti| evamapi tathaiva kalpeyuḥ| satyam avidyādīnāmutpādāt saṃskārādaya utpadyante| ātmanī tu satyāśrayabhūta iti| ataḥ paryāyadvayena nirdhārayati| yasyotpādādyadutpadyate tasminneva sati tadbhavati nānyasminnātmani satyāśrayabhūta iti| ācāryā iti pūrvācāryāḥ aprahīṇajñāpānārthamutpattijñāpanārtha ca| avidyāyāṃ satyāmaprahīṇāyāṃ saṃskārā bhavanti| na prahīyanta iti prathamena paryāyeṇa darśayati| tasyā evotpādādutpadyanta iti dvitīyena darśayati| evaṃ yāvajjātyāṃ satyāmaprahīṇāyāṃ jarāmaraṇadayo bhavanti na prahīyante| tasyā evotpādādutpadyanta iti| sthityutpattisandarśanārthamityapara bhadantaśrīlābhaḥ| tadartha paryāyadvayāmāheti| yāvat kāraṇastrotastāvat kāryastroto bhavatīti prathamena paryāyena| kāraṇasyaivot vo bhikṣavo deśayiṣyāmītyadhikṛte kaḥ prasaṅgaḥ kaḥ prastāvaḥ sthitivacanasya asmin satīdaṃ bhavatītyanena yāvat kāraṇasrotastiṣṭhati tāvat kāryasrotastiṣṭhatīti| evamasya sthityartho varṇayata iti| bhinnakramaṃ ca bhagavān kimarthamācakṣīta| utpattirhi prathamaṃ bhavati paścāt sthitiḥ| utpattipratibaddhā sthitiriti kṛtvā asyotpādādidamutpadyata iti pūrva vaktavyaṃ syāt| paścādimasin satīdaṃ bhavati| natvevam ato nāyamartha iti| punarāheti sa eva bhadantaśrīlābhaḥ| ācāryaḥ pratyāha| eṣa cediti vistaraḥ| asmin satīdaṃ na bhavatītyevāvakṣyaditi| nañaivābhāvanirdeso yuktarūpa iti darśayati| pūrvaṃ ca karyasyotpādamiti| asyotpādādidamutpadyate iti pūrvamavakṣyat utpādapūrvakatvādvināśasya |  
sūtra ukta jātipratyayā jarāmaraṇaśokaparidevaduḥsvadaurmanasyopāyāsāḥ sambhavantīti jarāmaraṇasaṃgṛhītā evaṃ te| nāṅgāntaramityavantavyam| tatra sattvāsattvasaṃkhyātasya viṣayasya pariṇāmādātmabhāvapariṇāmāccaite bhavanti daurmanasyasamprayukto vitarkaḥ śokaḥ| śokasamutthitaḥ pralāpaḥ paridevaḥ| duḥkhadaurmanasye vyākhyāte| vicchinnavegaṃ daurmanasyamupāyāsaḥ| śokaparidevapūrvakaḥ śrama upāyāsa ityapare| bālo hīti vistaraḥ| bālaḥ pṛthagajanaḥ saṃskāramātramiti| mātragrahaṇamātmapratiṣedhārtham| aprajānannityāveṇikīmavidyāṃ darśayati| ātmadṛṣṭayasmimānābhiniviṣṭaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ kāyādibhiḥ kāyavāṅmanobhistrividhaṃ karma puṇyāpuṇyāniñjalakṣaṇamārabhate| āṇiñjyamiti| igiḥ prakṛtyantaraṃ tasyaivaitadrūpam| ejateretadrūpamāneñjayamiti vā pāṭhaḥ| āyatisukhārtha puṇyamiti anāgatajanmasukhārtham| kim kāmāvacaraṃ kuśalaṃ karma| sukhā dūhkhasukhārthamāniñjayamiti| rūpārūpyāvacaraṃ kuśalam| susvārthamā tṛtīyāddhayānāt tasmādūrdhvamaduḥkhāsusvārtham| aihikasukhārthamapuṇyamiti| ihasukhāpekṣayā tat kṛtaṃ nāyatisukhāpekṣayetyarthaḥ| karmākṣepavaśād yathokta karmāvedhavaśāt tāṃ tāṃ gatiṃ narakadikaṃ gacchati jvālāgamanayogena yathā dīrdhā tṛṇānuṣaktā jvālā gacchatītyucyate tathā vijñānasantatibhirantarābhavasambandhādantarābhavasambandhenātiviprakṛṣṭadeśāmapi tāṃ tāṃ gatiṃ gacchati| evañca kṛtveti| yadi saṃskārapratyayamantarābhavapratisandhicittamupādāya yāvadupapattikṣaṇaḥ sarvāsau vijñānasantatiḥ ṣaṇṇāṃ vijñānakāyānāṃ saṃskārapratyayaṃ vijñānamabhipretaṃ tadupapannaṃ bhavati| vijñānāñganirdeśe vijñānāṅgasya tṛtīyasya nirdeśe| vijñānaṃ katamat ṣaḍvijñānakāyā iti| anyathā hi yadi ṣaṇṇāṃ vijñānakāyānāṃ santatirnābhipretā syāt saṃskārapratyayaṃ vijñānaṃ pratisandhicittamevābhipretaṃ syāt atra ṣaḍvijñānakāyā iti nokta syāt evaṃ tu vaktavyaṃ syāt| vijñānaṃ katamat manovijñānamiti| na hi pratisandhikṣaṇe pañcavijñānakāyasambhavo'sti| manovigyānenaiva pratisandhibandhāt|  
chedasandhānavairāgyahānicyutyupapattayaḥ|  
manovijñāna eveṣṭāḥ

iti vacanāt| vibhaṅga evaṃ nirdeśāditi pratītyasamutpādasūtre nāmarūpavibhaṅga evaṃ nirdeśāt| nāma katamat catvāro'rūpiṇaḥ skandhā iti| rūpaṃ katamat yatkiñcidrūpamiti vistarena| yāvadyaccedaṃ rūpaṃ yahcca nāma tadubhayaṃ nāmarūpamityucyata iti| kāmatṛṣṇeti kāmāvacarī tṛṣṇā| rūpatṛṣṇeti yā sukhāyāṃ triṣu dhyāneṣu| adukhāsukhāyāṃ caturthe| aduḥkhāsukhāyāmārūpyatṛṣṇeti ārūpyāvacarī| kāmādīnāmupādānamiti| kāmadṛṣṭiśīlavratātmavādānamupādānamityarthaḥ| pañca kāmaguṇā iti| kāmyanta iti kāmāḥ guṇyanta iti guṇāḥ kāmā eva guṇāḥ kāmaguṇāḥ| ke punaste| rūpaśabdagandharasaspraṣṭavayāni| dṛṣṭayo dvāṣaṣṭiryathā brāhyajāla iti| brahyajālasūtradarśanena tā dṛṣṭayo boddhavyāḥ| kukkuragovratādīnīti| ādiśabdena mṛgavratādīni gṛhyante| nirgranthādīnāmiti ādiśabdena pāṇḍarabhikṣvādīnāṃ grahaṇam| brahyaṇetyādivistaraḥ| brahyaṇānāṃ daṇḍājinam| pāśupatānāṃ jaṭābhasma| parivrājakānāṃ tridaṇḍamauṇḍayam| ādiśabdadvayena kāpālikādīnāṃ kapāladhāraṇādīni gṛhyante| tatsamādānaṃ śīlavratopādānam ātmeti vādo'sminnityātmavādaḥ| yathoktam ye kecicchamaṇā brahyaṇā vā ātmeti samanupaśyantaḥ samanupaśyanti sarve ta imāneva pañcopādānaskandhāniti vistaraḥ| ātmadṛśṭayasmimānāvityapara ātmavādamāhuḥ| ātmano hyasattvādātmavādopādānamiti| vādaśabdopanyāse prayojanaṃ darśayati| bālo'śrutavān pṛthagjana iti| yasya pūrvābhyāsavāsanānirjātopapattilābhikā prajñā nāsti sa bālaḥ yasyāgamajā nāsti so'śrutavān yasyādhigamajā satyābhisamayajā nāsti sa pṛthagjanaḥ| prajñaptimanupatita iti yathā saṃjñā yathā ca vyavahārastathānugata ityarthaḥ| teṣāmiti kāmādīnām| chandarā iti aprāpteṣu viṣayeṣu prārthanā chandaḥ prāpteṣu rāgaḥ| teṣu kāmādiṣu yaśchandarāgastadupā dānam| na tu

yathoktā eva sāvidyā dvidhā dṛṣṭivivecanāt|  
upādānāni

ityabhiprāyaḥ| paunarbhavikamiti punarbhavaphalam| idamatra bhavasyeti| idamatra bhavasya svalakṣaṇaṃ svabhāva ityarthaḥ| vijñānāvakrāntiyogeneti| jvālāgamanayogepāntarābhavasambandhādanāgataṃ janma jātiḥ pañcaskandhikā nāmarūpasvabhāvatvāt| jarāmaranaṃ yathānirdiṣṭaṃ sūtra iti| jarā katamā yattat khālitya pālitya miti vistaraḥ| maraṇaṃ katamat ya teṣāṃ teṣāṃ sattvānāṃ tasmāttaccyutiścya vanamiti vistaraḥ| kegalasyātmarahitasyetyartha darśayati mahata ityanādyantasya duḥkhaskandhasyeti duḥkhasamūhasya| sa eva tu vaibhāṣikanyāyo yaḥ pūrvamukta iti| dvādaśa pañcaskandhikā avasthā dvādaśāṅgānīti

yā na vidyā| cakṣurādiṣvapi prasaṅga iti| cakṣuḥśrotrādīnyavidyā prasajyante tānyapi hi na vidyeti kṛtvā| na kiñcit syāditi yadi vidyāyā abhāvo'vidyā adravyaṃ syādityarthaḥ| na cetadyuktamiti pratyayabhāvenopadeśāt| tadvipakṣa iti virodhe nañiti darśayati| na tu yaḥ kaścidanyo mitrāditi| na paryudāsamātra iti darśayati| nāpi mitrābhāva iti| nābhāvārtha iti darśayati adharmānarthākāryādayaśceti| ādiśabdenāyuktayavyavahārāmanuṣyādayo gṛhyante| dharmādipratidvandvabhūtā iti  dharmārthakāryayuktivyavahāramanuṣyādipratidvandvabhūtāḥ| na tu ye kecidanye dharmādibhyaścakṣurādayaḥ nāpi dharmādyabhāvaḥ| evamavidyāpīti vistaraḥ| kuta etat pratyayabhāvenopadeśāt| avidyāpratyayāḥ saṃskārā iti| na cāvidyāyā anyeṣāṃ cakṣurādīnāṃ pratyayabhāvenopadeśe yuktaḥ arhatāṃ saṃskārāsambhavāt| na cābhāvasya śaśaviṣāṇādīnāṃ pratyayatvāt|| 28||  
[ saṃyojanādivacanāt kuprajñā cetna darśanāt|  
dṛṣṭestatsamprayuktatvāt prajñopakleśadeśanāt || 29|| ]

saṃyojanaṃ yāvadyogaścāvidyocyata iti| avidyāsaṃyojanaṃ yāvadavidyāyoga iti sūtrepucyate| na cābhāvamātraṃ tathā bhavitumarhatīti| na saṃyojanādi bhaviturmahatītyarthaḥ| na cāpi cakṣurādayastathā bhavitumarhantītyadhikṛtam| sā ca dṛṣṭisvabhāveti nāvidyāṃ yujyata iti| dṛṣṭyavidyayoḥ saṃyojanāditvena pṛthak pāṭhāt| yā tarhi na dṛṣṭiriti| yā kliṣṭā prajñā rāgādisamprayuktā|  
dṛṣṭestat samprayuktatvāt

iti avidyāsamprayuktatvādityarthaḥ| kathañca dṛṣṭiravidyayā samprayuktā| yasmādavidyālakṣaṇo mohaḥ kleśamahābhūmau paṭhyate| kleśamāhabhūmikāśca sarve'pi mahābhūmikaiḥ saha samprayujyanta iti| dṛṣṭyā satkāyadṛṣṭyādikayā prajñāsvabhāvayā avidyā samprayujyata iti gamyate| tasmānnāvidyā prajñā dvayoḥ prajñādravyayorasamprayogāt| avidyopakliṣṭā prajñā na viśudhyatīti| etadudāharaṇam| yenāvidyā prajñāyā arthāntarabhūteti| na ca saiva prajñā tasyāḥ prajñāyā upakleśo yukta iti| prajñopakleśadeśanāyāṃ prajñāyā arthāntarabhūtā avidyā upakleśatvena nirdiṣṭatvāt rāgavat| ata āha| yathā cittasyānya iti vistaraḥ| vyavakīryamāṇā na viśudhyatīti| antarāntarotpadyamānayā kliṣṭayā prajñayā vyavakīryamāṇā kṣaṇāntarotpannā kuśalā prajñā na viśudhyatītyarthaḥ| kiṃ tadavaśyaṃ rāgaparyavasthitamiti| rāgasamprayuktamityarthaḥ| upahatantu tattathā rāgeṇeti| samudācaratā rāgeṇa vāsanadhānena tadupahataṃ bhavati| tāṃ bhāvanāṃ tad dauṣṭulthaṃ vyāvartayato yoginastaccittaṃ vimucyate| evamavidyopahatā kliṣṭā prajñā na śudhyatīti| ataḥ prajñā cāvidyā na ca doṣa iti darśayati| ko hi parikalpayanvāryata iti| parikalpanāmātrametadāgamanirapekṣamiti kathayati|  
yo'pi manyate sarvakleśāvidyeti| bhadantaśrīlābha evaṃ manyate avidyeti sarvakleśānāmiyaṃ sāmānyasaṃjñā| na rāgādikleśavyatiriktā'vidyā nāmāstīti| kasmāt sa evaṃ manyate buddhasūtrāt| na prajānāti na prajānātītyuṣman mahākauṣṭhila tasmādavidyocyata iti satkāyadṛṣṭyādayo'pi kleśā ajñānasvarūpā viparītagrahaṇata iti| tasyāpyata eveti vistaraḥ| tasya bhadantasyāta eva pratiṣedhavacamādvyudāsaḥ| pratiṣedhastanmatasyetyadhyāhāryam| kathamityāha| sarvakleśasvabhāvā hi satī saṃyojanādiṣu pṛthag nocyate| aniśayasaṃyojanādiṣvavidyāsaṃyojanamiti pṛthag nocyeta śeṣasaṃyojanāvyatirekāt| rāgo bandhanaṃ dveṣo bandhanamityuktvā moho bandhanamiti kāmarāgānuśayaḥ ityādyuktā avidyānuśaya iti agheṣvavidyagha iti yogeṣvavidyāyoga iti pṛthagnaivocyeta| kasmāt parasparāsaṃprayogāt| na hi dṛṣṭirdṛṣṭyā saṃyujyate rāgo vā rāgeṇa dṛṣṭyādisaṃprayuktā cāvidyoktā kleśamahābhūmikatvena mahābhūmikasamprayoganiyamāt| cittamapi cāvidyopakliṣṭamevoktaṃ bhavet| rāgopakliṣtaṃ cittaṃ na vimcuhyata iti nokta bhavet avidyopakliṣṭatvavacanenaiva rāgādyupakliṣṭatvasiddheḥ| atha matam viśeṣaṇārthaṃ tathoktamiti rāgalakṣaṇayāvidyayopaliṣṭacittaṃ na vimucyata iti prajñāyāmapi viśeṣaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ syāt| rāgeṇa pratighena mānena veti mābhūt sarvakleśagrahaṇaprasaṅga iti iṣṭa iti cet citte'pi na viśeṣaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ syāt| avidyopakliṣṭa cittaṃ na vimucyata ityevameva tu vaktavyaṃ syāt|  
saṃprakhyānaṃ prajñānamityeko'rthaḥ tathaiva dopo yathāvidyāyāmiti athāsaṃprakhyānamiti ko'rthaḥ yanna saṃprakhyānam| cakṣurādiṣvapi prasaṅgaḥ| saṃprakhyānābhāvastarhi evaṃ sati na kiñcit syāt| na caitad yuktamiti atha saṃprakhyānavipakṣabhūtaṃ dharmāntaram| tadidaṃ tathaiva na jñāyate yathavāvidyā kiṃ taditi| evaṃ jātīyako'pīti vistaraḥ| evaṃ jātīyakaḥ karmaprabāvita ityarthaḥ| tasmādavidyāyāḥ karmaṇā kāritreṇa svabhāvo vijñātavyaḥ astyasau kaściddharmo yo vidyāvipakṣa iti vidyāvipakṣatvaṃ tsyāḥ karmeti tad yathā cakṣḥuḥ katamat| yo rūpaprasādaścakṣurvigyānasyāśraya iti nanu ca rūpaprasāda iti svabhāvaprabhāvito'sya nirdeśa iti na apratyakṣatvānnityaṃ cakṣurvijñānameyatvācca asmīti sattvamayateti| avijñātārthametat| na tena sthavirenāsyābhidheyamavadhāritam| mayatā punaḥ sautrāntikaraividyā prakārabhinnā varṇayate māno vā | yāsau sūtra ukteti vistaraḥ| mayatānāmityavidyānāṃ prakārabhinnānām nāmeti grahaṇam tṛṣṇādīnāṃ pṛthagupadeśaditi bhadantadharmatrātasyābhiprāyaḥ| ācārya āha astyeṣā mayatā asti sutre uktā mayatā tatra jātinirdeśadekavacanam sā tvavidyeti| kutaḥ kāraṇādetat paricchidyate sāvidyeti| bhadantadharmatrātaḥ svābhiprāyaṃ vivṛṇoti yata eṣā nānyaḥ kleśaḥ śakyate vaktumiti| kasmānna śakyate tṛṣṇādṛṣṭāsmimānānāṃ pṛthagukteḥ| ācārya āha nanu cānyo māna eva syāditi asmimānādanyaḥ ṣannāmanyatamaḥ sa ca mayatā nāvidyetyabhi prāyaḥ| atra tu punavicāryamāṇe vahu vaktavyaṃ jāyata iti atra punavicāryamāṇe'vidyāṅge bahurayaṃ grantho jāyate yathoktamiti| tasmāttiṣṭhatvetat etāvadevāstviti athavā'nyo māna eva syāt na punaravidyeti| kuta etadityevamādike punarvicāre kriyamāṇe uttarapratyuttaravacanena bahu vaktavyaṃ jāyate tasmāttiṣṭhatveta dalaṃ vicārāntareṇetyarthaḥ|| 29 ||  
[ nāma tvarūpiṇaḥ skandhāḥ sparśāḥ ṣaṭ sannipātajāḥ|  
pañca pratighasaṃsparśāḥ ṣaṣṭho'dhivacanāhvayaḥ|| 30|| ]

atha nāmarūpamiti avidyāṅganirdeśānantaraṃ saṃskārāṅgaṃ nirdeṣṭavyam| tadanu vijñānāṅgam kimityetadatikramyopanyasyate  etadarthamatra vakṣyatyācāryaḥ

ukta ca vakṣyate cānyat

iti| rūpaṃ vistareṇoktamiti

rūpaṃ pañcendiryāṇayarthāḥ pañcāvijñaptireva ca

ityatra| nāmendriyārthavaśeneti vistaraḥ| nāmavaśena indriyavaśena arthavaśena cārtheṣu rūpādiṣu namīti nāma katamasya nāmno vaśeneti nāmadvaividhyāt pṛcchati| ācāryastvācaṣṭe yadidaṃ loke pratītamiti vistaraḥ| gauraśva iti samudāyapratyāyakam| rūpaṃ rasa iti ekārthapratyāyakam| tadetad vedanādisakndhacatuṣkaṃ nāmaśabdenocyate| yasmāttannāmavaśena saṃjñākaraṇavaśenārtheṣvapratyakṣeṣu pratyakṣeṣvapi vā namati pravartate| asya nāmno'yamartha iti| indriyārdhavaśena tu pratyakṣeṣu rapādiṣu namatyutpadyata ityarthaḥ| nāmni cāpyarthavaśena namati asyārthasyedaṃ nāmeti| etasya punaḥ kena nāmatvamiti| etasya saṃjñākaraṇalakṣaṇasya nāmnaḥ kena kāraṇena nāmatvam| teṣu teṣvartheṣu tasya nāmno namanāditi| yasyātteṣvartheṣu catuḥskandhalakṣaṇaṃ nāma tannāma namayati pravartayati tasmāt tasmāt etasya nāmatvam namayatīti nāmeti kṛtvā| iha nikṣipta iti vistara| maraṇakāle nikṣipte kāya upapattyantare namād gamanādarūpiṇo vedanādayaḥ skandhā nāmetyucyante| yathoktam| mṛtasya khalu kālaṃ gatasya jñātaya imaṃ pūtikāyamagninā vā dahanti udake vā plāvayanti bhūmau vā nikhananti vātātapābhyāṃ vā pariśoṣaṃ parikṣayaṃ paryādānaṃ gacchati| yat punaridamucyate cittamiti vā mana iti vā vijñānamiti vā śraddhāparibhāvitaṃ śīlatyāgaśrutaprajñāparibhāvitaṃ tadūrdhvagāmi bhavati viśeṣagāmyāyatyāṃ svargopagamiti|  
paḍāyatanamuktamiti|  
tadvijñānāśrayā rūpaprasādāścakṣurādayaḥ|  
vijñānaṃ prativijñaptirmana āyatanaṃ ca tat

ityatra| mana indriyasya punarnirūddhasyānāgatena dharmaṇa rūpādinot patasyāmānena vartamānena manovigyānena kathaṃ sannipātaḥ| yaḥ kārya karaṇabhāvaḥ kārya manovijñānam kāraṇaṃ mana indriyam dharmaśca rūpādiḥ| ekakāryārtho veti| ekakāryamthaḥ ekakāryārtha ekakāryatvamityarthaḥ katha mekakāryārtha ityāha sarve ca te trayo'pi mana indriyādayaḥ| sparśotpattau praguṇā anukūlā bhavantīti|  
paṭṣaṭko dharmaparyāya iti ṣaṭ ṣaṭkānyasminniti ṣaṭṣaṭkaḥ| ādhyātmikabāhyavijñānasparśavedanātṛṣnāṣaṭkasambhavāt| ṣaṭsparśakāyā iti jñāpakam| ananyatve hi satīndriyārthavijñāneṣūkteṣu sparśakāyavacanaṃ punarūkta syāt| tataśca ṣaṭṣaṭkatvaṃ hīyate| mābhūt dharmāyatanādvedanātṛṣṇayoḥ pṛthagbhāva iti| yadi pṛthaṅnirdeśāt pṛthagbhāvo bhavet vedanātṛṣne api dharmāyatanāt pṛthaṅnirdiṣte| tayorapi vedanātṛṣnayordharmāyatanāt pṛthagbhāvaḥ syāt| na ca tayoḥ pṛthagbhava iṣyate dharmāyatanāntarbhāvāt| naipa doṣa iti vistaraḥ| naiṣa doṣa iti yathoktastadvayatiriktasyāpi vedanātṛṣṇāvyatiriktasyāpi dharmāyatanasya tajjādisvabhāvasya bhāvādastitvāt| na caivamiti vistaraḥ| na caivaṃ bhavatastrāyāṇāṃ sannipātaḥ sparśa ityevaṃ vruvāṇasya sparśabhūtādindriyārthavijñānatrayādanyadasparśabhūtaṃ trayamindriyārthavijñānatrayamasti yasya śeṣasyāstara sūtre grahaṇaṃ syāt| ṣaṭ sparśakāyā iti prayojanavaśādapṛthag bhāve'pi sparśabhūtāttayāt| tasmādapṛthagbhāve'pi vedanātṛṣnayoḥ pṛthaṅ nirdeśoyukto vineyakāryavaśāt| na tu bhavataḥ sannipātamātrasparśavādinaḥ kenacidapi prayojanena ṣaṭ sparśakāyā iti pṛthaṅ nirdeśo yuktaḥ| yasmāt ṣaṭ sparśakāyā iti vacanenendiryārthavigyānatrayamuktamiti tadvacanaṃ prāptamityataḥ ṣaṭṣaṭko dharmaparyāya iti na sidhyet| mama tu sidhyati| sparśavedanātṛṣṇāṣaṭkavacane'pi tadvyatiriktadharmāyatanādyarthendriyavijñānaṣaṭkasambhavāt| tasmāt ṣaṭ sparśakāyā iti pṛthaṅnirdeśādasti cittsamprayukta sparśākhyaṃ dharmāntaramiti siddham| atha brūyādasau indriyārthāvavijñānakau nirdiṣṭāvasmin sūtre paḍādhyātmikāni ṣaḍ bāhyānīti vacanāt| ata indriyaviṣayavijñānatrayaṃ sparśabhūtamityasya śeṣasya jñāpanārthamatra grahaṇaṃ syāt ṣaṭ sparśakāyā iti| asya samādheḥ pratiṣedhārthamidamārabhyate| yadyapīndriyārthai syātāmavijñānakau| tat sabhāgāvitayarthaḥ| na tu punarvijñānaminindriyārthakam| avaśyaṃ hi vijñānaṃ sendriyārthakaṃ bhavati| tacca vijñānamapadiṣṭamatra ṣaḍvijñānakāyā iti tasmāttiṣu nirdiṣṭeṣu indriyārthavijñāneṣu ṣaḍvigyānakāyavacanena punaḥ sparśagrahaṇamanarthakaṃ prāpnoti ṣaṭ sparśakāyā iti| ataḥ sannipātādanyaḥ sparśa iti siddham| atra bhadantaśrīlābha āha na khalviti vistaraḥ| pūrvotpanne cakṣurūpe kāraṇam na tu vijñānasahotpannenāpi sarvacakṣurvijñānamiti| paścādutpanna pūrvayoścakṣurūpayorna sahotpannayoḥ| ato yeṣāṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvaste| vijñānendriyaviṣayāḥ sparśabhāvena vyavasthitāḥ ṣaṭ sparśakāyā ityasmin sūtre| tadidamanyathā na jñāpitaṃ syāt|  
na vā evaṃ paṭhanti vai iti nipātaḥ na vai evaṃ paṭhanṭityarthaḥ kathaṃ punaḥ paṭhanti| ya eṣāṃ dharmāṇāṃ saṅgatiḥ sannipātaḥ samavāyaḥ sa sparśa iti paṭhanti| kāraṇe vā kāryopacāro'yamiti| saṅgatau sannipāte samavāye kāraṇe kāryopacāraḥ sparśa iti| yathā sukho buddhānāmutpāda iti atibahuvistareṇa prakāreṇa visartu śīlamasyā iti atibahuvistaraprakāravisāriṇī|  
adhivacanamucyate nāmeti| adhyucyate'nenetyadhivacanam| vāṅnāmni pravartate nāmārtha dyotayati ityadhivacanaṃ nāma| tat kila nāmārthe manovijñānasamprayuktasyādhikaṃ bāhulyenā lambanam| ato'dhivacanaṃ saṃsparśa iti| sparśaḥ saṃsparśa ityeko'rthaḥ| mano vijñānena nīlaṃ vijānātīti abhidheyaṃ vijānātītyarthaḥ| nīlamiti ca vijānātīti asyārthasyedaṃ nāmeti vijānāti| eka āśrayapramāvita iti prathama āśrayeṇa prabhāvitaḥ pratighāśrayaḥ saṃsparśaḥ pratighasya vā saṃsparśaḥ pratighasaṃsparśaḥ| ālamvanaprabhāvitaḥ iti| adhivacanādālambanasaṃsparśaḥ| adhivacane vā saṃsparśaḥ adhivacanasaṃsparśa iti| vacanamadhikṛtyeti| vacanamavadhārya| artheṣu rūpādiṣu manovijñānasya pravṛttiḥ| na pañcānāṃ cakṣurvijñānādīnāṃ vacanamadhikṛtyārtheṣu pravṛttiḥ| atastadevādhivacanaṃ manovijñānaṃ vacane pravartate'dhivacanamiti| tena samprayuktastasya vā saṃsparśasyādhivacanasaṃsparśaḥ tena cāyaṃ dvitīyaḥ saṃsparśaḥ samprayogaprabhāvita ityucyate|| 30||  
[ vidyāvidyetarasparśā amalakliṣṭaśoṣitāḥ|  
vyāpādānunayasparśai sukhavedyādayastrayaḥ|| 31|| ]

vidyāvidyetarasparśāḥ

iti vidyā avidyā itaraśca vidyāvidyetare| ta eva sparśā vidyāvidyetarasparśāḥ| vidyā cāvidyā ceti vidyāhvaḥ avidyāhvaḥ tābhyāmitareścetyarthaḥ| vidyetyanāsravā prajñā avidyā kliṣṭamajñānam| naivavidyānāvidyā kuśalasāsravā prajñā tat saṃprayuktā abhīkṣṇasamudācārī nityasamudācārī ekadeśaḥ| tasya grahaṇād dvau sparśai bhavataḥ

vyāpādānunayasparśai sukhavedyādayastrayaḥ

iti sukhasya vedaḥ sukhavede sādhuḥ saukhavedyaḥ sukhaṃ vā vedyamasminniti sukhavedyaḥ sa ādirepāmiti sukhavedyādayaḥ| sukhavedanādihitvāditi sukhavedanādau hitaḥ sukhavedanīyaḥ prakakrītācchaḥ| vedyate tadvedayituṃ vā śakyamiti vedyata iti kamaimātre kṛtya iti darśayati| vedayitu śakyamiti arhe kṛtyatṛcaścetsminnarthe kṛtya iti darśayati| ta ete ṣoḍaśeti cakṣuḥsaṃsparśo yāvanmanaḥ saṃsparśa iti ṣaṭ| punaḥ pratighasaṃsparśo'dhivacanasaṃsparśo vidyāsaṃsparśo'vidyāsaṃsparśo naivavidyānāvidyāsaṃsparśo vyāpādasaṃsparśo'nunayasaṃsparśaḥ sukhavedanīyo dūḥkhavedanīyo'duḥkhāsukhavedanīyaśca sparśa iti ṣoḍaśa|| 31||  
uktaḥ sparśo vedanā vaktavyeti| nanu ca vedanāpyuktā

vedanānubhavaḥ

iti| anyathoktamapyucyate| na tarhīdaṃ vaktavyaṃ yaccokta yacca vakṣyamāṇam tanna vakṣyāmīṭi

ukta ca vakṣyate cānyat

iti vacanāt prakāranirdeśādadoṣaḥ| purva lakṣaṇamukta idānīṃ prakāra iti |  
[ tajjāḥ ṣaḍvedanāḥ pañca kāyikī caitasī parā|  
punaścāṣṭādaśavidhā sā manopavicārataḥ|| 32|| ]

jāte hīti vistaraḥ| jāte hi janye dharme dharmasya janakasya nāsti sāmarthya sahotpannatvāt| pratijñāviśiṣṭamiti pratijñāyā aviśiṣṭametat sādhanamityarthaḥ yadi pratijñāviśiṣṭamiti pratijñāyā aviśiṣṭametat sādhanamityarthaḥ yadi pratijñāviśiṣṭaṃ sādhanaṃ neṣyate anyonyajanakaprasaṅgāttarhi kiṃ kathaṃ sahotpannayorjanyajanakabhāvaḥ sidhyatītyadhikṛtam anena pratijñādopa udgrāhyate asti sahotpannayorjanyajanakabhāva iti pratijñā pūrvābhyupagamavirodhinīti| iṣṭatvādadoṣa iti| pūrvābhyupagamaṃ darśayati iṣṭamidaṃ sūtre tvaniṣṭamiti iṣṭamidamabhidharme|  
sahabhūrye mithaḥ phalāḥ

iti| sūtre tvaniṣṭam| tat pratipādayannāha | cakṣuḥ saṃsparśa pratītyot padyate cakṣuḥsaṃsparśajā vedaneti| sūtraprāmāṇyenānyonphalatvaṃ bhaṃktvā sahotpannakāryakāraṇabhāvavibhaṃkṣayā āha janakadharmātikramāccāyuktamiti| janakasyāyaṃ dharmaḥ prasiddho yā bhinnakālatā tadyathā pūrvaṃ bījaṃ paścādaṅkura ityādi tatrāpi pūrvamindriyārtho paścādvijñānamiti prathamakṣaṇotpannāvindriyārtho vijñānaṃ tu dvitīye kṣṇa iti yathā tarhi chāyāṅkurayoriti| kim sahotpannayorjanyajanakabhāvaḥ| utpanna eva hyaṅkuraśchāyāṃ janayati| na prathame kṣane'ṅkuro niśchāyo bhavati dvitīye sacchāya iti| evaṃ sparśavedanayorapīti anayorapi chāyāṅkarayoḥ pūrvasāmagrīheturiti śakyaṃ vaktum| sahabhūhetunirdeśe tvayamartha ukta iti na punarūcyate|  
sparśāduttarakālaṃ vedanetyapara iti bhadantaśrīlābhaḥ| so'sau trayāṇāṃ sanniyātaḥ sparśa iti| yo'yaṃ janakajanitabhāvaḥ| vedanātṛtīye kṣaṇe iti indriyārthakṣaṇaḥ prathamaḥ vijñānotpattikṣaṇo dvitīyo vedanotpattikṣaṇastṛtīya iti| na sarvatra vijñāne vedanā prāpnotīti| vijñānakṣaṇe dvitīye vedanāyā abhāvāt| na ca sarvavijñānaṃ sparśaḥ vedanākṣaṇe tṛtīye vijñānaṃ na sparśaḥ| pūrvadvitīyakṣaṇayoḥ sparśa eva vidhīyate| pūrvasparśahetukā hyuttaratna sparśa vedanā yo'yamidānīṃ dvitīyaḥ kṣaṇaḥ sparśabhāvenoktastatra pūrvasparśahetukā prathamakṣaṇavijñānahetukā vedanotpadyate| tat sparśapūrvikā'pyanyasminnityevaṃ sarvatra vijñāne vedanā prāpnoti| sarva ca vijñānaṃ sparśa iti te ca sparśāḥ savedanakā iti| bhinnalambana yoriti vistaraḥ| bhinnamālambanamanayostau bhinnalambanau| pūrvasya rūpamālambanamuttarasya śabdaḥ| tayorbhinnālambanayoḥ purvasparśahetukā rūpālambanasparśahetukā uttaratra sparśeśabdālambane yad vedanotpadyate ityetadayuktam| kathaṃ hi nāmeti doṣamāviṣkaroti anyajātīyālambanasparśā sambhūtā rūpaprakāralambanāt sparśāt sambhūtā vedanā anyālambanā bhaviṣyati śabdālambanā bhaviṣytati| vidhurakāraṇatvāt kāryasyāsambhāvanā| yadā hi rūpālambanāt sparśāt sambhūtā vedanā rūpālambanena vedanā bhaviṣyati tadā pūrvasparśahetukottaratra sparśe vedaneti yuktam yadā tu rūpālambanāt sparśāt śabdālambanā vedanotpadyate tadā kathanetadyokṣyate| kāranānurūpaṃ hi loke kāryadṛśyate| atha matam rūpālambanāt sparśāt sambhūtā vedanā śabdālamvanasparśa saṃprayuktāpi rūpālambanā bhavatīti | ata idamāha yena vā cittenasamprayuktā tato bhinnālambaneti| tataḥ śabdālambanāccittādbhinnalambanā yata utpannā rūpālambanā tena samālambanetyarthaḥ| kathaṃ cāsau tena samprayuktetyucyate samaṃ prayuktā hi samprayuktā| yadyabhinnāśrayatvāt kathaṃ śrotrāśrayotpannā satī rūpālambanā bhaviṣyati| kathaṃ na bhaviṣyati cittacaittānāmāśrayeṇaikaviṣayapravṛttatvāt| tasmādanyatarālambanatve'pi vedanāyā doṣaḥ| sa doṣaḥ pradarśayate yadi tāvat rūpālambanāt sparśādutpannā vedanā śabdādyālambanāntarabhālanbate| sa rūpālamvanaḥ sparśaḥ sukhavedanīyo yāvat duḥkhasukhavedanīya iti na prapnoti| ekālambanaya tu vedanayā tathā nirdeśo yukta iti| astu tarhīti vistaraḥ| yadyevaṃ dopo'stu tarhi tasmin kāle| kasmin kāle śabdālamvanakāle| sparśabhūtaṃ vigyānamavedanakam| tasmācca  śabdālambanad yat pūrva vijñānaṃ savedanakaṃ tanna sparśaḥ| evaṃ hi nirdoṣaṃ bhavatīti| anyathā hi yadi pūrvotpannaṃ savedanakaṃ vijñānaṃ sparśaḥ syāt tadaivaṃ sparśapratyayā vedaneti yathokto doṣaḥ syāt| kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇam kiñcidvijñānamavedanakam kiñcicca na sparśaḥ pratyayavaidhuryāt| vidhurā hi pratyayā vipratibandhenāvasthitāḥ| na hi sarvaḥ samanantarapratyayo vedanāpratyayaṃ sparaśabhūtaṃ vijñānaṃ janayituṃ samartha iti| tadevaṃ tasya bhinnalāmbanaṃ vijñānamekāntenāvedanakam tadutpādakaṃ cāsparśabhūtam| yadi tu rūpālambanāccakṣurvijñānādanantaraṃ rūpālambanameva cakṣurvijñānamevamutpadyate manovijñānaṃ tat savedanakaṃ purvotpannaṃ sparśa iti| evaṃ satīti| mahābhūmikaniyamo bhidyata iti sparśavedanayoḥ paryāyeṇa niyamāt| tisro bhūmaya iti| savitarkā savicārā kāmadhātuḥ prathamaṃ ca dhyānam| avitarkā vicāramātrā dhyānāntaram| avitarkā avicārā dvitīyāddhayānāt prabhṛti yāvadbhavāgram| kuśalā bhūmiḥ kuślā dharmāḥ| evamakuśalā avyākṛtāḥ| śaikṣī bhūmiḥ śakṣasyānāsravā dharmāḥ| aśaikṣī aśaikṣasyānāsravā dharmāḥ| naivaśaikṣīnāśaikṣī bhūmiḥ sāsravā dharmāḥ| asaṃskṛtā iha nocyante caitasambandhāt| tad ya etasyāmiti vistaraḥ| taditi vākyopanyāse ya iti ye caitasikāḥ| etasyāṃ sarvasyāṃ bhūmāviti savitarkasavicārāṃ bhūmau yāvannaivaśaikṣīnāśaikṣāṃ bhūmau| te mahābhūmikāḥ vedanācetanādayo yathoktāḥ| ye kuśalāyāmeva bhūmau te kuśalamahābhūmikāḥ śraddhādayaḥ| ye kliṣṭāyameva te kleśamahābhūmikā avidyādayo yathoktā eva| te punaryathāsambhavamiti visatraḥ| te punarmahābhūmikādayo ye yasyāṃ sambhavanti te tasyāṃ paryāyena| na tu sarva yugadityapare tad yathā vedanā sarvāsu bhūmiṣu bhavantyapi na yugapat saṃjñācetanāsparśamanaskārādibhirbhavati tathā sparśo vedanādibhiḥ| tathā kuhslāyāṃ vitarkādaya ityevamādikam| apare punarevaṃ vyācakṣate yathā pañcaskandhake likhitaṃ tathadaṃ grahītavyamiti| tadyathā chandaḥ katamaḥ abhipretavastunyabhilāṣaḥ anabhiprete nāsti chanda ityabhiprāyaḥ| adhimokṣaḥ katamaḥ niścite vastuni tathaivāvadhāraṇam| ramṝtiḥ katamā saṃstute vastunyasampramoṣaścetaso'bhilapanatā| samādhiḥ katamaḥ upaparīkṣyate vastuni cittasyaikāgratā| prajñā katamā tatraiva pravicayo yogāyogavihito'nyathā ca| ityevamādiḥ pañcaskandhakagrantho draṣṭavyaḥ| tatra hyukta pañca sarvatragā vedanāsaṃjñāsparśamanaskāracetanāḥ| pañca pratiniyataviṣayāyāśchandādhimokṣasmṛtisamādhiprajñā ityevamādi| akuśalamahābhūmikāstu pāṭhaprasaṅgenāsaṃhitā iti sūtrādiṣu pāṭhāt| kuśalamakuśalavyākṛtamityevaṃ pāṭhaprasaṅgenāsaṃhitāḥ adhyāropitā paścādityarthaḥ| pūrvaṃ na paṭhyante sma prakaraṇapāde mahābhūmikāḥ kuśalamahābhūmikāḥ kteśakuśalamahābhūmikāḥ parīttakteśamahābhūmikāśceti caturvidhāḥ paṭhyante| sahajātā ityucyante na sparśasahajātā iti| aviśeṣitatvādete vedanādayaḥ parasparasahajātāḥ| na tu sparśenatyarthaparigraheṇa parihartavyaṃ sūtramityarthaḥ|  maitrīsahagatamiti ayaṃ sahaśabdo nāvaśyaṃ yugapadbhāve samanantare'pi dṛṣṭaḥ| na hi maitrayāḥ smṛtisambodhyaṅgasya ca samavadhānamasti maitrayā ekāntasāsravatvā smṛtisambodhyaṅgasya caikāntānāsravatvāt| tasmādajñāpakametat| saṃsṛṣṭā iti sahotpannā ityabhiprāyaḥ| tatra hi sūtra hi| yatraivoktam| yā vedāna yā cetanā yā ca saṃjñeti vistareṇa tatra sūtra ityarthaḥ| tanan vijñāyata iti vistaraḥ| kiṃ tavādayameṣāṃ vedanādīnāmālambananiyamaḥ| ālambane niyamaḥ| yadevālambanaṃ vedakṣyate tadeva cetayate yāvadvijñānaṃ vijānātīti| vayaṃ brūmaḥ ālambananiyamo'yamiti| tasmānna sūtravirodha ityabhiprāyaḥ| ācārya āha tatraiva sūtre āyurūṣmaṇoḥ sahabhāvye saṃsṛṣṭavacanāt siddhaḥ kṣaṇaniyama iti| āyrūṣmā ca saṃsṛṣṭau imau dharmau na visaṃsṛṣṭāvityāyurūṣmaṇoḥ sahotpannatoktā bhavati tayoranālambanatvāt| tadevaṃ sādhitaṃ bhavati avaśyaṃ sahotpannāni vedanāsaṃjñācetanāvijñānanai tatra sūtre saṃsṛṣṭāvacanenoktatvāt āyurūṣmavaditi| tat kathamiti vistaraḥ| tat kathaṃ vijñānaṃ cāsti| na ca trayāṇāmindriyaviṣayavijñānānāṃ sannipātaḥ tasmādavaśyaṃ vijñānāstitve sannipāto'bhyupagantavyaḥ| sannipātaśca te sparśe iti tenedaṃ te virūdhyate na sarvavijñānaṃ sparśa iti sannipāto vā na sparśa iti|  
punaḥ sandhikaraṇaṃ cātra draṣṭavyamiti| pūrvatrāsiddhamiti sakāralopasyāsiddhatvāt guṇo na prāpnoti| parihārastu īṣadarthe'yaṃ nañ draṣṭavyaḥ| īṣatsiddhamasiddhamityataḥ siddhatvāt sakāralopasya guṇo bhavati saiṣa daśarathī rāma iti yathā| ṣaṭsaumanasyopavicārā iti vistaraḥ| cakṣuṣā rūpāṇi dṛṣṭā saumanasyasthānīyāni rūpāṇyupavicarati śrotreṇa śabdān śrutvā saumanasyasthānīyān śabdānupavicarati evaṃ yāvanmanasā dharmān vijñāya somanasyasthānīyān dharmān upavicarati evaṃ cakṣuṣā rūpāṇi dṛṣṭā daurmanasyasthānīyānyupekṣāsthānīyāni ceti vistarena yojyam| trayo bhaviṣyantīti saumanasyadaurmanasyopekṣāsvabhāvatraividhyāt| eka iti manovijñānamātrasamprayogāt| paḍiti rūpādiviṣayaṣaṭkabhedāt| tribhirapi sthāpaneti| yadvedanādravyaṃ manovijñānamātrasamprayuktamekam tat saumanasyādisvabhāvatrayabhedātridhā bhidyate| tat punaḥ pratyekaṃ vipayaṣaṭkabhedāt ṣodā bhidyate ityaṣṭādaśa bhavanti svabhāvavyavasthāpanāyāmasatyāṃ saumanasyadaurmanasyopekṣopavicārā iti na syāt| samprayogavyavasthāpanāyāmasatyāṃ manopavocarā ityeva na syādaṣṭādaśavyatiriktāśca syuḥ rūpādiviṣayaṣaṭkālamvanavyavasthāpanāyāmasatyāṃ pratyekaṃ saumanasyādiṣodābhedenāṣṭādaśa manoparicārā iti na syāt| tasmātribhirapi vyavasthāpaneti siddham| asambhinnālambanā iti asammiśrālambanāḥ| trayo dharmopavicārā ubhayatheti ye rūādiviṣayapañcakavyatiriktadharmālambanāste dharmopavicārā asambhinnālambanāḥ| ye tu rūpādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ viṣayāṇāṃ dvau trīn yāvat ṣaḍapi viṣayānālambante te sambhinnālambanāḥ|  
manaḥ kileti| kilaśabdaḥ paramatadyotakaḥ manaḥ kila pratītyāśritya viṣayānupavicarantyālambanta ityarthaḥ upavicārayantīti pravartayanti| kathamityāha vedanāvaśena manaso viṣayeṣu punaḥ punarvicāraṇāditi punaḥpunarityapaśabdasyārtha| nāpyupavicāriketi asantīriketyartha ayoga ityasambhavaḥ| yadi mana evāśritā vedanā manopavicāraḥ tṛtīye dhyāne yat sukhaṃ tanmana evāśritam| na cakṣurindriyādyāśritamiti tasya kasmānmanopa vicāreṣvagrahaṇam āditaḥ kileti vsitāḥ ādāvāditaḥ kileti paramate kāmadhātau manobhūmikaṃ sukhaṃ nāsti tasmānna tanmanopavicāraḥ| ataḥ paścādapi tṛtīye dhyāne sukhendriyasya mana āśritatve'pi manopavicāreṣvagrahaṇam| yathā cāsyopavicārasya pratidvandvena darumanasyopavicāraḥ naivaṃ sukhopavicārasya pratidvandvena duḥkhopavicāro'sti manovijñānāśritvādduḥkhasya| ataḥ sarvatra tat sukhaṃ na manopavicāra iti|  
saumanasyasthānīyānīti saumanasyajanakāni| pañceti vistaraḥ| pañcabhirvijñānakāyairabhinirhṛtatvamutpāditatvamabhisandhāya cetasikṛtvā etadukta cakṣuṣā rūpāṇīti vistareṇa mano bhūmikāstvete tad yathā aśubhā ca samāhitatvāt api tviti cakṣuṣā rūpāṇi dṛṣṭā yāvat kāyena spraṣṭavyāni spṛṣṭeti vacanādacodyametat yadi cakṣuṣā rūpāṇi paśyan saumanasyasthānīyāni rūpāṇyupavicaratītyukta syāt yāvat kāyena spraśṭavyāni spṛśanniti| syāccodyam na tvevamuktamityacodyamevaitat| yo'pyadṛṣṭeti vistaraḥ| yo'pi śrutvā parato manasopavicaratyālambate te'pi tasya manopavicārāḥ| itarathā hīti vistaraḥ| rūpadhātvālambanā rūpādyupavicārā na syuḥ| kāmadhātūpapannena rūpāvacarāṇāṃ rūpādīnāmapratyakṣīkṛtatvāt| kāmadhātvālambanāśca gandharasaspraṣṭavyopavicārā rūpadhātūpapannasya na syuḥ| tasya gandhādīnāma viṣayatvāt| yathā tu vyaktataraṃ tathoktamiti pratyakṣīkṝtarūpādyālambānānāṃ manoparicārāṇāṃ vyaktataratvāt| yo'pi rūpāṇi dṛṣṭā tatsahacarāñchabdā nupavicarati so'pi tasya manopavicāraḥ yathā tvanākulaṃ tathoktam| katha cānākulamityāha indriyārthavyavacchedata iti| cakṣurādīndriyavyavacchedena rūpādyarthavyavacchedena coktamiti| anyathā hyākulaṃ syāt| cakṣuṣā rūpāṇi dṛṣṭā saumanasyasthānīyāñ chabdānupavicaratītyevamādi| asti santānaṃ niyamyeti kasyacit sattvasya santānamavekṣya| yadasya kiñcit saumanasyasthānīyameva yāvadupekṣāsthānīyameva| na tvālambanam| kim niyamya| kim kāraṇam tadeva hyālambanam kasyacit saumanasyasthānīyam kasyacinneti|| 32||  
[ kāme svālambanāḥ sarve rūpī dvādaśagocaraḥ|  
trayāṇāmuttaro dhyānadvaye dvādaśa kāmagāḥ|| 33||]

evaṃ yāvadārūpyapratisaṃyuktā iti| kati rūpapratisaṃyuktāḥ teṣāṃ ca kati rūpālambanāḥ katyārūpyapratisaṃyuktāsteṣāṃ ca kati kimālambanā iti|  
kāme svālambanāḥ sarve

iti kāme kāmāvacarā manopavicorāḥ| sarve'ṣṭādaśa santi| sa eva ca kāmadhātusteṣāṃ sarveṣāmālambanam| svo dhāturālambanameṣāmiti svālambanā iti kārikāpadavigrahaḥ|  
rūpī dvādaśagocaraḥ

iti rūpī dhātuḥ kāmāvacarāṇāṃ dvādaśātāṃ manopavicārāṇāṃ gocaraḥ| ṣaḍgandharasopavicārānapāsyeti| dvau gandharasāvālambanau saumanasyopavicārau hitvā dvau gandharasālambanau daurmanasyopavicārau hitvā dvau tadālambanāveva copekṣopavicārau hitveti| kasmāt tatra rūpadhātau tayorgandharasayorabhāvāt|  
vinā gandharasaghrāṇajihvāvijñānadhātubhiḥ

iti vacanāt|  
trayāṇāmuttaraḥ

iti trayāṇāṃ saumanasyadaurmanasyopekṣādharmopavicārāṇāṃ kāmāvacarāṇāmuttaro dhāturārūpyadhāturālambanam tatra rūpādyabhāvāt|  
dhyānadvaye dvādaśa

iti| daurmanasyopavicārāṇāṃ paṇṇāmabhāvāt|| 33 ||  
[ svo'pṭālamvanamārūpyaṃ dvayordhyānadvaye tu ṣaṭ|  
kāmaḥ ṣaṇṇāṃ caturṇāṃ sva ekasyālambanaṃ paraḥ|| 34|| ]

svo'ṣṭālambanañ

iti| aṣṭānāmālambanamaṣṭālamvanam| gandharasopavicārāṃścaturo hitveti| saumanasyopekṣopavicārau gandhālambanau dvau rasālambanau ca dvau hitvetyarthaḥ tatra tayorabhāvāt|  
ārūpya dvayoḥ

iti| saumanasyokṣādharmopavicārayoḥ| prathamadvitīyadhyānabhūmikayorārūpya dhāturālamvanam rūpādyabhāvāt| ṣaḍupekṣopavicārā eva santi nānya iti| rūpaśabdagandharasaspraṣṭavyadharmālambanā upekṣopavicārā eva santi| saumanasyopavicārāṇāmapi tṛtīye caturthe cābhāvāt| gandharasālambanau tu tatra kāmāvacaragandharasālambanāvityavagantavyam|| 34||

[ catvāro'rūpisāmante rūpagā eka ūrdhvagaḥ|  
eko maule svaviṣayaḥ sarve'ṣṭādaśa sāsravāḥ|| 35||  
tatra catvāra iti| ākāśānantyāyatanasāmantakānantaryamārgasya caturthadhyānālamvanatvāt| tatra ca rūpaśabdaspraṣṭavyadharmasadbhāvāt| yeṣāṃ tadvayavacchinnatā lambanamastīti| yeṣāmācāryāṇāṃ matena tadākāśanantyāyatanasāmantakaṃ vyavacchinnālambanaṃ pṛthak pṛthagrapādyālamvanamasamastālambanamityarthaḥ| audārīkādibhirākāraistaccaturthadhyānamālambata ityekeṣāmācāryāṇāṃ matam| yeṣāmācāryāṇāṃ matena paripiṇḍitālambanameva tadākaśānantyāyatanasāmantakaṃ caturthdhyānabhūmikaskandhapañcakālambanamiti matam teṣāmācāryāṇāṃ tatraika eva sammiśrālambanpo dharmopavicāra iti|  
eka ūrdhvagaḥ

iti keṣāṃ pakṣe yeṣām

catvāro'rūpisāmante

iti pakṣaḥ| na hi molānāmiti vistaraḥ| maulānāmārūpyāṇāmadharo dhāturnālanbanamadharabhūmikaṃ sāsravaṃ vastu nālambanamityarthaḥ| paścāt pravedayiṣyāmīti|  
na maulāḥ kuśalārūpyāḥ sāsravādharagocarāḥ

iti|  
prathameti vistaraḥ| prathamadvitīyadhyānabhūmikairaṣṭābhiḥ samanvāgatastṛtīyacaturthadhyānabhūmikaścaiturbhiḥ| katamairityāha| kliṣṭaiḥ gandharasālambanāt paryudasya tatra gandharasābhāvāt| katham prathamadvitīyayordhānayordvādaśopavicārāḥ santītyuktam| saumanasyopekṣopavicārāṇām

ṣaḍviṣayālambanavyavasthāpanāt| tatra svabhūmyālambanā rūpaśabdaspraṣṭavyadharmālambanāḥ saumanasyopavicārāścatvāraḥ kliṣṭāḥ| ye copekṣopavicārāḥ kliṣṭā eva catvāraḥ taiḥ kāmadhātūpapanno rūpāvacarakuśalālābhī samanvāgataḥ| adharabhūmyupapanno hyūrdhvabhūmikaiḥ kliṣṭaiḥ samanvāgato bhavati na tu viśiṣṭaiścaturbhigandharasālambanaiḥ teṣāmakliṣṭatvāt rūpāvacarakuśalālābhitvācca| nāstyakliṣṭarūpāvacaradharmalābha iti na taiḥ samanvāgataḥ| tṛtīyacaturthadhyānabhūmikā api ṣaḍevopekṣapavicārā uktāḥ  teṣāṃ ye svabhūmyālambanāḥ kliṣṭā taiḥ samanvāgat stathā yau tu gandharasālamabanau na tau kliṣṭau| ataḥ pūrvoktenaiva nyāyena na tābhyāṃ samanvāgataḥ| kliṣṭenaiveti nākliṣṭena kuśalasyābhāvāt| lābhī rūpāvacaṃrasyeti vistaraḥ| kāmadhātūpapanna eva lābhī rūpabacarasya kuśalasya cittasyānāgamyasaṃgṛhītasyāvītarāgaḥ kāmadhātoḥ sarvai kāmāvacarairaṣṭādaśabhirapi samanvāgataḥ| prathamadhyānabhūmikairdaśabhiriti| kathaṃ daśabhirityāha saumanasyopavicāraiḥ kliṣṭairgandharasālambanāvāpāsyeti saumanasyopavicārāḥ sāmāntakeṣu na bhavanti sāmāntakānāmupekṣendriyasamprayogitvāt| ato maulasaṃgṛhītaireva saumanasyopavicāraiḥ kliṣṭaiḥ svabhūmyālambanaiścaturbhiḥ samanvāgataḥ na tu gandharasālambanābhyāṃ maulakuśalālābhenākliṣṭalābhābhāvāt| na hi kliṣṭa dhayānamadharālambanamasti| ṣaḍbhirūpekṣopavicārairanāgamyabhūmikairiti| atrāviśeṣābhidhānādupekṣopavicāraiḥ kliṣṭairapi caturbhiḥ samanvāgataḥ sāmantakasaṃgṛhītaiḥ ṣaḍbhiścopekṣopavicāraiḥ kuśalaiḥ kāmadhātvālamvanaiḥ svabhūmyālambanairapi| dvitīyatṛtīyacaturthadhyānārūpyajaiḥ pūrvavaditi| katham| sa eva kāmadhātūpapanno lābhī rūpāvacarasya kuśalasya cittasyāvītarāgo dvitīyadhyānabhūmikairaṣṭābhiryarevālābhī gandharasālambanānāpāsya| tṛtīyacaturthadhyānabhūmiścaiturbhiḥ kliṣṭaireva gandharasālambanau paryudasyārūpyā vacareṇaikena kliṣṭenaiva| anyā vartanyā mārgeṇa śeṣamanugantavyam| katham prathamadhyānopapanno dvitīyadhyānabhūmikasya kuśalasya cittasyālābhī prathamadhyānabhūmikaiḥ sarvaiḥ samanvāgataḥ| dvitīyadhyānabhūmikasya kuśalacittasya tadbhamyavītarāgaḥ sarvaiḥ prathamadhyānabhūmikaiḥ dvitīyadhyānabhūmikairaṣṭābhiḥ| caturbhiḥ saumanasyopavicāraiḥ kliṣṭaiḥ gandharasālambanāvapāsya| caturbhiścopekṣopavicārairdvitīyadhyānasāmantakasaṃgṛhītaḥ| tṛtīyadhyānanbhūmikasihcaturbhiḥ kliṣṭaiḥ gandharasālambanau paryudasya| evaṃ caturthadhyānabhūmikarārūpyavācareṇaikena kliṣṭenaiva| dvitīyadhyānopapannastṛtīyadhyānabhūmikasya kuśalasya cittasyālābhī dvitīyadhyānabhūmikaiḥ sarvaiḥ samanvāgataḥ tṛtīyadhyānabhūmikasihcaturbhiḥ kliṣṭaiḥ| caturthadhyānārūpyajaiḥ pūrvavat| lābhī tṛtīyadhyānabhūmikasya kuśalascittasya tadbhūmyavotarāgaḥ sarvaidvitīyadhyānabhūmikaiḥ| tṛtīyadhyānabhūmikaiścaturbhirūpekṣipavicāraiḥ kliṣtairgandharasālambanavapāsya| caturbhiścopekṣopavicāraistṛtīyadhyānasāmantakasaṃgṛhītaiścaturthadhāyānarūpyajaiḥ pūrvavat| eṣā dik| anayā diśā disahā tṛtīyā diṣvapi dhyāneṣūpapannasya yojyaḥ| upekṣādharmopavicāreṇeti kāmāvacarasya nirmāṇacittasyopekṣāsamprayogitvāt|  
apara āheti ayameva śāstrakāraḥ| na hi yo yasmāditi vistaraḥ| yaḥ pudgalaḥ yasmādrūpādervītarāgo na sa tadrūpādyālambanamupavicarati| kathamupavicarati tatrānunīyate vā pratihanyate vā apratisaṃkhyāya vopekṣata ityevam| yadā pratisaṃkhyāyopekṣate nābhujati tadākuśalatvam ato'pratisaṃkhyāyeti viśeṣaṇam| ataḥ sāsravā api na sarve kiṃ tarhi sāṃkleśikāḥ| saṃkleśe bhavāḥ sāṃkleśitāḥ saṃkleśānukūlāḥ yaiḥ saumanasyādibhirmano viṣayānuvicarati| yeṣāṃ pratipakṣeṇa ṣaṭ sātatā vihārā iti| yeṣāṃ sāṃkleśikānāṃ prativyūhena satataṃ bhavāḥ sātatā vihārā yogaviśeṣā bhavanti te ca ṣaṭ| kathamityāha cakṣuṣā rūpāṇi dṛṣṭa| naiva samunā bhavati nānunīyate| na durmanā na pratihanyate| upekṣako bhavati nābhujati| kathaṃ nābhujati kimapratisaṃkhyāyāhosvit pratisaṃkhyāyeti| viśeṣayannāha smṛtimān samprajānānniti smṛtisamprayuktayā prajñayā pratisamīkṣyamāṇa ityarthaḥ| evaṃ yāvanmanasā dharmān vijñayeti| viṣayaṣaṭkabhedāt ṣaṭ sātatā iti| na hyarhata iti vistaraḥ| arhato laukikaṃ kuśalaṃ sāsravaṃ dharmālambanaṃ dharmāyatanālambanamadhigamadharmālambanaṃ vā saumanasyamasti tasya hi buddhasān tānikān guṇān sammukhīkurvataḥ| kuśalaṃ saumanasyamutpadyate| na tasyaiva pratiṣedho yujyate| kasya tarhi yattu tat sāṃkleśikaṃ manasa upavicārabhūtam| tasyaiṣa pratiṣedho lakṣyate| tasmāt sāsravā api na sarve saumanasyādaya upavicārā iti| atra bhadantānantavarmāha ayuktametat kasmāt sūtre'nyathā nirdeśāt| sūtre hi bhagavatā na sāṃkleśikā evopavicārā uktāḥ| evaṃ hyāha tatra bhikṣavo ya ime ṣaṭ saumanasyopavicārāstānāśritya tān pratiṣṭhāya ya ime ṣaṭ saumanasyopavicārāstān prajahīta tatra bhikṣavo ya ime ṣaḍupekṣopavicārāstānāśritya tān pratiṣṭhāya ya ime ṣaḍupekṣopavicārāstānāśritya tān pratiṣṭhāya ya ime ṣaḍupekṣopavicārāstān prajahīta| dve bhikṣavo upekṣe nānātvasanniśritā ekatvasanniśritā ca| tatra bhikṣavo yeyamekatvasanniśritopekṣā tāmāgamya tāmadhiṣṭhāya yeyaṃ nānātvasanniśritopekṣā tāṃ prajahīteti| etasmāt sūtrājajñāyate na sāṃkleśikā eva saumanasyādaya upavicārā iti| tadetajjñāpakam prahāṇavacane'pi sāṃkleśikatvāvinivṛtteḥ| na hītareṇetarasya sanniśrayaprahāṇavacane'pi satyeṣāṃ manopavicārānāṃ sāṃkleśikatvaṃ vinivartate anyathāsambhavāt| evamāha mānaṃ niśritya māna eva prahātavya iti| na tu jātu kvacinmāno vyavadānikaḥ sambhavati| tasmāt kleśānāṃ parasparavirodhena vṛttervṝttyantarasya cāpekṣayā guṇavattvāt gurūlāghavabhisandhāya sūtre vacanādadoṣaḥ| nandādayo'tra nidarśanam| ataścaitadevaṃ yadupekṣopavicārāṇāmupekṣopavicārāneva prahāṇāya sanniśrayatvena dideśa bhagavān dve bhikṣava upekṣe ityādivacanāt| nanu ca gardhāśritanaiṣkramyāśritabhedena ṣaṭtriṃśacchāstṛpadānīti vacanāt  siddhameṣāṃ sāṃkleśikavyavadānikatvaṃ vaibhāṣikamatānusārāt| adoṣaḥ ācāryasyāpi manopavicāravyatiriktāstatra saumanasyādayaḥ santi| punasta eveti vistaraḥ| ta evāṣṭādaśopavicārāḥ kecid bhavāśritā abhiṣvaṅgāśritāḥ kecinnaiṣkramyāśritāḥ| naiṣkramyaṃ niṣkramabhavāḥ saṃkleśāt saṃsāradūhkhādvā| gardhanaiṣkramyāśritabhedena dvāvaṣṭādaśakau ṣaṭtriṃśacchastṛpadāni| tadbhedasya teṣāṃ manopavicārāṇāṃ dvudhābhedasya śāstrā buddhena gamitatvāddeśitatvād yathāsūtram|| 35||  
[ ukta ca vakṣyate cānyadatra tu kleśa iṣyate|  
bījavannagavanmūlavṛkṣavatta ṣavattathā||  36||  
tuṣitaṇḍulavat karma tathaivauṣadhipuṣpavat|  
siddhānnapānavadvastu tasmin bhavacatuṣṭaye|| 37||  
upapattibhavaḥ kliṣṭaḥ sarvakleśauḥ svabhūmikaḥ|  
tridhānye traya ārūpyeṣvāhārāsthitikaṃ jagat|| 38|| ]

kleśakarmavastūnīti prajñāpitamiti|  
kleśāstrīṇi dvayaṃ karma sapta vastu

iti vacānāt|  
evaṃ karmāṇi vipākotpattāviti| āsanna kāraṇamityadhikṛtam| kleśo'pi hi kāraṇaṃ vipākasya tattu virakṛṣṭam|  
te ca vyākhyātā iti|  
mṛtyūpapattibhavayorantarābhavatīha yaḥ

sa punarmaṇṇāt pūrvamupapattikṣaṇāt paraḥ

iti vacanādvayākhyātāḥ| na paryavasthānaiḥ svatantrairiti īrṣāmātsaryakodhamrakṣaiḥ svātantrayameṣām| avidyānirmuktairanyakleśairasamgrayogāt| avidyā hi sarvatraiva vidyata iti na tatsaṃprayogenāsvātantrayam 'hrīkyādibhistvanyakleśasamprayuktaiḥ pratisandhibandho na pratiṣidhyate| yadyapi sāvasthā mandiketi vistaraḥ| yadyapi sā maraṇāvasthā mandikā cittacaittasamudācārasyāpaṭutvāt|  
upekṣāyāṃ cyutodbhavau

iti vedanāmāndyāt| yastu pudgalo yatra kleśe'bhīkṣnaṃ satataṃ caritaḥ pravṛttaḥ āsannaśca maraṇakālasya pudgalasya tadānīṃ maraṇakāle sa eva kleśaḥ samudācarati samutthāya mithyādṛṣṭyādirapi pūrvavedhāt pūrvākṣepāt pūrvābhyāsādityarthaḥ| athavā yo'sau tadānīṃ kteśa upatiṣṭhate tasya pūrvaṃ phalabhāvena parigrahāvedhāt ākśepāt sa tadānīmupatiṣṭhata iti| kāmarūpadhātvoraparisaṃkhyānāt sarva eva catvāro bhavāḥ santīti| traya evārūpyeṣu ārūpyeṣveva ca traya ityavadhāraṇāt| anayoritarayordhātvorbhavacatuṣṭayaṃ tu jñātaṃ bhavati| niṣyandābhāvāditi mūtrapurīṣābhāvādityarthaḥ| sūkṣmāṇāṃ vā sūkṣmā iti| sūkṣmāṇāṃ sa sūkṣmāhāraḥ| bālakā jātamātrāḥ| svedajantukādayo yūkādayaḥ ādiśabdena garbhasthāḥ|| 36-38||  
[ kavaḍīkāra āhāraḥ kāme trayāyatanātmakaḥ|  
na rūpāyatanaṃ tena svākṣamuktānanugrahāt|| 39|| ]

lavaḍīkarāhārāḥ

kāme iti| kāmadhātāveva kavaḍīkārāhāra ityavadhāraṇam| kasmādityāha tadvītarāgāṇāṃ kavaḍīkārāhāravītarāgāṇām| tatra rūpārūpyadhātvorūpapatteḥ| kavaḍīkṛyābhyavaharaṇāt piṇḍīkṛtya gilān kavaḍīkāraḥ kavaḍīkṛtyābhyavaharaṇaṃ punarmukhena nāsikayā grāsavyavacchedāditi| gandharasaspraṣṭavyāyatanāni sarvāṇyeva kavaḍīkāra ityukte pṛcchati| cchāyātapajvālāprabahasu teṣāṃ gandhādīnāṃ kathamāhāratvam| na hi tatratyānāṃ gandhādīnāṃ kavaḍīkāratvamiti pratipadyate| bāhulyena kilaiṣa nirdeśa iti| na tatratyānāṃ gandhādīnāṃ kavaḍīkāratvamiti pratipadyate| bāhulikastu nirdeśaḥ

kāme tryāyatanātmakaḥ

iti| kila śabdaḥ paramatena svābhiprāyo'dhikṛyate| yānyapi tviti vistaraḥ| yānyapi tu nābhyavahiyante chāyādiṣu sthitiṃ cāharanti yāpanāṃ cāharanti tānyapi sūkṣma āhāraḥ snānābhyaṅgavat| yathā sthānaṃ cābhyaṅgaśca na kavaḍīkṛtyābhyavahiyate āhāraśca tadvaditi|  
svākṣamuktānanugrahāt

iti| svākṣasya svendriyasya tenānugrahāt muktānāñca kāmadhātoranāgāmyarhatāmananugrahāt| na rūpāyatanamahārāḥ| rūpāyatanaṃ cābhyavahārakāla iti| yasmin kāle antarmukhapraviṣta āhāro bhujyate cakṣurviṣāyātītaḥ tasmin kāle| svamindriyaṃ cakṣurindriyam tanmahābhūtāni ca tasyendriyasya yāni mahābhūtānyāśrayastāni nānugṛhṇāti tadrūpāyatanam| kuta evānyānīndriyāṇi śrotrādīnyanugrahīṣyati aviṣayatvāt| aviṣayo hi śrotrādīnāṃ rūpāyatanam| dṛśyamāna tarhi bhojanakāle'nugrahīṣyatītyata āha| yadāpi ca dṛśyamāna iti vistaraḥ| atha kathamidaṃ gamyate| tadāpi tadālambanaḥ sukhavedanīyaḥ sparśa āhāro na rūpamiti| ata āha muktānāmiti vistaraḥ| yadi taddṛśyamānamāhārakṛtyaṃ kuryāt| muktānāmanāgābhyarhatāṃ kavaḍikārāhāravītarāgāṇāṃ sumanojñamapyāhāraṃ paśyatāpnugrahaṃ kuryāt| yathā gandharasaspraṣṭavyāyatanānīndriyamahābhūtānāmanugrahaṃ kurvantyabhyavahārākāle tasmāttadālambanaḥ sukhavedanīyaḥ sparśa āhāro bhavatīti siddham|| 39||  
[ sparśasañcetanāvijñā āhārāḥ sāsravāstriṣu|  
manomayaḥ sambhavaiṣī gandharvaścāntarābhavaḥ|| 40||  
nirvṛttiratra vṛddhayarthamāśrayāśritayordvayam|  
dvayamanyabhavākṣepanivṛttyartha yathākramam|| 41|| ]

sparśasañcetanāvijñā

iti| vijñetyādantametat prajñeti yathā vijñānamityarthaḥ| kavaḍīkārāhārastrayāyatanātmakatvāt sāsrava iti siddhaḥ| trayastu sparśādayaḥ sāsravāḥ anāsravāśca sambhavantītyatasta eva viśeṣitāḥ|  
sāsravāḥ

iti| na caivamanāsravā dharmā iti| na caivaṃ te bhūtānāṃ sthitaye yāpanāyai sambhavaiṣiṇāṃ cānugrahāyeti| manonirjātatvāditi|  
nirvṛttiḥ

iti| upapattyabhimukhāvadhāraṇam| ata eva vratīti śukraśoṇitādikaṃ kiñcid bāhyamanupādāya bhāvāditi| ādiśabdena kardamapuṣpādīnāṃ grahaṇam| upapattyabhimukhatvādabhinirvṛttiriti| upapattyabhimukhī nirvṛttirjanmāsyetyabhinivṛttiḥ| savyāvādhamiti saduḥkham avyāvādhamabhinirvartyati| idamatra jñāpakam bhavo'bhinirvṛttiriti| tathāsti pudgala iti vistaraḥ| asmāt sūtrāccatuṣkhotikādabhinirvṛttirantarābhava iti asti pudgalo yasyābhinirvṛttisaṃyojanaṃ prahīṇaṃ nopapattisaṃyojanam| asti yasyopapattisaṃyojanaṃ prahīṇaṃ nābhinirvṝttisaṃyojanam| asti yasyābhinirvṛttisaṃyojanaṃ prahīṇaṃ nābhinirvṛttisaṃyojanam| asti yasyābhinirvṛttisaṃyojanaṃ ca prahīṇamupapattisaṃyojanaṃ ca| asti yasya navābhinirvṛttisaṃyojanaṃ prahīṇaṃ nopapattisaṃyojanam| dvidhātuvītarāgasyeti kāmarūpadhātuvītarāgasyeti| anāgāmina iti viśeṣaṇaṃ pṛthagjanānāmantarābhavasaṃyojanasyātyantāprahāṇāt taddhi bhikṣavaḥ prahīṇa yadāryathā prajñayā rahīṇamiti vacanāt tasya ca punarbhāvitvāt| tatra prathamā koṭirdvidhātuvītarāgasyānāgāminaḥ dvitīyāntarāparinirvāyiṇaḥ tṛtīyārhataḥ caturthī tadanyeṣāmasmādādeḥ pṛthagjanasyāraysa cāvītarāgasya kāmadhātuvītarāgasya rūpadhātāvupapadyamānasya ceti| bhūtā vārhanta iti pūrvamevoktaḥ paryāyaḥ| bhūtā utpannāḥ sambhavaiṣḥiṇo'ntarābhavikā iti| idānīmayamaparaḥ paryāyaḥ| bhūtā arhantastraidhātukavītarāgatvāt sambhavaiṣiṇaḥ satṛṣnāḥ traidhātuke sambhavaiṣitvāt| sthitaya ityavasthāpanāya| anugrahāyeti punarbhavāya sambhavāyetyarthaḥ|  
sarve'pyubhayatheti| sarve'pi catvāra āhārā bhūtānāṃ sattvānāṃ sthitaye'nugrahāya  ca| tathā sambhavaiṣiṇāmiti| yukta tāvanmanaḥsañcetanā karmasvabhāvatvāt| tatprabhāvitaṃ ca vijñānaṃ bījam sparśo'pi tatsamprayukto bhavatyanugrāhayeti| kavaḍīkārastu kathaṃ sambhavaiṣiṇāmanugrahāyeti ata ucyate| kavaḍīkāro'pi hīti vistaraḥ| tadrāgiṇāmiti kavaḍīkārarāgiṇām| punarbhavāya saṃvartata iti asya jñāpanārthamidaṃ sūtrapadamupanyasyate| uktaṃ hi bhagavateti vistaraḥ| tatra rogādibhiḥ pañca upādānaskandaha uktāḥ| tasya rogasya catvāra ahārā mūlam mūlaṃ ca heturityanena kavaḍīkāro'pi tadrāgiṇā punarbhavāya saṃvatata iti siddham| anye pinajarāmaranasya pratyaya ityetadevodāharaṇaṃ bruvate asmājjanamnaḥ pareṇa jarāmaraṇaṃ vyavasthāpitamityabhiprāyaḥ| kavaḍīkāro'pi bhūtānāṃ sattvānāmiha sthitaya iti yukta pratyakṣatvāt tathā sukhavedanīyaḥ sparśo vijñānaṃ cānugrāhakmityuktam| atha manaḥ sañcetanā kathamiti ata idamucyate| manaḥsañcetanāpi ceti vistaraḥ| ata eva manaḥsañcetanāgrahaṇena sparśavijñāne api gṛhīte bhavataḥ tatsamprayogitvāt| pradratā iti prasṛtāḥ| saṅgītiparyāya ityabhidharmaśāstre teṣāṃ puṣṭaye sparśa iti sukhavedanīyenānugrahāt yaḥ kaścid vedanāskandhaḥ saṃjñākasnahaḥ sarvaḥ sa sparśa pratītyeti vacanat| manaḥsañcetānāyāḥ punarbhavasya ākṣepa iti tasyāḥ karmasvabhāvatvāt | kathañca punastayoḥ punarbhava ākṣipyate| tadutpattipratyayaparasparānuykūlyāvasthānāt| karmaparibhāvitādvijñānabījādibhinirvṛttiriti bījādivāṅkurasya punarbhavasyotpāda ityarthaḥ|  
yaḥ kavaḍīkāraḥ sarvaḥ sa āhāra iti praśne āha| syāt kavaḍīkāro nāhāraḥ syādāhāro na kavaḍīkāraḥ syādubhaya syānnobhayam iti catuṣkoṭikam dvitīyā koṭistraya āhārā iti manaḥ sañcetanāsparśavijñānāni| caturthyetānākarāt sthāpayitveti śabdo duḥkhavedanāsamprayuktāśca sarve cittacaittāḥ| evaṃ sparśādibhirapi yathāyogaṃ catuṣkoṭikāni kartavyānīti| yaḥ kaścit sparśaḥ sarvaḥ sa āhāraḥ| syāt sparśo nāhāra iti catuṣṭokiṭikam| evaṃ yā kācinmanaḥsañcetanā sarvā sā āhāraḥ| syānmanaḥ sacetanā nāhāra iti catuṣkoṭikam| tathā yat kiñcidvijñānaṃ sarvaṃ tadāhāraḥ| syādvijñānaṃ nāhara iti catuṣkoṭikam| sparśasya tāvat prathamā koṭiryaṃ sparśaṃ pratītyendriyāṇāmupacayo bhavati mahābhūtānāṃ ca paribhedaḥ| dvitīyā traya āhārāḥ tṛtīyāṃ yaṃ sparśa pratītyendriyāṇāmapacayo bhavati mahābhūtānāñca vṛddhiḥ| caturthyetānākārān sthāpayitveti evaṃ yāvadvijñānaṃ catuṣkoṭikam| prathamā koṭiyadvijñānaṃ pratītyendriyāṇāmapacayo bhavati mahābhūtānāṃ ca vṛddhiḥ| caturthyetānākārān sthāpayitvā| syāt sparśadīna pratītyeti vistareṇāparaḥ praśnaḥ| sparśadīniti sparśacetanāvijñānadharmānityevaṃ netavyam| evaṃ ca granthaḥ kartavyaḥ syāt sparśādīn pratītyendriyāṇāmupacayo bhavati mahābhūtānāṃ ca vṛddhiḥ na ca te āhārāḥ| syāt anyabhūmikānanāsravāṃśca| pratītyetyabhisaṃbandhād dvitīyāprayogaḥ| yo'pi hi paribhukta iti vistaraḥ| kena sambandhenedamuktam| anyanbhūmikānāsravāṃśca pratītyānāhāra ityukte'rthādetadukta bhavati khabhūmikām sāsravān pratītyāhāra iti yaṃ cehā paribhukta iti kavaḍīkāro bhokturvādhāmādadhāti| sa kimāhāraḥ so'pyāhāraḥ | āpatebhojanavelāyāmanugrahāt| sarvāsu sarvaḥ evaṃ yoniṣviti| kāmadhātau pañcānāṃ gatīnāṃ catasṛṇāṃ ca yonīnāṃ sadbhāvāt| catuṣkoṭikaṃ bādhyate prakaraṇagranthaśceti| prathame catuṣkoṭike yeyaṃ tṛtīyā koṭiḥ| yaṃ kavaḍīkāraṃ pratītyendriyāṇāmupacayo bhavati mahābhūtānāṃ ca vṛddhirityasyā bādhāccatuṣkoṭikaṃ bādhyate| na kevalamupacakaraḥ| kiṃ tarhi apacarakaro'pi kavaḍīkārāhāra iti| prakaraṇagranthaśca kiṃ bādhyate kīdṛśaḥ prakaraṇagrantha iti taddarśayannāha| kavaḍīkārāhāraḥ katamaḥ yaṃ kavaḍīkāraṃ pratītyendriyāṇāmupacayo bhavati vistareṇa yāvadvijñānamiti upacayāhārābhisandhivacanādavirodhaiti| upacayāhārābhiprāyeṇa catuṣkoṭivacanaṃ prakaraṇagranthavacanaṃ ca tasmāttayoravirodhaḥ| yo hyupacayāya sa mukhyaḥ| āhāralakṣaṇaprāptatvāditi| kavaḍīkārāhāralakṣaṇaprāptatvāt| kathamityāha so'pi hi jighatsāṃ pipāsāṃ ca pratihantuṃ samartha iti| pradīptāyaṣpiṇḍalakṣaṇa āhāro jighatsāpratighāte samarthaḥ kvathitatāmralakṣaṇaḥ pipāsāpratighāte iti yojyam| kavaḍīkārāhāraprasaṅgenedamucyate uktaṃ bhagavatā yaśca bahyakānāmiti vistaraḥ | jambudvīpo jambuṣaṇḍaḥ| tathā hyakta bhagavatā yāḥ kāścit jambuṣaṇḍāt sravantyaḥ sarvāḥ samudranimnāḥ samudrapravaṇāḥ samudraprāgbhārāti tannivāsino jambuṣaṇḍagatāḥ kukṣimanto bhojanaśaktayupetā akalalādyavasthā ityabhiprāyaḥ| tadetadayuktamekamiti vacanāditi| yadi jambudvīpanivāsinaḥ kukṣimanto jambuṣaṇḍagatā iṣyeran| yaśca jambuṣaṇḍagatān pṛthag janāniti vaktavyaṃ syāt ukta ca yaścaikaṃ jambuṣaṇḍagatamiti| tasmādayamartho na ghaṭate| kaścātra viśeṣa iti kimatrāścarya yadvahūn bhojayitvā bahu puṇyaṃ syāt na tvalpīyasaḥ śatamātrān vītarāgānityarthaḥ sannikṛṣṭo bodhisattva iti āsannābhisambodhiḥ|  
bpdhisattvaḥ kuto yāvadyato lakṣaṇakarmakṛt

ityabhiprāyaḥ| na tviyamanvarthā saṃjñeti nārthānugatā nārthavācakaṃ padamityarthaḥ| nāpi paribhāṣitā arthamanapekṣya| bodhisattva eva tveṣa jambuṣaṇḍagata iti kṛṣigrāmakaṃ vyalokanāya nirgataḥ sarvārthasiddho bodhisattvo jambuvṛkṣamūle niṣannaḥ prathamaṃ dhyānamutpādtavān| ata āha sa hi pṛthagjanaḥ kāmavairāgyasambandheneti| sa cāpi hi bodhistatvaḥ pṛthagjanaḥ kāmavītarāgaśca bāhyakāśca tathaiva kāmavītarāgāḥ pṛthag janāścānena sambandhena tebhyo bāhyakebhyo bahutareṇa dānaphalena viśiṣyamāṇa ityevamuktaḥ| sa tebhyo bāhyakebhyo'nantebhyo viśiṣyamāṇo

bodhisattvāya  cāmeyā anāryebhyo'pi dakṣiṇā

iti vacanāt| śatagrahaṇaṃ tu pūrvādhikārāditi tatraiva sūtre śatādhikāro'dhikṛtaḥ| yaśca tiryagayonigatānāṃ śataya dānaṃ dadyāt yaścaikasmai duḥśīlāya manuṣyabhūtāya dānaṃ dadyāt tataḥ śataguṇo vipākaḥ pratikāṅikṣatavyaḥ| yāvad yaśca bāhyakānāmiti vistaraḥ| itthaṃ caitadevamiti itthaṃ caitadevaṃ bodhisattava eva tveṣa jambuṣaṇḍe niṣaṇṇo yujyata iti  yadena bodhisattvamapāsya bāhyakebhya eva srotāpattiphalapratipannakaṃ viśeṣayāmbabhūveti| bāhyakebhyo vātarāgebhyaḥ srota āpattiphalapratipannakāyeti vistaraḥ| yadi nirvedhabhāgī jambuṣaṇḍagato'bhaviṣyadjambuṣaḍaṇgātādeva vyaśeṣayiṣyat katham yaśca jambuṣaṇḍagatānataratvādaṃ śataṃ bhohayedayścaikaṃ strota āpattiphalasākṣātkriyāyai pratipannam ato dānādidaṃ mahāphalataramiti | na tvevam| srota āpattiphalapratipannakādibhyo viśiṣṭatratvādato bodhisattva eva tveṣa jambuṣaṇḍagato yujyate| āyuḥ kṣayādibhiriti astyāyuḥkṣayānmaraṇaṃ na puṇyakṣayādasti puṇyakṣayānnāyuḥ kṣayādityādityādi|| 40-41||  
[ chedasandhānavairāgyahānicuyutyupapattayaḥ|  
manovijñāna eveṣṭā upekṣayāṃ cuyatodbhavau||  42|| ]

chedasandhānavairāgya iti vistaraḥ| kuśalamūlasamucchedo mithyādṛṣṭyā| sā ca mānasyeva santīkaratvā| kuśalamūlapratisandhānaṃ samyagdṛṣṭyā vicikitsayā vā te ca manasyau| dhātubhūmivairagyam dhātuto bhūmito vā vairāgyaṃ manovijñāna eva samāhita eva citte labhyatvāt| parihāṇirayoniśomanasikārapravartitā manovijñānena cāyoniśomanaskikāro vikalpa iti| cyutiḥ saṃkṣiptapañcendriyagocarasya pravāhacchedānukūle vijñāne bhavati upapattirapi viparyastamaterbhavatīti manovijñāna eva yujyate| antarābhavapratisandhirapa iti pratisandhisāmānyādanukto'pyuktakalpa iti nocyata ityabhiprāyaḥ| cyutireva cyutam| ekārthatā darśayati bhāvektotpannatvāt| itare hi vedane paṭyāviti sukhaduḥkhe|  
visabhāgabhūmikatvāditi samāhitaṃ cittaṃ samāhitabhūmikatvādvisabhāgaṃ kāmadhātoḥ| ataścyuticittamupapatticittaṃ vā na yujyate| yadā tarhi rūpadhātāveva mriyate upapadyate vā tadā samāhibhūmikaṃ cittaṃ tadbhaviṣyatītyata āha ābhisaṃskārikatvāditi| abhisaṃskāro yatnaḥ abhisaṃskāreṇa nivṛttamābhisaṃskārikam tadbhāvaḥ tasmāt| na tadābhisaṃskārikaṃ cyuticittamupapacittacittaṃ vā yujyate maraṇāvasthāyā apaṭutvāt anugrāhakatvācca| yasmācca samāhitaṃ cittamanugrāhakaṃ na cchedānukūlamato'pi samāhitacittasya cyutirnāsti nāpyacittakasyeti vistaraḥ| nāpyacittakasya| kim cyutirūpapattirveti prakṛtam| acittako nirodhasamapattisamāpanno'saṅgisamāpattisamāpannastadvipāke vāvasthitaḥ| so'cittaka pakrantu mārayituṃ na śakyate śastrādibhiḥ| ato nācittakasyāsti cyutiḥ| yadā cāsyā śrayo vipariṇantumārabhauteti śastreṇāgninā vopakramānnirodhasamāpattimasaṃjñisamāpatti vā samāpannasyāsaṃjñisamāpattervipāke sthitasya vipākāvedhaparisamāpteravaśya masya tadānīmāśrayapratibaddhaṃ cittamāśraye bījabhāvenāsti| tat sammukhībhūya paścātpracyaveta| kaḥpracyevata pudgalaḥ| yadi pudgalo'dhikriyeta asamānakartṛkatvā ktāvidhirna prāpnoti| cittaṃ tarhi pracyaveta| athavaivamabhisambandhaḥ yadā cāśrayo vipariṇantumanyathātvamāpattumārabheta avaśyamasya pudgalasya tadāśrayapratibāddhaṃ bījabhāvenāvasthita cittaṃ sammukhībhūya samudācarya pariṇantumārabheta| paścāt pracyaveta pudgala iti| athavā sammukhībhūyeti sammukhībhāvyetyartheṇilopāt|  
asti parṇarūho vāto asti parṇamusvo'paraḥ

iti yathā| upapattau tvayuktamacittakatvāt| kasmāt cittacchedahetvabhāvāt| avaśyaṃ kliṣṭatayā pratisandhicittasya nirodhasamāpattyādyasambhavāt| vinā ca kleśenānupapatteḥ| sarvakleśairhi tadbhūmikairūpapattipratisandhibandho bhavati| na ca cittamantareṇa kleśāḥ kadācidapi bhavantīti| ato'pi nāstyacittakasyopapattiḥ|  
maraṇabhavastriprakāra ityuktamiti|  
upapattibhavaḥ kliṣṭaḥ sarvakleśaiḥ svabhūmikaiḥ

iti| upapattibhava eva kliṣṭa ityavadhāraṇāt| maraṇapūrvāntakālāntarābhavāstriprakārāḥ kuśalākuśalāvyākṛtā uktāḥ| athavā

nirodhayatyuparamo nārūpye jīvitaṃ manaḥ|  
upekṣāṃ ca

iti vistareṇa yāvat

śubhe sarvatra pañca ca

ityuktam ato maraṇabhavastriprakāra ityutsargaḥ|| 42||  
[ naikāgrācittayoretau nirvātyavyākṛtadvaye|  
kramacyutau pādanābhihṛdayeṣu manaścyutiḥ|| 43|| ]

arhastu

nirvātyavyākṛtadvaye

ityevamapavādaḥ| asti cet kāmadhātau vipāka upekṣeti|  
adho'pi madhyamasteke

ityekīyamatena| nāsti cediti|  
ataḥ param|  
aduḥkhāsukhavedyam

ityaparamatena| airyāṣathika eveti| ariyāpathikajātīyaṃ tadyatra nirvāti|  
vipākajairyāpathikaśailpasthānikanairmitam|  
caturdhā vyākṛtaṃ kāme iti naitāvadevāvyākṛtamiṣyata iti vyākhyātametat| taddhi cittacchedānukūlamiti apratisandhikacittacchedānukūlam durbalatvāt paramāpaṭutvādityarthaḥ| sapratisandhikacittacchede kuśalākuśalamapi cittaṃ sambhavati|  
teṣāṃ yathāsaṃkhyamti pādayorapāyagāmināṃ vijñānaṃ sannirūdhyate nābhyāṃ manuṣyagāminām hṛdaye devagāminām| ārūpitvādadeśasthaṃ vijñānaṃ kathaṃ teṣu nirūdhyata iti ata āha kāyendriyasya teṣu nirodhāditi| teṣu pādādiṣu| kāyendriyapratibaddhavṛtti hi kāmadhātau rūpadhātau ca vijñānamiti| teṣāmapi hṛdaye vijñānaṃ nirūdhyata iti| yathāsaṃkhyanirdeśādevāyamartho labhyate| katham hṛdayaṃ ca hṛdayaṃ ca hṛdaye pādaśca nābhiṣa hṛdaya ceti pādanābhihṛdayānīti catvāro'rthāḥ|| 43||  
[ adhonṛsuragājānāṃ marmacchedastvabādibhiḥ|  
samyaṅmithyātvaniyatā āryāntaryakāriṇaḥ|| 44|| ]

adhogaśca nṛgaśca suragaśca ajagaścetyete'pi catvāro'rthāḥ tena yathāsaṃkhyasiddhiḥ|  
prāyeṇeti grahaṇaṃ devādīnāṃ sukhamṛtyutvāt na tu punastāni kāṣṭhavacchidyante| kiṃ tarhīti tīvrābhirvedanābhiśchidyanta iveti| chinnavadvā na puna śceṣṭhanta iti chinnānītyucyanta iti| cchinnāṅgapratyaṅgavat na punaśceṣṭanta iti chinnānītyucyante| saiṣā kālpanikī chedavṛttimarmaṇāṃ dvidhābhāvākaraṇāt| apatejovāyūnāṃ anyatamenetyukte pṛcchati kasmānna pṛdhivīdhātuneti| apatejovāyudhātupradhānā yathāyogamiti yathāsambhavam apadhātupradhānaḥ śrleṣmā tejodhātupradhānaṃ pittam vāyudhātupradhān vāyuśeṣa iti| bhājanalokasaṃvatanīsādharmyeṇetyapara iti| tisro bhājanasaṃvartanyaḥ| apasaṃvartanī tejaḥ saṃvartanī vāyusaṃvartanī ca| tāsāṃ ca sādharmyeṇa apatejovāyudhātūnāmanyatameneti vistareṇoktamiti|  
kathamārādyātā ātyantikavisaṃyogaprāptilābhāt| pṛthagjānānāṃ tvasti visaṃyogaprāptilābhaḥ na tvātyantika iti te nārādyātā eveti te hi mithyātve'pi niyatā bhaveyuriti| yadyānantaryāṇi kuryurityarthaḥ| na ca ta iti vistaraḥ| na ca te mokśabhāgīyalābhinaḥ kālaniyamena samyakatve niyatāḥ| yathā saptakṛtvaḥ paramādayaḥ| ādiśabdena kūlaṃkulaikavīcikādayo gṛhyante| kālaniyamaścaiṣāṃ pugdalanirdeśe vakṣyate niyatebhyo dvayebhya ebhyo'nye'niyatāḥ| pratyayāpekṣaṃ hīti vistaraḥ| yadi samyakatve pratyayaṃ labhante samyakatve niyatāḥ| atha mithyātve mithyātve niyatāḥ| athobhayasminna pratyayaṃ labhante anubhayabhāktve'pi|| 44||  
[ tatra bhājanalokasya saṃniveśamuśantayadhaḥ|  
lakṣaṣoḍaśakodvedhamasaṃkhyaṃ vāyumaṇḍalam|| 45||  
apāmekādaśodvedhaṃ sahasrāṇi viṃśatiḥ|  
aṣṭalakṣocchayaṃ paścāccheṣaṃ bhavati kāñcanam|| 46   ]

uktaḥ sattvalokaḥ vidyādikramanirdeśena|  
uśanti

icchantītyarthaḥ|  
lakṣaṣoḍaśakodvedhamasaṃkhyaṃ vāyumaṇḍalam

lakṣaṣoḍaśakaṃ vedhanabhāvena pariṇāhenāsaṃkhyamiti samantaparikṣepeṇāsaṃkhyamityarthaḥ| karmādhipatyena karmasāmarthyena| tatra nirśanam yathā bhuktaṃ pītaṃ cānnaṃ pānaṃ ca yathāsaṃkhyena nāpakka pakkaśayaṃ patati| pakkameva sa pakkasthānaṃ gacchatītyarthaḥ| kusūlanyāyena koṣṭhanyāyena| samantāt prakārāvadavasthitamityarthaḥ| pakkakṣīraśarībhāvayogena pakkakṣīraśarībhāvanyāyena| yathā pakkasya kṣīrasya śarīstrīliṅganirdeśaḥ śara ityarthaḥ| athavā śarasya bhāva śarībhāvaḥ strīprayogāḥ| yathā'sau dravo ghanībhāvena bhavatyevaṃ jalasyopariṣṭhāt kāñcanamayī bhūmiriti| śeṣaṃ trayo lakṣāḥ sahasrāṇi ca viṃśatiriti viṃśatisahasrottarāṇāmekādaśānāṃ lakṣaṇāmaṣṭavapanītyaitāvaccheṣaṃ bhavati|| 45-46||  
[ tiryak trīṇi sahasrāṇi sārdhaṃ śatacatuṣṭayam|  
lakṣadvādaśakaṃ caiva jalakāñcanamaṇḍalam|| 47||  
samantatastu triguṇaṃ tatra merūryugandharaḥ|  
īṣādharaḥ khadirakaḥ sudarśanagiristathā|| 48||  
aśvakarṇo vinitako nimindhagiristataḥ|  
dvīpā bahiścakravāḍaḥ sapta haimāḥ sa āyasaḥ|| 49||  
cūtaratnamayo merūrjale'śītisahasrake|  
magna ūrdhaṃ jalānmerūrbhūyo'śītisahasrakaḥ|| 50||  
ardhārdhahāniraṣṭāsu samocchāyaghanāśca te|  
sītāḥ saptāntarāṇyeṣāmādyāśītisahasrikā|| 51|| ]

tiryak nnīṇi sahasrāṇi

iti vistaraḥ| caturdvīpakasya etatparimāṇamucyate| na trisāhasramahāsāhasrasya samānaṃ hyetadubhayam| etaditi

jalakāñcanamaṇḍalam

samantatastu triguṇam

iti samantaparikṣepeṇa triguṇametadubhayam| sarvasya parimaṇḍalasyeyaṃ sthitiḥ| yadasya tiryakpramāṇaṃ triguṇameva samantaparikṣiptasya pramāṇamiti| tena caturdvīpakaścakrīkṛta iti| cakra kṛtaścakrīkṛtaḥ| cakrākāratāṃ gamitaḥ ata eva

cakravāḍaḥ

ityucyate śastrakamityayomayaḥ| yathāsaṃkhyaṃ caturṣu pārśveṣviti uttarakurūpārśva suvarṇamayaṃ pūrvavidehapārśva rūpyamayaṃ jambudvīpapārśvaṃ vaidūryamayamavaragodānīyapārśva sphaṭikamayam|  
nānāvidhabījagarbhāḥ nānāprakārasāmarthyaiyuktāḥ| bahuvidhaprakārabhinnairvāyubhiriti tadutpādane anekaprakārasāmarthyabhinnairvāyubhirityarthaḥ asamādhāneneti ayugpadbhāvenetyarthaḥ| na hi yadā kāryaṃ tadā kāraṇaṃ tiṣṭhati| na hyavasthitasya dravyasyeti rūparasādyātmakasya| dharmāntaranivṛttāviti kṣīranivṛttau| dharmāntaraprādurbhāva iti dadhijanma| sa eva dharmī neti| rūpādyātmakakṣīrādidharmebhyo'nyo dharma utpādavyaya 'pyanupanno'vinaṣṭa pariṇāma iti kṣīranivṛttau dadhibhāvaḥ tadeva cedamiti kṣīrameva dadhi na cedaṃ tatheti na cedaṃ dadhi kṣīram| tadevbedamiti yat pariṇāmenetyuktam| na cedaṃ tatheti| tasyaiva dravyasyānyathībhāvamātraṃ pariṇāma iti vacanāt| apūrveṣā vācoyuktiriti svavacanavirūddhetyabhiprāyaḥ| pūrvottarayorhi kṣaṇayoranyathātvamiṣyate  yayoścānyathātvaṃ tayoranyatvaṃ dṛṣṭam tadyathā devadattayajñadattayoḥ| tasmāt kāryakāraṇayoranya tvena bhavitavyam|  
suvarṇādaya iti| suvarṇarūpyamaṇimṛdādayaḥ| ṣaṣṭiyojanaśatasahasrasamucchāya iti| ṣaṣṭayadhiko yojanalakṣa ucchāya ityarthaḥ|| 47-51||  
[ ābhyantaraḥ samudro'sau triguṇaḥ sa tu pārśvataḥ|  
ardhārdhanāparāḥ sītāḥ śeṣaṃ bāhyo mahodadhiḥ|| 52||  
lakṣatrayaṃ sahasrāṇi viṃśatirdve ca tatra tu|  
jambudvīpo dvisāhasratripārśvaḥ śakaṭākṛtiḥ|| 53||  
sārdhaṃ triyojanaṃ tvekaṃ prāgvideho'rdhacandravat|  
pārśvatrayaṃ tathāsyaikaṃ sārdhatriśatayojanam|| 54||  
godānīyaḥ sahasrāṇi sapta sārdhāni maṇḍalaḥ|  
sārdhe dve madhyamasyāṣṭau caturasḥ kurūḥ samaḥ|| 55|| ]

aṣṭāṅgopetasyeti|  
śītalācchalaghusvādumṛduniḥpūtigandhikam|  
pītaṃ na bādhate kukṣḥiṃ na kaṇṭhaṃ kṣiṇoti tajjalam||  
iti| aṣṭāṅgasaṃgrahaśrlokaḥ| pārśvatastu triguṇo bhavati yugandharatīreṇa gaṇyamāna iti| sa

ābhyantaraḥ samudraḥ

yugandharapārśe nāyāmataḥ saṃkhyāyamānaḥ

triguṇa

iti kṛtvā catvāriṃśadyojanasahasrādhikalakṣadvayamāyāmo bhavati yugandharapārśve| aśītirekā tadubhayataścetarayordiśostat sītānadīvaipulyayogenāpare dve aśīti iti tisro'śītayaḥ| catvāriṃśadadhikaṃ lakṣadvayaṃ bhavatīti|  
lakṣatrayaṃ sahasrāṇi viṃśatidvaca iti| nimindharagiricakravāḍayorantara ekasyāṃ diśi bāhyasya samudrāsya tat parimāṇamuktam| atra sahasramekaṃ dve śate cānye ardhāṣṭāśītireveti vaktavyam nyūnatvāt| evamaparasyāṃ diśi vaktavyam| evaṃ dvayoḥ sahasrayoḥ pañcasu śateṣu pañcasaptatau ca yojanānāṃ prakṣipte sa ādhāraḥ pūrito bhavati ya uktaḥ

tiryak trīṇi sahasrāṇi sārdha śatacatuṣṭayam|  
lakṣadvādaśakaṃ caiva jalakāñcamaṇḍalam||  
iti anayā caturdvīpasamkhyayā yathāvinyāsamatiricyamānatvāt| tadidaṃ parihriyate| naiṣa doṣaḥ| cakravāḍabahirjalakāñcanamaṇḍalasya etāvatātiricyamānatvāt| na tarhi caturdvīpakastena cakravāḍena cakrīkṛtaḥ| uparibhūmiranena cakrīkṛtā na jalamaṇḍalamityavagantavyam| apara āha| na sumervādayaḥ parvatā atyantacchinnataṭāḥ samucchāyaghanā bhavanti| kiṃ tarhi parvatākāranyāyena kiñcidatiricyamānādharabhāgā durlakṣaviśeṣatvāt

samocchāyaghanāśca te

ityuktāḥ| atastaistaiḥ kiñcidatiracyamānairadhobhāgaistadādheyaṃ sumervādikamādhāraṃ jalakāñcanamaṇḍalātmakaṃ vyāpnotītyavirodha eṣa iti|  
anya iti vistaraḥ| na bodhisattvāśrayādanya āśrayo vajrāsanādvānyaḥ pradeśastaṃ vajropamaṃ samādhiṃ sodu samartha iti| yaśca dvīpo yadākṛtiriti pratyakṣamtat bhūmivaśāt sattvānāṃ vaicitryam himavadvindhyavāsināṃ kirātaśavarāṇāṃ gauraśyāmate dṛśyate|| 52-55||  
[ dehā videhāḥ kuravaḥ kauravāścāmarāvarāḥ|  
aṣṭau tadantaradvīpāḥ śāṭhā uttaramantriṇaḥ|| 56||  
ihottareṇa kīṭādrinavakāddhimāvāṃstataḥ|  
pañcāśada vipulāgādhasaro'rvāg gandhamādanāt|| 57||  
adhaḥ sahasairviśatyā tanmātro'vīcirasya hi|  
tadūrdhva sapta narakāḥ sarve'ṣṭau ṣoḍaśotsadāḥ|| 58||  
kukūlaṃ kuṇapañcaiva kṣuramārgādikaṃ nadī|  
teṣāṃ caturdiśaṃ śītā anye'ṣṭāvabudādayaḥ|| 59||  
ardhena meroścandrākau pañcaśat saikayojanau|  
ardharātro'stagamanaṃ madhyāhna udayaḥ samam|| 60|| ]

dehā videhāḥ kuradhaḥ

iti| sthānināṃ bahutvāt sthānasyāpi bahuvacananirdeśaḥ | eko rākṣaseriti| cāmarāvarayoranyataraḥ|  
kīṭādrinavakāt

iti kīṭākṛtīnāṃ parvatānāṃ navakāt| tadadhikāreṇā'yaṃ jambudvīpa iti| jambuvṛkṣādhikāreṇa jambuvṛkṣacihno dvīpo jambudvīpaḥ| phalādhikāreṇa veti| jambā lup ceti phalamapi jambu| tatphalacihno jambudvīpa ityarthaḥ| catuḥ skandhā iti| catuḥprākārā ityarthaḥ| catuḥsanniveśa ityapare| ayaḥ prakāraparyantā iti| ayaḥ prakāraparikṣiptā ityarthaḥ| ayasā pratikubjitā iti| ayasopariṣṭācchāditā| ayasā pihitadvārā ityapare| sphuṭeti vyāptāḥ| pūrvavaditi utkṣipte pāde punarapi sañjāyate tvaḍyāṃsaśoṇitamityarthaḥ|  
kṣurārgādikam

trayamiti kṣuramārgo'sopatravanaṃ śālmalīvanaṃ ceti| trayaḥ śyāmalaśabalāśca śvāno bhakṣayantīti asipatravanāntarbhūtāḥ| ayastuṇḍāśca vāyasā iti śālmalīvanāntarbhūtāḥ| prāsā bhindipālādayaḥ| adhikayātanāsthānatvādutasadā iti| ucchabdo'dhikārthaḥ| adhikaṃ sīdyanta atretyutsadāḥ| punaryātanādādhikyamaparaḥ| narakeṣviti vistaraḥ| apara iti sthaviramanorathaḥ sa ūrdhvārthamucchabdamiti| tenāha| ā narakāvarodhādavīcyādinakāvarodhāt ūrdhvameṣḥu kukūlādiṣu sīdanti atastadutsadā iti praśnāt praśāntaramiti| ṣoḍaśot sadāḥ katama ityuktā caturtha utsado vaitaraṇī yasyāṃ sattvā aśiśaktiprāsahastaiḥ purūṣairūbhābhyāṃ tīrābhyāṃ prativāryamāṇādūrdhvamapi ta  svidyante pacyanta iti| tasmādidaṃ praśnāntāramupājayate| kiṃ te narakapalā iti vistaraḥ| vivārtanīvāyuvaditi yathā vivartanyāṃ vāyavo bhājanalokābhinivṛttaye ceṣṭante| tadvatte bhūtabhautikāmātrā narakapālā iti| yamarākṣasā iti| pāpakarmaṇāḥ sattvā narakapālā jāyanta ityarthaḥ| ye te yemeneti| ye te yamena devadūtīyasūtroktenānuśāsanenānuśiṣṭāstān sattvān narakasthāneṣu prakṣipanti yamarājadhānī nivāsinaḥ| ta ete yamarākṣasā uktāḥ| na tu ye kāraṇā yātanāḥ kārayanti asiśaktiprāsahastādayaḥ| tasyedānīṃ karmaṇāḥ ka vipāka iti| narakapālānāṃ narakakṛtasya karmaṇaḥ ka vipākḥ| teṣveveti vistaraḥ| yeṣvānantaryakāriṇāṃ vipākavākaśasteṣu narakeṣu teṣāṃ narakapalānāṃ ko vipratibandhaḥ |  etadukta bhavati teṣāmapi tāvadānataryakāriṇāṃ mahādānantaryakarmaṇo vipākasyātimahato narakeṣvavakāśo'sti| kimaṅga punarakapālānāṃ yat karma tat kāraṇāḥ kārayatāmiti| kṛtāvadhitvā kṛtamaryādatvānnarakapālān prati ete na dagdhavyā iti| bhūtaviśeṣanirvṛttervā bhūtaviśeṣāstādṛśāsteṣāṃ karmabhirabhinirvṛttāḥ yattenāgninā na dahyante| kāyaśabdavikārānurūpāṇiti | kāyavikārānurūpāṇayarbudī niravuda utpalaḥ padyo mahāpadyaśceti nāmānyarvudādyākārakāyatvāttatratyānāṃ sattvānām śabdavikārānurūpāṇayaṭhaṭho hahavo huhuva ityetāni nāmāni ata eveti dhānyarāśivadadhoviśālatvāt bahiriti nāntargataḥ|  
taijasaṃ sūryakāntātmakamāpyaṃ candrakāntātmakaṃ yathāsambhavamiti| tadubhayamaṇḍalaṃ dṛṣṭādīnāṃ deśakālāvasthāntarānurūpyeṇānugrahārthamupaghātārtha ca| evamanyeṣvapi yojyamiti| katham yadā pūrvavidehe'rdharātrastadā jambudvīpe sūryāstagamanamavaragodānīye madhyāha uttarakurāvudaya ityevamanyatrāpi yojyam|| 56-60||  
[ prāvṛṇmāse dviye'ntyanavamyāṃ vardhate niśā|  
hemantānāṃ caturthe tu hīyate'haviparyayāt|| 61|| ]

prāvṛṇamāse dvitīye'ntyanavamyām

iti prāvṛṇamāsāścatvāraḥ śrāvaṇo yāvatkārttikaḥ sarve ca māsāḥ kṛṣṇapakṣādyāḥ| tatra bhādrapadaśuklapakṣanavamyāṃ rātrivardhate| hemantakānāṃ catruthe hīyate| hemantā māsāścatvāro mārgaśīrṣādyāḥ phālgunāntāḥ| tatra phālgunaśuklapakṣanavamyāṃ hīyate|| 61||  
[ lavaśo rātrayaharvṛddhī dakṣiṇottarage ravau|  
svacchāyayārkasannidhyād vikalendusamīkṣaṇam|| 62|| ]

[ lavaśaḥ

iti lavaṃ lavam kālādhvanoratyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā| aparapārśve chāyā patantī vikalamaṇḍalaṃ darśayatīti tadyathā stambhe pradīpachāyā patantī yathā yathā stambha āsanno bhavati tathā tathā stambhaḥ svacchāyayācchādyate dūre hi vartamāne pradīpe paripūrṇastambho dṛśyate kiñcidāsanne kiñcit kṣīyate yāvadasanne stambho naṣṭarūpo vartate tadvadetat vāhayogaḥ sa tādṛśa iti| punastiryagavanāmonnāmayogenādhobhāgaścandramaṇḍalasya kṣīyate| ūrdhvaṃ vardhate ceti yogācārāḥ|| 62||  
[ pariṣaṇḍāścataso'sya daśasāhasikāntarāḥ|  
ṣoḍaśā'ṣṭau sahasāṇi catvāri dve ca nirgatāḥ|| 63||  
karoṭapāṇayastāsu mālādharāḥ sadāmadāḥ|  
mahārājikadaivāśca parvateṣvapi saptasu|| 64|| ]

cāturmahārājakāyikā iti| caturmahārājānāṃ kāyaḥ tatra bhavāścāturmahārājakāyikāḥ | sumerūpariṣaṇḍādīnīti ādiśabdena yugandharādīni|  
daśasāhasrikāntarāḥ

iti| yojanadaśasahasrapramānāntarā ityarthaḥ| ardhabhākṣiptamitu| ardhamavaṣṭavdham

sadāmadāḥ

iti sadāmattā iti vaktavye śrlokabandhānuguṇyena sadāmadā ityuktam arthaikatvāt| caturthyā catvāro mahārājānaḥ svayaṃ prativasantīti| dhṛtarāṣṭraḥ purvadigabhāge virūdako dakṣiṇe virūpākṣaḥ paścime vaiśravaṇa uttarasminnityanukramo'vagantavyaḥ| cāturmahārājikā devā ityuktāmiti yasmāccaturthyā pariṣaṇḍāyāṃ catvāro mahārājānaḥ svayaṃ prativasanti ata evamuktam|  
mahārājikadevāḥ

iti| mahārāje bhavā iti kṛtvā| mahiṣṭho mahattamaḥ|| 63-64||  
[ merūmurdhni trāyastriṃśāḥ sa cāśītisahasadik|  
vidikṣu kūṭāścatvāra uṣitā vajrapāṇibhiḥ|| 65||  
madhye sārdhadvisāhasapārśvamadhyardhayojanam|  
puraṃ sudarśanaṃ nāma haimaṃ citratalaṃ mṛdu|| 66||  
sārdhadviśatapārśvo'tra vaijayanto bahiḥ punaḥ|  
taccaitrarathapārūṣyamiśranandanabhūṣitam|| 67|| ]

sa viṃśatisahasradigiti| yadyevaṃ kathamidamukta

samucchāyaghanāśca te

iti madhyabhāgamevābhisamīkṣyaivamuktamiti teṣāmabhiprāyaḥ| adhyardhamiti| adhikamardhamasminniti| adhyardha yojanamasyeti

adhrdhayojanam|  
samucchāyeṇetyadhyāhāryam| dhātuśatena raṅgaśatena nānāratnasthānavidhānasampadeti| nānāratnavidhānasampadā nānāsthānavidhānasampadā ca| sarvānyabhavanānāṃ śriyastāsāṃ mahimāno mahattvāni| hepaṇo lajjano lajjājanaka ityarthaḥ| dairdhyeṇārdhatṛtīye yojanaśate pārśvamiti| ekaṃ pārśvametatpramānam| yāvaccaturthamapyetatpramāṇamiti| caitrarathādīnyapi caturasrasārdhadviśatapārśvāni|| 65-67||  
[ viṃśatyantaritānyeṣāṃ subhūmīni caturdiśam|  
pūrvottare pārijātaḥ sudharmā dakṣḥiṇāvare|| 68|| ]

subhūmīni

iti śobhanā bhūmaya eṣāmiti subhūmīni krīḍasthānani|  
caturdśim

iti| catasro diśā asyeti caturdiśaṃ kriyāviśeṣaṇam| bhāgurimatena dikśabda ākārānto bhavati| viṃśatiyojanāntyatikramyeti| krīḍāsthānāni bhavantīti| viṃśatiyojanānyantaritānītyucyate|| 68||  
[ tata ūrdhvaṃ vimāneṣu devāḥ kāmabhujastu ṣaṭ|  
dvandvāliṅganapāṇyāptihasitekṣiptamaithunāḥ|| 69|| ]

trāystriṃśānāmiti trayastriṃśāsteṣu bhavāstrāyastriṃśāḥ teṣām| kāmaratiprakarṣālayaḥ kāmarativiśeṣasthānam| vṝkṣānatikramaṃ sandhāyoktamiti| taṃ kovidāravṛkṣa mana tikramya sa gandho vartate| tasya pañcāśadyojanāni śākhāpatrapalāśamiti| evaṃ vṛkṣānatikramaṃ sandhāyokta pañcāśadyojanāni prativātaṃ gandho vātīti| atrottaramāhuḥ na hi nāma prativātaṃ vāto bhavati yatraivotpadyate tatraiva dhvaṃsyate na deśāntaraṃ santānaṃ santanoti| ukta ca pañcāśadyojanāni prativātamiti| tasmādasamādhireṣaḥ| tasmāt samādhyantaramidamāha| tasyaiva tviti vistaraḥ| kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇam yojanaśatamatra vātaṃ gandho vāti prativātantu pañcāśāt| na tataḥ paretyata āha mandatadaratasamārambhāttviti vistaraḥ| mṛdumārūtapratibadhyamānatāyā mandataratamasamārambhaḥ| tataśca sa gandhasantāna āśveva samucchidyate| ataḥ kāraṇāt na tathā viprakṛṣṭamadhvānaṃ yojanaśatapramāṇaṃ prasarpatīti| utāho vāyuradhivāsito jāyat iti| yathā tileṣu puṣpagandhādgandhāntaramutpadyate'nyadaiva na sa pauṣṇo gandha ityāha| yadi pañcāśadyojanaṃ prativātaṃ gandho vāti| yattrhi bhagavatoktaṃ na puṣpagandhaḥ prativātametīti vistaraḥ| mauloko gandho mustādiḥ| kṛtyākṛtyaṃ samarthayanta iti| kāryākārya saṃpratīcchanti saṃpradhārayantītyarthaḥ|  
samāsena yeṣāṃ bhājanaṃ prajñāyata iti| bhājanaṃ sthānam vistarenānye'pi devāḥ krīḍāpramoṣakaprahāsakādayaḥ santīti| ataḥ samāsenoktam dvandvena yāvadīkṣitena maithunameṣāmiti

dvandvāliṅganapāṇyāptihasitekṣitamaithanāḥ|  
sarveṣāṃ samāpattyeti| ṣaṇṇāmapi kāmabhujāṃ dvandvasamāpattyā paridāhavigamaḥ| kālaparimāṇaṃ tu prajñaptāvuktamiti| yāvatā kālena dvandvāliṅganapāṇyāptihasitekṣitāni bhavanti tāvatā kālena eṣāṃ bhūmisambandhavāsināṃ yāmatuṣitanirmāṇaratiparanirmitavaśavartināṃ yathākramaṃ dvandvasamāpattiriti vaibhāṣikāḥ| yāvadyāvaditi vistaraḥ| yāvadyāvat pareṇa paratareṇa viṣaṇāṃ viṣayāṇāṃ tīvrataratvāt rāgo'pi tīvratarastāvattāvanmaithukāra ityabhiprāyaḥ|| 99||  
[ pañcavarṣopamo yāvad daśavarṣopamaḥ śiśuḥ|  
sambhavatyeṣu sampūrṇāḥ savastrāścaiva rūpiṇaḥ|| 70||  
kāmopapattayastisnaḥ kāmadevāḥ samānuṣāḥ|  
sukhopapattayastisro nava tridhyānabhūmayaḥ|| 71 || ]

te punaścatvāro devanikāyā iti| cāturmahārājakāyikā yāvattuṣikāḥ| yathotpannaparobhogaḥ| sa eṣāmastīti yathotpannapari bhoginaḥ tadbhāvastasmāt| yathecchayātmanimitam tasya paribhogaḥ pūrvavat| yathecchātmaparinirmitaparibhogitvāditi| ātmanā pareṇa ca nirmite yathecchayātmaparanirmite tayoḥ paribhogaḥ pūrvavat| yathecchaparanirmitaparibhogitvāditi kecit paṭhanti| tatra yāvadevaṃ vigrahaḥ parinimitaṃ yathecchaṃ paranirbhitaṃ tasya paribhoga iti pūrvavat nirmāṇaratīnāṃ paranirmitān kāmān paribhoktu na samṛdhyati| tadevaṃ paranirmitavaśavartināṃ nirmāṇaratibhyo viśeṣaḥ pradarśito bhavati| bāhyarūpadiviṣayanirmāṇaṃ cātra veditavyam| vivekajena prathamadhyānabhūmikenākuśalaviviktatvāt| samādhijena ca dvitīyadhyānabhūmikena vitarkavicāravigamāt prītisukheneti| prītilakṣaṇena sukhena niḥprītikena ca sukhena saumanasyavigamāt| sukhaṃ viharanta iti sukhamiti kriyāviśeṣaṇam| sukhopapattitvaṃ vicāryamiti| susvābhāvānna dhyānantaraṃ susvopapattiriti śakyaṃ vaktum| kuśalvipākatvena susvakalpatvāt susvopapattiriti na śakyaṃ vyavasthāpayitum| caturthe'pi dhyāne sukhopapattiprasaṅga iti cet| na tasyāṃ bhūmau sukhābhāvāt| tasmādvicārya saṃpradhāryametaditi bhāva|| 70-71||  
[ sthānāt sthānādadho yāvattāvadūrdhvaṃ tatastataḥ|  
nordhva darśamastyeṣāmanyatradhiparāśrayāt|| 72   ]

evaṃ śeṣā iti| ṛddhayā vā yāmastuṣitāṃ gaccheyuḥ yadi ṛddhimatā nīyeran devena vā tatrayena| samānabhūmikāstu cāturmahārājakāyikāstrayastriṃśāṃ gacḥanti| āgataṃ turdhvopapannaṃ paśyediti| samānabhūmikaṃ nordhvabhūmikamiti| prathamadhyānopapanno na dvitīyadhyānopapannamityādi| tadicchayeti ūrdhvopapannecchayā| ihatyamiveti| iha bhava ihatyaḥ| adhobhūmika ityarthaḥ| ihatyamiva paśyediti nikāyāntarīyāḥ| yathāspraṣṭavyamityudāharaṇamātrametat| yathā śabda ityetadapi gamyeta caturṇā kāmāvacarāṇām yāmādisthānānām| dviguṇottaramiti yāmasthānāddviguṇaṃ tuṣḥitasthānamityādi| caturtha tvaparimāṇamiti| tārakāvadatalavāṭakapratibaddhavimānatvādaparimāṇatvasambhava ityabhiprāyaḥ|| 72||  
[ caturdvīpakacandrākamerūkāmadivaukasām|  
brahyalokasahasrañca sāhsraścūḍiko mataḥ|| 73||  
tat sahasra dvisāhasro lokadhātustu madhyamaḥ|  
tatasahasra trisāhasraḥ samasaṃvartasambhavaḥ|| 74|| ]

sahasra brahyalokānāmiti| brahytakāyibrahyapurohitamahābrahyaṇāmityarthaḥ| loke tathā pratītvāt|  
cūḍikaḥ

iti mahato lokadhātoścūḍābhūtvāccūḍika ityucyate| sambhavo hi vivarta iti vividhavartanaṃ vivartaḥ| vividhā vartante'sminniti vivartaḥ| saṃvartanaṃ saṃvartaḥ savartante vāsminniti saṃvartaḥ| tasmin hi kāle sattvā ekasmin dhayāne dvitīye tṛtīye caturthe vā saṃvartante saṃgacchanta iti|  
vivartate ca saṃvṛtta āste saṃvartate samam

iti uttaratra vyākhyāsyāmaḥ||  
[ jāmbudvīpāḥ pramāṇena catuḥsārdhatrihastakāḥ|  
dviguṇottaravṛddhayā tu pūrvagodottarāhvayāḥ|| 75|| ]

jāmbudvīpāḥ pramāṇena catuḥsārdhatrihastaikāḥ

iti sārdhatrihastapramāṇaśarīrāḥ| kecittu caturhastapramāṇaśarīrāḥ|  
pūrvagodottarāhvayāḥ

iti pūrvagod uttara iti cāhvaya eṣāṃ te purvagodottarāhvayāḥ| teṣāṃ yathāsaṃkhyamaṣṭahastapramāṇāḥ pūrvavidehakāḥ ṣoḍaśahastapramāṇā godānīyā dvātriṃśaddhasta pramāṇakā auttarakauravā iti|| 75||  
[ pādavṛddhayā tanuryāvat sārdhakrośo divaukasām|  
kāmināṃ rūpiṇāṃ tvādau yojanārdhaṃ tataḥ param|| 76||  
ardhārdhavṛddhirūrdhvaṃ tu parīttābhebhya āśrayaḥ|  
dviguṇadviguṇo hitvā'nabhrakebhyastriyojanam|| 77|| ]

iti vistaraḥ|  
yāvat sārdhakrośaḥ

iti| vakṣyamāṇatvāt pāda iti krośasyeti gamyate| krośacaturthabhāgamātraṃ ca śarīraṃ cāturmāhājakāyikānām dvipādamātraṃ trāyastriṃśānāṃ yāvadadhyardhaṃ krośamātraṃ paranirmitavaśavartinām| yojanatrayeṇa hīnaṃ dviguṇamiti| dvigunaṃ kṛtvā trīṇi yojanānyapaneyāni| pañcaviṃśayojanaśatamanabhrakāṇāṃ śarūrapramāṇamiti| puṇyaprasavānāmiti| puṇyaprasavānāmardhatṛtīye yojanaśate śarīrapramāṇām| bṛhatphalānāṃ pañcayojanaśatānīti gaṇayitavyam|| 76||  
[ sahasramāyuḥ kurūṣu dvayorardhārdhavajitam|  
ihāniyatamante tu daśābdānādito'mitam|| 78||  
nṛṇāṃ varṣāṇi pañcāśadahorātro divaukasām|  
kāme'dharānāṃ tenāyuḥ pañcavarṣaśatāni tu|| 79||  
dviguṇottaramūrdhvānāmubhayaṃ rūpiṇāṃ punaḥ|  
nāstyahorātra āyustu kalpaiḥ svāśrayasammitaiḥ|| 80||  
ārūpye viṃśatiḥ kalpasahasrāṇyadhikādhikam|  
mahākalpaḥ parīttābhāt prabhṛtyadharmadhastataḥ | 81||  
kumudapadyavaditi| yatheha kumudānyahani saṅkucnati rātro vikasanti padyāni tu viparyayeṇa tathā tatra keṣāñcideva puṣpāṇāṃ saṅkocadahani ca vikāśādahorātravyavasthānam| śukīnāñca kūjānakūjātāt| akūjanadrātiḥ kūjanāt prabhātam| viparyena vā yathā śakunijoti middhāpagamopagamācca middhopagamādrātriḥ prajñāyate middhāpagamāt prabhātamiti|  
adhikādhikam

iti viṃśatiradhikam adhikaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ| tenāha| evaṃ teṣāṃ yathākramaṃ catvāriṃśat ṣaṣṭiraśītiḥ

kalpasahasrāṇi

āyuḥpramāṇamiti| vijñānāntyāyatane catvāriṃśat| ākiñcanyāyatane ṣaśṭiḥ| bhavāgre'śītiriti| tasmādadho mahākalpasyārdhamiti vistaraḥ| aśītirantarakalpāmahākalpāḥ tasyārdha catavariṃśadantarakalpāḥ| tasya mahākapārdhasya kalpīkṛtaṃ catvāriṃśadantarakalpāstavrahyapurohitānāmāyuḥprāmāṇam| tadevamātmīyārdha mahābrahyaṇa āyuḥpramāṇam| sa hyavyardha kalpa ityucyate ṣaṣyantarakalpasaṃkhyāḥ| mahābrahayadīnāmiti ādiśabdena brahyapurohitānāṃ brahyakāyikānāṃ ca prātilomyena grahaṇam|  
parīttābhāt devanikāyāditi nirdeśapekṣaṇāt|| 78||  
[ kāmadevāyuṣā tulyā ahorātrā yathākramam|  
saṃjīvādiṣu ṣaṭsvāyustaisteṣāṃ kāmadevavat|| 82||  
ardha pratāpane'vīcāvantaḥkalpaḥ punaḥ punaḥ|  
kalpastiraścā pretānāṃ māsāhaḥśatapañcakam|| 83||  
vāhādvaṣaśatenaikatiloddharakṣayāyuṣaḥ|  
arbudāṃ viśatiguṇaprativṛddhyāyuṣaḥ pare|| 84||  
kurūvarjyo'ntarāmṛtyuḥ ]

teṣāmāyuḥpramāṇamuktimiti| teṣāṃ mahābrahyapurohitānāṃ brahyakāyikānām

kāmadevāyuṣā tulyāḥ

iti vistaraḥ| cāturmaharājakāyikānāṃ yadāyuḥpramāṇamuktam| sa sañjīve ahorātraḥ| evaṃ yāvadyat parinirmitavaśavartināmāyuḥpramāṇamukta sa tapane ahorātraḥ|  
kāmadevavat

iti kāmadevānāmiva kāmadevavat| tatra tasyebveti vatiḥ tena yāvad dvādaśa māsakeneti tena tatastyenāhorātreṇa teṣāṃ triṃśadrātrakeṇa māsena dvādaśamāsakena saṃvatsareṇa tatratyāni divyāni pañca varpaśatānyāyurityādi| evamanyeṣvapi yathāyogaṃ yojyamiti| yadyāmānāmāyuḥpramāṇaṃ tat saṃghāte mahānarake ekaṃ rātrindivam tena rātrindrivena tasmin varṣasahasradvayamāyuḥpramāṇam| evamanyepvapi yathāyogaṃ yojyam| aṣṭāvime bhikṣava iti| aṣṭāvime bhikṣavo nāgā mahānāgāḥ kalpasthā dharaṇīndharā apratyuddhāryāḥ suparṇinaḥ pakṣirājasya devāsuramapi saṃgrāmamanubhavatyapi pratyanubhavantyapi| te punaḥ katame| tad yathā nando nāgarājaḥ upanando nāgarājaḥ aśvatarī nāgarājaḥ mucilindo nāgarājaḥ manasvī nāgarājaḥ dhṝtarāṣṭro nāgarājaḥ mahākāro nāgarāja elapatro nāgarāj iti| cūḍikābaddha iti śikhābaddhaḥ|  
ekajātipratibaddhasyeti ekajanamapratibaddhabuddhatvasya| caramabhavikasyeti antyajanmanaḥ| yena tasminneva janmanyarhattvaṃ sākṣāt kartavyam tasya nāstyantareṇa kālakriyā yāvadarhattvaṃ na prāpnoti| śraddhānusāridharmānusāriṇoḥ| nāstyantareṇa kālakriyā yāvacchaddhādhimuktadṛṣṭiprāptau na bhavataḥ| tadgabhayoriti bodhisattvacakavartigarbhayoḥ tanmātrornāstyantareṇa kālakriyā yavanna tau jāyete| evamādīnāmiti ādiśabdena nirodhāsaṃjñisamāpattisamāpannānāṃ maitrayādisamāpannānāṃ ca nāstyantareṇa kālakriyā yāvattasmāt samādherna vyuttiṣṭhante||  
[ oramāṇvakṣarakṣaṇāḥ|  
rupānāmādhvaparyantāḥ paramāṇuraṇustathā|| 85||  
lohāpacaśāvigochidrarajolikṣāstadudbhavāḥ|  
yavastathāṅgulīparva jñeyaṃ saptaguṇottaram|| 86||  
caturviśatiraṅgulyo hasto hastacatuṣṭayam|  
dhanuḥ pañcaśatānyeṣāṃ krośo'raṇya [ tathā ] matam|| 87||  
te'ṣṭau yojamityāhuḥ ]

caturviśatiraṅgulyo hastaḥ iti| abhidharmavacanakāle yaḥ purūśahastaḥ sa grahītavyaḥ| yena caturdvīpakādi parimāṇavyavasthānam||  
[ viṃṣatkṣaṇaśataṃ punaḥ|  
tatkṣaṇāste punaḥ ṣaṣṭirlavastriṃśadguṇottarāḥ|| 88||  
trayo muhūrtāhorātnamāsā dvādaśamāskaḥ|  
saṃvatsaraḥ sonarātraḥ ]
