hemantānāmiti| yathā varṣā ityekasminnayarthe bahuvacanaṃ prasiddhaṃ tathā hemantā grīṣmā iti draṣṭavyam| pravacane traya evartavo na yathā loke ṣaḍiti| śiśiro hi śītasāmānyāddhemanta ityukta| vasanto'pyūṣmasāmānyād grīṣmaityuktaḥ| sahradapi vṛṣṭisāmānyādvarṣā ityukteti| hemantagrīṣmavarṣāṇāmiti sarvabauddhānāṃ hemantāḥ prathama ṛtuḥ grīṣmā dvitīyaḥ varṣāstṛtīyaḥ| tatrādhikamardhamastetyadhyardhā māsaḥ| tasminnadhyardhemāsi nirgate mārgaśīrṣge supauṣārdhe'dhikrānte| śeṣe'dhaimāse| pauṣāsyārdhe'vaśiṣṭe kṛṣṇacaturdaśyām ūnarātro vdivadbhirbauddhairnipātyate tyajyata ityarthaḥ| cāturdaśiko'tra bhikṣubhḥ poṣadhaḥ kriyate| evaṃ mādhe phālgunārdhe'tikrānte śeṣe'rdhamāse phālgunāvaśiṣṭe punarapara ūnarātro nipātyate| tathā grīṣmeṣu vaiśākhakṛṣṇacaturdaśyāmāṣādakṛṣṇacaturdaśyā cāparāvūnarātnau nipātyete| varṣāsvapi bhādrapadakṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ kārttikakṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ cāparāvūnarātrau nipātyete||  
[ kalpo bahuvidhaḥ smṛtaḥ|| 89||  
saṃvataikalpo nārakāsambhavādbhājanakṣayaḥ|  
vivartakalpaḥ prāgvāyoryāvannārakasambhavaḥ|| 10||  
antākalpo'mitādyāvaddaśavarṣāyuṣastataḥ|  
utkarṣā apakarṣāśca kalpā aṣṭādaśāpare|| 11||  
utkarṣa ekaste'śītisahasādyavādāyuṣaḥ|  
iti loko vivṛtto'yaṃ kalpāṃstiṣṭhati viṃśatim|| 92||  
vivartate ca saṃvṝtta āste saṃvartate samam|  
te'hyaśitirmahākalpastadasaṃkhyatrayodbhavam|| 93||  
buddhatvam ]

gatisaṃvartanīti narakādyāḥ pañcagatayo gatyekadeśe devagatau saṃvartanta ekasthībhavantīyarthaḥ| dhātasavartanī ceti kāmadhātū rūpadhātau saṃvartata iti| sattvasavartanī yadā sattvā eva saṃvartante dhyāneṣvekasthībhavanti| na tu tāvadbhājānāti saṃvartante vinaśyantītyarthaḥ| bhājanasavartanīti| yadā bhājanānyeva saṃvartante na tu sattvāḥ ekasyāpi sattvasya tatrābhāvāt| yadaya loka iti vistaraḥ| yadayaṃ lojo viṃśatimantarakalpāna vivṛto jātaḥ| yāvannarakotpattipravṛtti niryātaṃ tanniṣṭhitaṃ vaktavyamityarthaḥ| tatapratipananaṃ vaktavyamiti| tatsaṃvartanamārabdhaṃ vaktavyamityarthaḥ| evaṃ tiryakasavartanī pretasaṃvartanīti ca vaktavyeti| yadā tiryakṣvekasattvo'pi nāvaśiṣṭo bhavati| iyatāyaṃ lokaḥ saṃvṛtto bhavati| tiryakasaṃvartanyā yasyā tadānīṃ niyataṃ tiryag vedanīyaṃ karma dhiryate| sa lokadhātvantaratiryakṣu kṣipyate| evaṃ pretasaṃvartanī yojyā yadā preteṣviti vistareṇa| manuṣyasahacariṣṇava iti manuṣaysahacaraṇaśīlā gomahiṣādayaḥ| dharmatāpratilambhikamiti| dharmatā nāma kuśalānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ tadanīṃ pariṇāmaviśeṣaḥ tathā pratilabho'syāstīti dahrmatāpratilambhikam| yanmanbuṣyeṣviti| ambuddhīpamanuṣyeṣvityabhiprāyaḥ| evaṃ pūrvavidehagodānīyasaṃvartanyau yojyau| uttarakurūsavartanī tvevaṃ vaktavyā bhavati| sa kālo yadutatrakurau manuṣyāścyavanta eva nopapadyante| yadā tatraikasattvo'pi nāvaśiṣṭo bhavati| iytāyaṃ lokaḥ saṃvṛtto bhavatyuttarakurūsaṃvartanyeti| kasmādevaṃ vaktavyā na tu jambudvīpasaṃvartanyādivadityāha| tatra vairāgyā bhavāt tarottarakurau varāgyābhāvāt| ita eva sāmantakāditi| cāturdvīkasāmantakādityarthaḥ| tdākṣepake karmaṇi parikṣīna ti bhājanākṣepake bhājanotpādake parikṣīṇa ityarthaḥ| brahya vimānaṃ nirdahādarciḥ pareti gacchatītyarthaḥ| tacca tadbhūmikamevārciriti| prathamadhyānabhūmikam| kasmāditayā na hi visabhāgā visadṛśā apakṣālā upadravā aganyādayaḥ kramante tāpayanta ityarthaḥ| āha yadyevaṃ kimidamucyate| tasmādeva ceti vistareṇetyata ucaytā tatsambaddhasambhūtvāditi vistaraḥ| tena kāmāvacareṇārciṣā sambaddhaṃ sambhūtaṃ rūpāvacaramarhcitastadbhāvastasmāti| evamanyasyāmiti ytathā tejaḥ saṃvartanyāmuktamevamanyasyāmapyapasaṃvartanyāṃ vāyusaṃvartanyā ca yathāsambhavaṃ veditavyam| kathaṃ kṛtvā narakādisaṃvartanī yāṃ purvavaduktā yāvadiyatā'yaṃ lokaḥ saṃvṛtto bhavati sattvasaṃvartanyā| tataḥ śūnye bhājane sahajo'dbhātu sambhavti| yastaṃ bhājanalokaṃ lavaṇamiva vilopayati sa kāmāvacaro'bdhātuḥ prathamadhyānabhūmikamabdhātu sambadhāti sa ca dvitīyadhyānabhūmikaṃ sambadhāti| sa cābdhātustribhūmiko'pi svena svena bhājanena sahāntardhīyate| evaṃ vāyusaṃvartanyāṃ sahajo vāyuḥ sambhavatīti vaktavyam| kathamiti pūrvavaduktā yāvat sahajo vāyudhātuḥ sambhavatīti| yastaṃ bhājalokaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ yāvattṛtīyadhyānabhūmikaṃ krameṇa pāṃśurāśimiva vikarati vidhvaṃsayati| yāvattenaiva sārdśamantadhīyate|  
vivartakalpaṃ vistareṇa vaktukāma āha| tathāh i saṃvṛtto loka iti vistaraḥ| tatheti yathā varṇitam| tadeti vistaraḥ  madākāśe mandamandā vāyavaḥ syandante tadā prabhṛti yadayaṃ loko viśatimantarakalpāna saṃvṛtto' sthāt taniryāta parisamāpta vaktavyam tadupayāta vaktavyamiti tadārabdhaṃ vaktavyamityarthaḥ| yathoktakramavidhānamiti yathokytena krameṇa vidhānena ca sarva jāyate| yathākta kramo vāyumaṇḍalamabamaṇḍalaṃ tataḥ kāñcamanayī pṛthivī tataḥ samurvādayacceti| yathoktaṃ vidhānaṃ yasya yadvidhānumaktamāyāmodvedhapariṇāhasaṃsthānalakṣaṇam tattathā sarva jāyate| prathamaṃ tu brāhyamiti vistaraḥ|yat paścāt saṃvartate tatpūrva vivarta iti sthitiḥ| tenāha prathamaṃ tu brahyaṃ vimānamutpadyate tato yāvadyāmīyamiti| yāvacchabdena brahyavimānāntaraṃ paranirmitavaśavartivimānaṃ tadanantaraṃ nirmāṇarativimānaṃ tato'ntaraṃ yāvadyāmīyaṃ vimānamutpadyate| tato yāmīyavimānotpattyantaraṃ vāyumaṇdalādīniti| yaduta bhājanavivartanyeti na sattvavivartanyeti darśayati śūnye brahye vimāna utpadyate iti mahābrahyā| anye'pi ca sattvā iti tattparivārāḥ| tataścyutvā brahyapurohiteṣviti tata ābhāsvarebhyaḥ| tanniryāta bhavatīti tadvivartanaṃ lokasya parisamāptaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | tadaupayāta bhavatīti| ārabdhaṃ vivṛttāvasthānāyetyarthaḥ ekonaviṃśatirantarakalpā aparimitāyuṣāmiti| eko'ntarakalpo brahyalokavimānādibhājananivṛttyā'tikrānta ityekonaviṃśatiravaśiṣṭā antarakalpā aparimitāyuṣāṃ manuṣyāṇāmatikrāmāntyānarakasattvaprādurbhāvāt| sā ca vivartamānāvasthātikāntaivāvagantavyā| prādurbhūte nārake sattva vivṛttā vasthā prārabdhā| seyamucyate aparimitāyuṣāmeva hasatāṃ yāvaddaśavarṣāyuṣāmeko vivṝttāvasthāntarakalpaḥ| tenāha so'sau vivṛttānāṃ tiṣṭhatāṃ prathamo'ntarakalpa iti| tato'ṣṭādaśa

utkarṣā apakarṣāśca|  
tatastu

utkarṣa ekaḥ

iti| viṃśatyantarakalpaparimāṇā vivṛttāvasthāvagantavyā|  
pañcskandhasvabhāvaḥ kalpaḥ

ta evādhā kathāvastu

iti| muktakamiti na caturāgamāntargatamityarthaḥ| aṣṭakaṃ madhyādvismṝtimiti| aṣṭau sthānāni kāpi pradeśe pramuṣitatvānna paṭhitāni| tenātra dvāpañcāśat sthānāni bhavanti| ṣaṣṭhyā ca saṃkhyāsthānairbhavitavyam| tānyaṣṭakāni svayaṃ kānicinnamāni kṝtvā paṭhitavyāni yena ṣaṣṭhisaṃkhyāsthānāni paripūrṇāni bahveyuḥ| āsaṃkhyā iti asaṃkhyeyāḥ saṃkhyānenāsaṃkhyeyā asaṃkhyā iti| bahuvacananirpheśādanuktā āpa taddhitā bhavanti| yadyevaṃ

tadasaṃkhyatrayoddhavam

iti kathamuktam| naiṣa doṣaḥ| nāsti saṃkhyā pareṇaiṣvityasaṃkhyā asaṃkhyeyāḥ kalpāḥ| asaṃkhyāyaścāsaṃkhyāścāsaṃkhyāścetyasaṃkhyāḥ teṣāṃ trayam teṣāṃ kalpanāmasaṃkhyatraya udbhavatīti tadasaṃkhhyatrayodbhavaṃ buddhatvamityevaṃ tadvayākhyātavyam| tasyābhimatatvāditi| tasya parārthasyābhimatatvāt | yo hyabhimataḥ sa svārtho dṛṣṭa iti  taddhetoriti ātmansehahetoḥ |  ātmanasehaṃ tebhya iti vistaraḥ| ātmasnehaṃ tebhyaḥ svāsāntānikebhyaḥ saṃskārebhyo nivrtya apekṣāṃ karūṇālakṣaṇāṃ vardhayitvā taddhetorapekṣāhetorduḥkhānyudvahantīti sambhāvya pratipattavyam| madhyo duḥkhanivṝttimeveti| prateyekabuddhaḥ śrāvako duḥkhanivṝttimeva mokṣameva prārthayate na sukhaṃ saṃsārikam  kutaḥ duḥkhāspadaṃ tadyataḥ syamāt tat saṃsāraikaṃ sukhaṃ duḥksthānaṃ bhāti| śreṣṭho bodhisattvaḥ savasantatigatairdukhaiḥ pareṣāṃ sukhamābhyudayikaṃ duḥkhātnyantanivṝttimeva ca niṣḥreyasasvabhāvāṃ pareṣāṃ prārthayate| athavā pareṣāṃ sukhamābhyudayikanaiḥsreyasikaṃ prārthayate duḥkhātyantanivṛttimeva ca buddhalakṣaṇamātmīyāṃ parahitakriyopāyabhūtāṃ prārthayate duḥkhātyantanivṛttimeva ca buddhakṣaṇāmātmīyāṃ parahitakriyopābhūtāṃ prārthayate| kasmādityāha yatastaddukhadukhyeva sa iti| yasmāt paradūḥkahi sa bodhisattavo duḥkhī bhavati| ata eva sukhaṃ dviprakāraṃ prārthayata iti||  
[ apakarṣe tu śatadyavattadudbhavah|  
dvayoḥ pratyekabuddhānāṃ khaḍgaḥ kalpaśatānvayaḥ|| 94||  
apakarṣasyādhastāditi vistaraḥ| varṣatasyādhastāt pañcakaṣāya abhyutasadā bhavanti abhyadhikā bhavantītyarthaḥ| varṣaśate'pyutasadāna tvabhyut sadā yathādahstāt| āyuḥkaṣāya iti kiṭtabhūtaṃ pratyavaraṃ kaṣāyaḥ āyureva kṣāya āyuḥkaṣāyaḥ| evaṃ kalpaṣayādayaḥ| yathākramamiti jīvitavipattirāyuḥkaṣāeṇa upakaraṇavipattiḥ kalpakaṣāyeṇa dhānyapuṣpaphalauṣadhādīnyupakaraṇānyalparasavīryavipākaprabhāvāni hi tāni bhavanti naiva va bhavanti| dvābhyāṃ kuśalapakṣavipattiriti kleśakaṣāyeṇa dṛṣṭikaṣāyeṇa ca kuśalapakṣasya vipattiḥ kathamityāha| kāmasukhallikātmallakamathanuyogādhikārāriti| kāmasukhameva kāmasukhallikā kāmasukhalīnateti vā kāmatṛṣnā vā kāmasukhallikā yathā kāmasukhe sajyate| ātmaklamatha ātmopatāpa ātmapīḍetyarthaḥ| kāmasukhallikātmaklamathayoranuyogo'nusevanaṃ tasyādhikārastasmāt| taduktam kāmasukhallikānuyogamātmaklamathānuyogaṃ vādhikṝtyārambha kleśadṛṣṭikaṣāyābhyāṃ kuśalapakṣa vipattirbhavatti| evaṃ tāvadaviśeṣeṇa| gṛhīpravrajitapakṣayorvā gṛhipakṣasya kleśakaṣāyeṇa kuśalapakṣavipattiḥ pravrajitapakṣasya dṛṣṭikaṣāyeṇa| yathākramaṃ kāmapradhānā gṛhiṇo dṛṣṭipradhānāḥ pravrajti iti| ekeneti sattvakaṣāyeṇā| dvividhā hi pratyekabuddhā iti| yasmād vargacariṇo'pi pratyekabuddhāḥ śrāvakapūrviṇo bhavanti na kevalaṃ khaḍgaviṣāṇakalpāḥ tasmādutkarṣe'pi teṣāmutpattirna virūdhyate| ye hyutpāditasrota āpattiphalasakṛdāgāmiphalā antarhite buddhaśāsane svayamarhattvamadhigcchanti te vargacāriṇaḥ te ca buddhotpādakāla eva prākṛtasamvegātvānna punaḥ saṃvejanīyā iti| tadīyarapathasandarśanāditi pratyekabuddheryāpathasandarśanāt| paryaṅkabaddhasamādhirūpasandarśanāt| na cāryāḥ santaḥ kaṣṭhāni tapāṃsi tapyeranniti| satyadarśanakāla eva śīlavarataparamārśadṛṣṭeḥ prahīṇatvāt śīlavrataparāmaraśadṛṣṭipūvakatvācca kaṣṭatapaḥ kriyāyā| khaḍgaviṣaṇakalpā iti| yathā khaḍgaviṣaṇā advitīyā bhavantyevaṃ te gṝhasthapravrajitairanyaiśca pratyekabuddhairasasṛṣṭavihāriṇa iti| khaḍga viṣāṇakalpā ityucayante|  
kalpaśatānvayaḥ

iti kalpaśatakṛtabodhiheturityarthaḥ| ata eva vyācaṣṭe mahākalpānāṃ śata bodhisambhāreṣu śīlasamādhiprajñālakṣaṇeṣu caritaḥ kṛtaprayoga iti| śakyañca tairiti vistaraḥ 'thāpi te pratisaṃvitprāptā na bhaveyuḥ| tathāpi taiḥ praṇidhijñānena pūrvabuddhānāmanuśāsanamanbusmṝtya dahrmodeśayitu śakyate| ṛddherāviḥkaraṇāditi ākāśagamanādikāyāḥ sattvānugrahārtha prakāśanāt| nāpi sattvānāmabhavyatvāt| dharma na deśayantīti vākyaśeṣaḥ| kasmādityāha tathāhīti vistaraḥ| yasmāddhi laukikavītarāgāstādānīṃ  saṃvidyate tasmāllokottaravītarāgā api sambhaveyurityabhiprāyaḥ| yadyete na hetavo dharmadeśanāyā akaraṇe jastarhi heturityāha| kiṃ tarhīti vistaraḥ| pūvābhyāsavaśena asaṃsargābhyāsavaśena alpitsukatādhimuktatvāt| tatkṛtarūcitvādityarthaḥ| gaṇaparikaṣaiṇaprasaṅgaparihāya ca notsahante gambhīradharmagrahaṇāya pareṣāṃ vyāpartam| tataha vyāpāre hi sati gaṇaḥ parikraṣṭavyaḥ syāt| gaṇaparikarṣaṇaprasaṅga punaḥ kasmāt pariharanti| vyākṣepasaṃsargabhīrūtvāt vyākṣepasaṃsargābhyāṃ bhīrūtvāt| vyākṣepo'bhipretasamādhyādikarmaṇayapravṛttiḥ| saṃsargo'nyasamparkaḥ| vyākṣepasaṃsargabhīrītvaṃ punaḥ pūrvābhyāsavaśāt|| 94||  
[ cakravartisamutpatirnādhoaśītasahasrakāt|  
suvarṇarūpyatāmrāyaścakriṇaste'dharakramāt|| 15||  
ekadvitricaturdvīpā na ca dvau saha buddhavat|  
pratyudyānasvayaṃyānakalahāstrajito'vadhā|| 96||  
deśasthottaptapūrṇatvairlakṣaṇātiśayo muneḥ||  
eṣa prajñaptika iti prajñaptiśāstranirdeśaḥ| caturvidhāścakravartina iti sūtre ta suvarṇacakravartayavoktaḥ prādhānyāt| asthānaṃ vartamānakāpekṣayā anavakāśe'nāgatāpekhṣayā| anāgate'pyadhvani sambhavadyugapaddayoriti| apūrvācaramāviti  naikaḥ pūrvo nāparaḥ paścāt| kiṃ tarhi sahetyarthaḥ| samasama iti vīpasā| athavā samaiḥ sarvasattveṣu vuddharbhagavadbhiḥ sama iti samasamaḥ| tāvato'nabhisaṃskāreṇeti tāvato lokadhātoranābhogena darśanāt| nikāyāntarīyā iti mahāsāṃdhikaprabhṛtayaḥ| na cakatreti| lokadhātau purūṣāyuṣamiti| puṣasyāyuḥ purūṣāyuṣam tadbibhrateti vartate| idamindriyamiti śraddhādi| asyāṅgayeti| asya pratyayasya| kimidamekaṃ lokadhātumadhikṛtyetai| ekacāturdvīpakaṃ trisāhasramahāsasara vā lokadhātumadhikṝtyaita duktamityabhiprāyaḥ| cakravartino'pi ceti vistaraḥ 'sthānamanavakāśo yadapūrvācaramau dvau tathāgatau utpadyeyātāmiti nānena vacanena lokadhātvantarotpādapratiṣedhaḥ tathāgatānaṃ tathoktatvāt| tad yatha cakravartinām| cakravartino|api hi tathoktāḥ 'sthānamanavakāśo yadapurvācaramau dvau cakravartinau loka utpodyetamiti| janāḥ prākṛtamanuṣyāḥ| manuṣyāstu matimanatḥ|śāstrāṇyavahantyi utkṣipanti| gṛhapatiratna kośādhyakṣajātīyaḥ| pariṇāyakaratna balādhyakṣajā tīyaḥ atatasambadhajātīyamiti| tena hcakravartinā sambaddhaḥ tatsambaddhasya tat sambaddhena vā saṃvartanaṃ tasmai hitaṃ tadanukūlaṃ tatsambaddhasaṃvartanīyamiti| yena sattvena karmāpacitaṃ tasminnutpanne hcakravartina svānyenaivaṃ sattvaṃ karmāṇyutpādayanti| na tu sattvaścakravartikuśalavipākotpattipratyayabhāvamāpadyate| sa ca kuśalavipākaścakavartina evetyavagantavyam| deśasthatarāṇīti abhraṣṭasthānanai| uttaptarāṇi prabhāsvaratarāṇi| saṃpūrṇarāṇykhaṇḍarūpāṇi| cakravartirājaprastāvenedaṃ vicāryate||  
[ prāgāsan rūpivat sattvā rasarāgāttataḥ śanaiḥ|| 91||  
ālasyāt saṃgrahaṃ kṝtvā bhāgādaiḥ kṣetnapo bhṝtaḥ|  
tataḥ karmapathādhikyādapahāse daśāyuṣaḥ|| 98||  
kiṃ khalu yāvat sarājakā iti rūpāvacarā ivāsannityasyārthasya pratipādānāya sūtramānayati| sūtra uktamiti vistaraḥ| dṛśyarūpatvā drapiṇaḥ| upapādukatvānmanomayāḥ  hastapādatadagulyādyupetatvāt sarvāṅgapratyaṅgopetāḥ samagrendiryatvādavikalāḥ| kāṇvibhrāntādyabhadvādahīnendriyāḥ| darśanayasṃsthānatvāc śubhāḥ| ramaṇīyavarṇatvād varṇasthāyinaḥ| ādityādiprabhānapekṣatvāt svayaprabhāḥ| karmarddhisaṃyogenākāścaratvād vihāya saṃgamāḥ| kavaḍīkārāhārānapekṣatvāt prītibhakṣāḥ prītyāhārā ti paryāyau| tathā dīrghāyuṣo dīghamadhvānaṃ tiṣṭhantīti| madhusvādurāsai ti madhuna iva svāduraso'sya rāsa iti ayoniśomanaskārasya grāhabhūtsya grāsatāṃ gatānaṃ sattvānāṃ kāma eva grahaḥ| tenāveśastasyārambha iti| sāyamāśārthamti| aparāhabhojanārtham| prātarāśārthamiti prātarbhojanartham sannidhikāraḥ saṃgrahaḥ| kṣetrāṇ vibhajya akṛṣḥṭotena śālinā tadvattikalpanāt| kṣatriyādisaṃjñānaṃ nairūktena vighinā siddhiḥ||  
[ kalopasya śastrarogābhyāṃ durbhikṣeṇa ca nirgamnaḥ|  
divasān saptamāsāṃśca varṣāṇi ca yathākramam|| 99|| ]

nirvyāṇamiti parisamāptiḥ| adharmarāgaraktā iti| kāmamithyācārarāgādhyavasitāḥ| viṣamalobhābhibhūtā iti| viṣamalobhaḥ parakīyasvīkaraṇecchā cauryād balādvā tenābhibhjtā| mithyādharmaparītā iti| viparītadharmaparidīpakāḥ| amātṛgyā apitṛgyā iti vistaraḥ| punaḥ kalpayasya niryāṇakāla iti iha kalpasya niryāṇāni trīṇyucyante śastrarogadurbhikṣāṇi| kimakasya daśarvaṣāyuḥ kalpasya trīṇi niryāṇāni krameṇa bhvānti āhosvidekaikasyaikaikaṃ krameṇeti| eke tāvadāhuḥ ekaikamiti| pare punarāhuḥ ekasya krameṇa trīṇi bhavanti pūrvadurbhikṣaṃ tadanantaro rogastadataraṃ śastramiti| pūrvakastu pakṣa iṣta iti paśyāmaḥ| taireva doṣariti| ardhādirāgādibhiḥ| amanuṣyāḥ piśācādaya īti vyādhyādikāmutasṛjanti ajo vāharanti prabhāvato vā puṣphalauṣadhiprabhṛtīnāṃ mahābhūtāni dūśayanti| yatasteṣāmasādhyā vyādhayaḥ prādurbhavanti| yato mriyante tadā ceti vistarah| yadeha jambudvīpe śāstra prāṇātipātastadā dvayordvīpayo pūrvavidehāvaragodānīyayorvayapāda udrekaprāpto bhavati| yadeha rogastadā tayodarubalyam yadeha dubhikṣa tayorjighatsamāpaipāse|| 99 ||  
[ samvartanyaḥ punastiso bhavantyagnayambuvāyubhiḥ|  
dhyānatrayaṃ dvitīyādi śīrṣa tāsāṃ yathākramam|| 100||  
tadapakṣālasādhamryānan caturthe'styaniñjanāt|  
na nityaṃ saha sattvena tadvimānodayavyāt|| 10||  
tīrthakārāḥ kaṇabhukaprabhṛtayaḥ| vāyurbījamuktamiti| bahuvidhaprabhāvabhinnairvāyubhirabhimathyāmānā iti vacanāt | tasya nimittamiti| rūpāvacarovāyuravinaṣṭa| kāmavacarasya vivartakāle prathamakṣaṇotpannasya vāyornimittam| bījānyāhiyanta iti| pañca bījajātāni mūlabījādīni| mūlabīma  phallanbījaṃ bījabījagrabījaṃ skandhabījam| evamapīti yadyapyāhriyante bījāni na te sthūlabhāvānāṃ bījādibhyo bījāṅkugaṇḍādibhyo'ṅkurādīnāmaṅkurakāṇḍādīnāmutpattimicchanti| bījādīni hi teṣāṃ nimittakāraṇāni samavāyikāraṇāni| samavāyikaṇṇaiśca bhavitavyam| ata āha| kiṃ tarhi svebhya evāvayavebhya iti vistaraḥ| tad yathāṅkurādyavayavī aṅkurādyavayevebhyaḥ| teṣāmapyaṅkurādyavayavānāṃ svebhya evāvayavebhyaḥ| teṣāmapi svebhya eveti evaṃ yāvat paramāṇubhyaḥ| anyatra paramāṇupasarpaṇāditi| na janane bījādīnāṃ sāmarthya kiñcidasti| aṅkurādiparamāṇūpasarpaṇāttu teṣāṃ sāmarthyamiṣyata ityabhiprāyaḥ| aniyamo hi syāditi tantvādibhyo'pi kaṭādyutpattiḥ syādityarthaḥ| śaktiniyamāditi yathā bījādīnāṃ sāmarthyaniyamānnaniyamo bhaviṣyati| śabdapākajotpattiva| yathā śabdo'bhighātādibhyo vijātīyebhya utpadyate atha ca na yataḥ kutaścid gandharāsadervijātīyādutpadyate abhighātādīnāmeva vijātīyānāṃ tadutpādane sāmarthyāt| tathā pākajā rūpādayo vijātīyādagnyāderūtpadyante atha ca na yataḥ kutaścidvijātīyāccakṣurāderūtpadyante agnyādereva tadutpādane sāmarthyāt| vaiśeṣika āha citro vai guṇadharmaḥ svajātīyebhyo vijātīyebhyaścotpadyate| svajātīyebhyastāvadraparasagandharūparśadayaḥ kāraṇapūrvā rūparasagandhasparśādibhya evotpadyante vijātīyebhyo'pi saṃyogabibhāgapākajādayaḥ karmādibhyaḥ| tadevaṃ guṇadharmo drāvyadharma pratyanudāharāṇam| ta eva hi te thate vistaraḥ |vīṇādaya eva hi te tathā tena prakāreṇa kaṭādirūpeṇa sanniviṣṭhā vyavasthitāstāṃ kaṭasaṃjñāṃ labhante pipīlikādipaṃktivat yathā papīlikastathā sanniviṣṭāḥ paṃktiriti saṃgyālabhante na ca tābhyo'nuyat oṃktidravyamasti bhavatāmapi siddhante 'kathaṃ gamyate tāni vīṇādīni tathā sanniviṣṭāni kaṭādisaṃjñāṃ labhante na punastebhyo dravyāntarāṇīti| ata āha ekatantusaṃyoge paṭasyānupalambhāt| ārabdhakārye hyekasmin tantau cakṣusaṃsparśābhayaṃ saṃyukte tatsamavetaḥ paṭ'pi ca tābhyāṃ sannikṛṣṭa ityasāvapi tathaivopalabhyeta| yadi vrūyāt pratibandhāttasyānupalabdhiriti ata āha ko hi tadā sato vidyamānasya paṭasyopalabdhau pratibandha iti| nāsti pratibadnah ityabhiprāyaḥ| tasmānna paṭo nāma dravgyāntaramastīti| syānmatam ekaikasmin tantau pradeśena paṭo vartate na sarvātmanā vartata iti| ata ucyate akṛtasnavṛttāviti vistaraḥ 'kṛtsnāvṛttau paṭasya kalpyamānāyāṃ paṭabhāgo'traikaikasmin tantau syānna paṭaḥ| tataḥ kimiti cet| ata āha samūhāmātraṃ ca paṭaḥ syāditi vartate| ekasmin tantāveko bhāgo aparaminnpara iti tantuvartināṃ bahūnāṃ bhāgānāṃ samudāyaḥ paṭa iti ca prāpnoti na ceṣyate| kaśca tantubyyo'nyaḥ paṭabhāga it kaṇabhugabhaktāḥ praṣṭavyā| yena bhāgenāyamindriyasannikṛṣṭe tantau varteta| tasādetāmapi kalpanāṃ kalpayitvā nāto'nyaḥ paṭaḥ sidhyati| syānmatam ekaikasminnapi tantau paṭho vartate| paṭopalabdhestu paṭendriyasannikarṣaḥ paṭasyānekāśrayasaṃyogāpekṣo nimittamiti| ato'nekāśrayasaṃyogāpekṣāyāmupalabdhau kalpayamānāyāṃ daśāmātrasaṃghāte gṛhamāṇe paṭa upalabhyeta na tūpalabhyate paṭamadhyabhāgādyadarśanāt| atha matam naiva tadānīmapīndriyeṇa madhyādibhāgāḥ samprayujyanta iti| ato daśāmātrasaṃghāte paṭolabdhirna bhavatīti| tata idamabhidhīyate| na vā kadāciditi vistaraḥ na vā kadācit paṭopalabdhiḥ| syāt| madhyaparabhāgānāmindriyeṇāsannikarṣāt naiva sambhavo'sti yatadāmbhakā madhyaparabhāgāḥ sarva aindriyeṇa sannikṛṣeran| tathā ca sati atntvādiṣvapyeṣa prasaṅga iti na ca kiñcidapi kāryamupalabhyeta| kiñca kramasannikarṣe ceti vistaraḥ| yadā krameṇa cakṣuḥ saṃsparśanendriyaṃ vā paṭaṃ gṛhṇīyāttadā paṭagrahaṇaṃ na syāt yugapadanekāśrayasaṃyogābhāvāccakṣḥuḥsparśanendriyayoḥ| evamanyeṣāmapyavayavānāṃ grahaṇaṃ na syāt| tasmāditi vistaraḥ| yasmāt krameṇa paṭubdudhiḥ kaṭabuddhirvā tasmādavayaveṣḥveva paṭāvayaveṣu kaṭāvayaveṣu vā tadbuddhiḥ paṭabuddhiḥ paṭabuddhiḥ kaṭabuddhirvā vikalpavaśādabhavati| alātacakravat yathālāte śīghrasañcārāttatra tatrotpadyamāne alātacakrabuddhirbhavati tadvat| sādhanaṃ cātra na dravyasat paṭaḥ avayavagrahaṇasapekṣagrahaṇātvāt alātacakravat| bhinnarūpajātikriyeṣviti vistaraḥ| bhinnarūpeṣu tantuṣu nīlapītādibhedāt bhinnajātiṣu dukūlakarpāsādibhedāt bhinnakriyeṣūrdhvādhogamabhedāt| paṭasya rūpādyasambhavāt| kīdṛśaṃ tatra rūpaṃ bhavatu jātiḥ kriyā vā| syādeṣā buddhiḥ citramasya rūpādīti| tadevaṃ citrarūpaditve kalpyāne vijātīyārambho'pi syāt tantuvīraṇayorapi kaṭārambhaḥ syāt| tathā stai dharmaviśeṣaviparyayaḥ prāpnoti atyantavijātīyā rambhakatvaṃ dravyāṇāṃ prāpnotītyarthaḥ| avicitre ca pārśvāntare kapārśvacitrasya paṭasyādarśanaṃ syāt tasya citrarūpābhimatvtvāt| rūpadarśane hi tadāśrayadravyopalabdhiriṣyate vaiśeṣikaiḥ| mahatyanekadravyavattvādrūpāccopalabdhiḥ| rūpsaṃskārā bhāvād vāyoranupalabdhiriti vacanāt| citrarūpadarśanaṃ vā acitre pārśve syād yadi paṭo dṛśyeta| citraṃ hi tasya rūpamiṣyate| kriyāpi ca citreyyati citramiti ekaṃ tad dravyaṃ vicitrakriyamiti na yujyate| kriyāvaicitraye hi kriyāvadabhimatānāṃ dravyāṇāmanyatvamiṣyate| tāpaprakāśabhede ceti vistaraḥ| agniprabhālakṣaṇasyāvayavina dravyāṇāmanyatvamiṣyate| tāpaprakāśabhede ceti vistaraḥ| agnoprabhālakṣaṇasyāvayavina ādimadhyānteṣḥvārambhakeṣu ceti vistaraḥ| agniprabhālakṣaṇasyāvayavina ādimadhyānteṣvārambhakeṣu tāpaprakaśebhde sparśanabhedo dṛṣṭaḥ| sa ca tadbhedastatra yathākramaṃ tīvramadhyamandatā| tasyāvayavinaḥ prabhādravyasya tau rūpaspaśau nopapadyete| na hytekasyāvayavino'nekaṃ rūpaṃ sparśo vā yujyate| tena nāvayavebhyo'vayavivyamanyadastiti| atha matam ayaditantvādibyo'vayavebhyo na paṭādyavayavī vyatirikto'sti paramāṇūnāmatīndriyatvāt na ca tairavayaivaraindriyaka ārabdha iti kṛtsnaṃ jagadapratyakṣaṃ syāt ca gavādi dṛśayte| tasmāt tīndriyaiḥ paramāṇubhirarthāntaramanyadaindriyakamābdhamiti siddhirityatrocyate| paramaṇvatīndriyatve'pīti vistaraḥ| yathā bhavatāṃ vaiśeṣikāṇāmatīndriyatve'pi samastānāṃ kāryarambhakatvaṃ na vyastānām evamasmākamapi samastānāmeva pratyakṣatvaṃ na vaystānāmasatyavavinyarthāntarabhūte| yathā cakṣḥurādīṇāṃ cakṣūrūpālokamanaskārāṇāṃ samastānāṃ cakṣurvigyānotpattau kāraṇatvaṃ naikaikasya evaṃ pramāṇūnāma tīndriyatve'pi samastānāṃ pratyakṣatvaṃ naikaikasya| taimirikāṇāṃ ca pūrūṣāṇaṃ vikīrṇānāmasaṃyuktānāṃ keśānāṃ samūha upalabhyate anārabdhe'pyavavini| na tvekaikaḥ kleśaḥ| teṣāṃ taimirkāṇāṃ paramāṇuvedakaḥ kehso'tīndriyaḥ| ityato nāsti te'nyadravyam|  
tadevamavayavinaṃ pratiṣidhaya rūpādibhyo guṇebhyo'rthānaraṃ guṇinaṃ pratiṣeddhukām āha rūpādiṣveva ceti vistaraḥ| rūpādiṣveva paramāṇuriti saṃjñāviniveśaḥ| tadvināśe rūpādivināśe siddhaḥ pramāṇuvināśa iti| vaiśeṣikā āhuḥ dravyaṃ hi paramāṇuh| anyacca rūpādibhyo dravyam rūpagandharasasparśavatī pṛthivbī ityevamādisiddhāntavacanāt| aprayuktamasyānyatvam yasmānna kecacit paricchiydate imāni pṛthivypatejāmsi| ime eṣāṃ pṛthivādīnāṃ rūpādayo rūpasrasagandhasparśā guṇā iti| brūyādatīndiryāṇi tatvato na nirdhāryanta iti ata āha cakṣuḥsparśanagrāhyaṇi ca tāni pṝthivyādīni pratijñāyanta iti dagdhjeṣu ceti vistaraḥ ūrṇādīnyavayavidravyāṇi ca pacyanta iti pailukāḥ| tadukta bhavati prāktanuguṇanivṝttau pākajaguṇanivṝttau pākajaguṇotpāttau tadaśrayāstu dravyāṇintīt tadavasthānānyeva bhavantīti| tathaivorṇādibuddhiḥ syāt| na ca bhavati tasmāttaddavyabuddhayabhāvāt rūpādiṣveva tadbuddhirna dravye'rthāntarabhūta iti| yadi hi ūrṇadidravyaṃ rūpādibhyo 'nyat sytāttatatadvasthānaṃ bhavatīti rūpādīnyevāgnisambandhāduṣṇena nirūddhānītyūrṇādibuddhiḥ syāt na ca bhavati tasmādrapādiṣḥvevorṇādibuddhiḥ| vaiśeṣikā āhuḥ yadi rūpādimātre ghaṭaḥ syāt agnisambandhādrūpādīni pākajādīnyanyānutpadyanta iti sa evāyaṃ ghaṭa it ina parijñāyeta| yasya tu pākādrūpādīnyevotpadyante nirūdhyante ceti sa eva ghatastadavasthāno bhavatīti tasya tat parijñānaṃ yujhayata ityaya idamucyate| okjajotpattau ghaṭaparijñānaṃ saṃsthānasāmānyaditi| rūpādyākṝtisāmānyādityarthaḥ| paṃktivat| yathā na vastat pipīlikānāṃ tadvayatirikata paṃktidravyaṃ nāmāsti atha ca pūrvapaṃktisaṃsthānasāmānyadanyasyāmiti tatparijñānaṃ bhavati saiveyaṃ na tu tādavasthyāditi| ātrocyate cihnamapaśyato'parijñānaditi yasmāt khaṇḍanimnonnatādi cihnamapaśyatastatparijñānaṃ na bhavatyataḥ saṃsthānasāmānyāditi siddham|  
sā ceti vistaraḥ sā ca prītiḥ prasrabdhiyogena| prītermanasaḥ prasarabdhirjāyata iti prasrabdhiyogenāśrayamṛdukaraṇādpakalpā| ata eva ceti vistaraḥ| āśrayamṛdukaraṇāt| tasmin dvitīye samāpattidhyāne kṛtasnasya kāyasthairyasyāpagamādduḥkhendriyanirodha uktaḥ| sukhasya ca prahāṇādduḥkhasya ca prahāṇāt| pūrvameva saumanasyadaurmanasyayorasta gamādaduḥkhāsukhamupekṣāsbhṝtipariśuddhaṃ caturthaṃ dhyānamupasaṃpadya viharatīti vacanāt| ādhyātmikāpakṣālarahitatvāditi| ye vitarkavicārādayo'ṣṭāvādhyātmikāpākṣālāḥ

vitarkavicārau cāsū sukhādi ca catuṣṭaya

iti tadrahitvādityarthaḥ| ānejyamiti| ejū kampana ityasya dhātoretadrupamānejayamiti yadā tvāniñjayamiti pāṭhastadā igeḥ prakṛtyantarasyaitadrūpaṃ draṣṭavyam| na taiḥ śuddhavāsakāyikaiḥ śakyamārūpyātpraveṣṭūṃ vipaśyanācaritatvāt| śamathacaritāścārūpyāḥ| nāpyanytatra gantumadharāṃ bhūmiṃ viśeṣagāmmityvāt|| 100||  
[ saptāgninādbhirekaivaṃ gate'dbhiḥ saptake punaḥ|  
tejasā saptakaḥ paścād vāyusaṃvartano tataḥ|| 102|| ]||

etene krameṇeti| yathoktena saptānāṃ tejaḥsaṃvartanīnāmanantaramadbhiḥ saṃvartanī bhavatyekā| punaḥ saptānāṃ tejaḥsaṃvartanīnāmanantaramadbhiḥ saṃvartanī bhavati dvitīyā| evaṃ yāvat saptamyadbhiḥ saṃvartanī |  
evaṃ gate'dbhiḥ saptake punaḥ|  
tejasā saptakaḥ

tataḥ

paścāt

ekā

vāyusaṃvartanī

bhavati| kiṃ kāraṇam yathāvarṇītaṃ bahunā kālaprakarṣeṇāpasaṃvartanī bhavati| tato'pi bahutareṇa vāyusaṃvartanītyata idamucaytte| yathaiva hi teṣāmiti vistaraḥ| yathā prathamadhyānād dvitīyadhyānasamāpattiviśeṣādātmabhavānāṃ dvitīyadhyānabhūmikānāṃ sthitivsiśeṣaḥ| ardhamahākalpādha prathamadhyānopapannānāmāyuḥpramāṇam kalpadvayaṃ dvitīyadhyānopapannānāmiti| tathā bhājanānāmapi| kim sthitiviśeṣa iti| evaṃ dvitīyādapi tṛtīyadhyānasya viśeṣo vaktavyaḥ| evaṃ ca kṛtveti| yasmādaṣtakṛtvastejaḥsaṃvartyanīsaptakā iti ṣaṭpañcaśattejaḥsaṃvartanyo bhavanti saptāpasaṃvartantha ekā vāyusaṃvartanīti catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ saṃvartanyo bhavanti| tasmād yadukta prajñaptibhāṣye catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ kalpāḥ śubhakṛtasnānāmāyuḥpramāṇamiti| tata sūkta bhavati| yathā

ūrdhvantu parīttābhebhya āśrayaḥ|  
dviguṇadviguṇaḥ

ityavrokta śubhakṛtānāṃ catuḥṣaṣṭiyojanānāṃ śarīpramāṇam| śarīrapramāṇena ca teṣāmāyuḥpramāṇamuktam|  
āyustu kalpaiḥ svāśrayam

iti vacanāt | ekaikā saṃvartanī mahākalpa iti kṝtvā| iyatā hi kālena teṣāmayuṣaḥ parisamāptiriti|| 102||  
ācāryaśomitrekṛtāyāṃ sphuṭārthāyāmabhidharmakośavyākhyāyāṃ tṛtīyaṃ kośasthānam||
